````````markdown
# CopyCat media player — AI Assistant Context & Architecture Guide

> **Purpose of this file:** Give any AI assistant (Gemini, Claude, ChatGPT, GitHub Copilot, Cursor, Antigravity, local Ollama models, or MCP agents) instant, complete understanding of how **CopyCat media player** is structured, how to modify its UI/features, and how to control the running player programmatically via HTTP.

---

## 1. Project Overview
**CopyCat media player** is a standalone frameless Windows desktop media player built with **Electron + Vanilla HTML5/CSS3/JS + Web Audio API**, faithfully implementing Juxtopposed's *VLC After 25 Years* UI redesign with a custom cat icon identity.

Zero build-step complexity: edit any file in `src/` and press `Ctrl + R` inside the app (when Developer Mode is enabled) or restart via `npm start`.

## 2. File Structure
```text
CopyCat media player/
├── package.json          # Electron app manifest (`npm start`, `npm run dist`)
├── main.js               # Electron Main Process: app lifecycle, single instance lock, window creation
├── preload.js            # Electron Preload: exposes `window.copycat` / `window.vlcopycat` IPC bridge securely
├── AI_CONTEXT.md         # This architecture & AI control reference
├── README.md             # User quick-start, hotkeys, and customization guide
├── export_notebooklm.ps1 # Exports complete project into CopyCat-Gesamtprojekt.txt
├── assets/
│   ├── icon.ico          # Multi-resolution Windows icon (16, 24, 32, 48, 64, 128, 256px)
│   └── cat_icon.svg      # Source vector cat design
├── src/
│   ├── main/
│   │   ├── container.js      # Lightweight IoC ServiceContainer & WindowManager (Dependency Injection)
│   │   ├── stream-manager.js # Dedicated FFmpeg live streaming & thumbnail preview worker manager
│   │   ├── logger.js         # Structured, prefixed logger (`[VLCopyCat:...]`) with file backup
│   │   ├── session.js        # Windows session, Taskbar thumbar buttons, desktop shortcuts & AI breakout
│   │   ├── updater.js        # GitHub Releases auto-updater, asset stream downloader & installer runner
│   │   ├── media-resolver.js # Fast local ffprobe media analyzer, byte-range handler & subtitle extraction
│   │   ├── downloader.js     # Native stream downloader, sliding-window season queue, JIT sniffer, Dual-Audio muxer & Explorer view optimizer
│   │   ├── anime-skip-resolver.js # Automated AniList/MyAnimeList & AniSkip resolver with FFmpeg chapter embedding
│   │   ├── stream-sniffer.js # Modular Sniffer Router & Dispatcher
│   │   ├── sniffers/
│   │   │   ├── base-sniffer.js   # Dynamic CDN Header Registry, cache & headless Chromium runner
│   │   │   ├── miruro-sniffer.js # Miruro & strm.cx Anime Sniffer with Sub/Dub discovery
│   │   │   ├── videa-sniffer.js  # Hungarian Anime & Video Host Sniffer (Videa, JobbMintATv, Indavideo)
│   │   │   └── generic-sniffer.js# Universal HTML5 Video & fallback sniffer
│   │   ├── ipc.js            # Declarative IPC Registry with input validation & injected services
│   │   └── server.js         # Local HTTP Server (`127.0.0.1:4500`) with dynamic CommandRegistry
│   ├── index.html        # Player DOM: Top Header, Left Library Sidebar, Center Video Stage,
│   │                     #   Right Queue/Bookmarks Drawer, Bottom Bar, Popovers & Settings Modal
│   ├── styles.css        # Design tokens (--accent, --accent-rgb), 9 color themes, cat iconography,
│   │                     #   Juxtopposed [ON|OFF] switches, seekbar & glassmorphic surfaces
│   ├── icons.js          # Crisp inline vector SVGs matching the redesign's iconography & custom cat
│   └── app.js            # Core Player State, Web Audio 200% Gain + EQ + Visualizer, offscreen
│                         #   hover-thumbnail generator, SRT/VTT subtitle engine & AI IPC handler
└── tests/
    └── test-suite.js     # Automated regression test suite (syntax, IoC, security, semver, anime, range)
```

## 3. Design System & Theme Variables (`src/styles.css`)
All accent colors are driven by two CSS custom properties on `:root`:
- `--accent`: Hex or CSS color (default Traffic Cone Orange `#E07A16`)
- `--accent-rgb`: Comma-separated RGB triplet (`224, 122, 22`) used for translucent fills like the signature dark-tinted seekbar fill (`rgba(var(--accent-rgb), 0.28)`).

9 built-in presets in `src/app.js` (`ACCENT_PRESETS`):
`orange`, `amber`, `lime`, `emerald`, `cyan`, `blue`, `purple`, `magenta`, `rose`.

## 4. Built-in Local AI Control API (`http://127.0.0.1:4500`)
While CopyCat media player is running, it hosts a lightweight local JSON HTTP server on `127.0.0.1:4500` so any AI assistant, CLI tool, PowerShell script, or MCP tool can inspect and control playback in real time.

> **Security Model:** Port 4500 strictly binds to `127.0.0.1` (loopback only) and enforces Origin & `Sec-Fetch-Site: cross-site` inspection to reject unauthorized external web browser requests on control/status endpoints with `403 Forbidden`, while permitting internal fragmented MP4 media streaming (`/api/stream`, `/api/thumb`) from Electron's renderer. By design, any local user-level process/script on the host machine can inspect and control the player. Never bridge or expose port 4500 directly to external or public networks.

### Endpoints
- **`GET http://127.0.0.1:4500/`** — Self-documenting API schema & available commands.
- **`GET http://127.0.0.1:4500/api/status`** — Returns live player state:
  ```json
  {
    "app": "CopyCat media player",
    "playing": true,
    "title": "example.mp4",
    "filePath": "C:\\Videos\\example.mp4",
    "currentTime": 136.4,
    "duration": 468.0,
    "volume": 100,
    "muted": false,
    "speed": 1,
    "accent": "orange",
    "queueLength": 4,
    "streamEngine": "ffmpeg-live"
  }
  ```
- **`GET http://127.0.0.1:4500/api/stream?path=...&start=0&vcodec=h264`** — Zero-disk FFmpeg fragmented MP4 live stream for non-Chromium containers/codecs.
- **`GET http://127.0.0.1:4500/api/thumb?path=...&t=120`** — Instant on-demand MJPEG seekbar hover preview frame generator for streamed media.
- **`POST http://127.0.0.1:4500/api/control`** — Execute an action:
  ```json
  { "action": "togglePlay" }
  ```
  Supported `action` values:
  - `"play"`, `"pause"`, `"togglePlay"`, `"stop"`, `"next"`, `"prev"`
  - `"seek"` with `"value": 120` (seconds) or `"delta": 10` (relative seconds)
  - `"volume"` with `"value": 125` (`0` to `200`, supports 200% VLC boost!)
  - `"mute"`, `"toggleFullscreen"`, `"togglePip"`
  - `"speed"` with `"value": 1.25` (`0.25` to `2.0`)
  - `"setAccent"` with `"value": "cyan"` (any preset name or `#RRGGBB` hex color!)
  - `"openFile"` with `"filePath": "C:\\path\\to\\video.mp4"`
  - `"openFolder"` with `"folderPath": "C:\\path\\to\\folder"`

### PowerShell One-Liner Examples for AI Agents
```powershell
# Check what is playing
Invoke-RestMethod http://127.0.0.1:4500/api/status

# Switch theme to purple
Invoke-RestMethod -Method Post http://127.0.0.1:4500/api/control -ContentType "application/json" -Body '{"action":"setAccent","value":"purple"}'

# Open a local video file
Invoke-RestMethod -Method Post http://127.0.0.1:4500/api/control -ContentType "application/json" -Body '{"action":"openFile","filePath":"C:\\Users\\Schual\\Desktop\\video.mp4"}'
```

---

## 5. Automated Anime Opening & Ending Skip Architecture
- **AniList & AniSkip Pipeline (`src/main/anime-skip-resolver.js`)**: Automatically extracts clean series titles and episode numbers from video filenames or stream titles, resolves MyAnimeList IDs via AniList's public GraphQL API, and retrieves crowdsourced opening (`op`) and ending (`ed`) interval timestamps via the AniSkip API.
- **Strict Verification (Zero Guessing)**: Metadata strictly uses verified crowdsourced intervals for that specific episode. Incomplete or missing intervals are never fabricated, guessed, or inherited across candidate episodes.
- **Contiguous Chapters & FFmpeg Atoms**: Contiguous chapters (`Prologue` -> `Opening` -> `Episode` [-> `Ending`]) are baked directly into downloaded MP4 files using FFmpeg stream-copy (`-map_chapters 1 -map_metadata 1 -c copy -movflags +faststart`) without re-encoding or quality loss.
- **Hardware-Isolated UI Overlay**: The floating skip button (`.skip-segment-btn` in `src/styles.css`) is styled in signature CopyCat orange (`var(--accent)`), features a tactile `Tab` hotkey badge and vector SVG icon, uses GPU layer isolation (`transform: translateZ(0); will-change: transform, opacity;`), and `showSkipSegmentPrompt` in `src/app.js` guards against per-tick DOM reflows.

## 6. Security & Audio DSP Hardening
- **HTTP Origin Guard & DNS Rebinding Shield (`src/main/server.js`)**: Port 4500 enforces strict loopback `Host` header checks (`127.0.0.1`, `localhost`, `::1`) to prevent DNS rebinding attacks from attacker-controlled domains. It blocks cross-site browser requests (`Sec-Fetch-Site: cross-site`), untrusted null origins on `/api/control`, and external web domains with `403 Forbidden` to prevent CSRF, while seamlessly routing internal fragmented MP4 media streaming (`/api/stream`, `/api/thumb`).
- **Headless Stream Sniffer Isolation (`src/main/sniffers/base-sniffer.js`)**: Ephemeral, isolated browser partitions with all device permissions denied (`setPermissionRequestHandler`), popup window creation blocked (`setWindowOpenHandler` deny), protocols restricted strictly to `http:` and `https:`, and subframe redirects to loopback addresses blocked.
- **Hardware-Accelerated Stream Passthrough (`src/main/stream-manager.js`)**: Real-time FFmpeg live stream automatically detects H.264 video streams and utilizes zero-copy (`-c:v copy`) video passthrough with stereo AAC audio, eliminating CPU transcoding overhead and providing instant playback and seeking for MPEG-TS containers.
- **Two-Stage Safety Peak Limiter & Soft Clipper (`src/app.js`)**: Positioned downstream of the 200% GainNode, combining a fast-attack `DynamicsCompressorNode` (threshold: `-0.5 dBFS`, ratio: `20:1`, attack: `0.002s`, release: `0.05s`) with a 2x-oversampled hyperbolic tangent (`tanh`) `WaveShaperNode` soft-clipper to attenuate digital overshoot and protect speaker hardware and ear health during heavy volume amplification.
- **Atomic Updater Integrity & SHA-256 Verification (`src/main/updater.js`)**: Enforces HTTPS, stages downloads into atomic `.part` files, extracts trusted SHA-256 checksums from GitHub release manifests or release notes, enforces cryptographic hash matching before execution, and validates Windows PE `MZ` binary headers.
- **Automated Regression Suite (`tests/test-suite.js`)**: Run via `npm test` or `npm run lint` across syntax, IoC container, updater semver, cryptographic checksums, DNS rebinding guards, security origin filters, and media processing services.
````````

``````powershell
# CopyCat — Projekt-Export für NotebookLM
# Durchsucht alle relevanten Projektdateien im Arbeitsverzeichnis,
# sortiert sie in logischer Architektur-Reihenfolge und schreibt alles UTF-8-kodiert in './CopyCat-Gesamtprojekt.txt'.

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

$ProjectRoot = if ($PSScriptRoot) { $PSScriptRoot } else { Resolve-Path './' }
$OutputFile  = Join-Path $ProjectRoot 'CopyCat-Gesamtprojekt.txt'

$AllowedExtensions = @('.json', '.js', '.html', '.css', '.md', '.ps1')
$ExcludedDirs      = @('.git', 'node_modules', 'dist', 'out', 'build', 'bin', 'assets', 'vendor')
$ExcludedFiles     = @('CopyCat-Gesamtprojekt.txt', 'CopyCat-Konversationsprotokoll.txt', 'package-lock.json', 'export_conversation.py', 'export_conversation.ps1')

$PriorityOrder = @(
    './package.json',
    './README.md',
    './AI_CONTEXT.md',
    './main.js',
    './preload.js',
    './src/main/container.js',
    './src/main/stream-manager.js',
    './src/main/logger.js',
    './src/main/session.js',
    './src/main/updater.js',
    './src/main/media-resolver.js',
    './src/main/media-editor.js',
    './src/main/downloader.js',
    './src/main/anime-skip-resolver.js',
    './src/main/stream-sniffer.js',
    './src/main/sniffers/base-sniffer.js',
    './src/main/sniffers/miruro-sniffer.js',
    './src/main/sniffers/videa-sniffer.js',
    './src/main/sniffers/generic-sniffer.js',
    './src/main/ipc.js',
    './src/main/server.js',
    './src/index.html',
    './src/styles.css',
    './src/icons.js',
    './src/app.js',
    './tests/test-suite.js',
    './export_notebooklm.ps1'
)

$AllFiles = Get-ChildItem -Path $ProjectRoot -Recurse -File | Where-Object {
    $ext = $_.Extension.ToLowerInvariant()
    if ($AllowedExtensions -notcontains $ext) { return $false }
    if ($ExcludedFiles -contains $_.Name) { return $false }

    $relPath = $_.FullName.Substring($ProjectRoot.Path.Length).TrimStart('\', '/')
    $segments = $relPath -split '[\\/]'
    foreach ($dir in $ExcludedDirs) {
        if ($segments -contains $dir) { return $false }
    }
    return $true
}

$Files = $AllFiles | Sort-Object {
    $rel = './' + ($_.FullName.Substring($ProjectRoot.Path.Length).TrimStart('\', '/') -replace '\\', '/')
    $idx = $PriorityOrder.IndexOf($rel)
    if ($idx -ge 0) { $idx } else { 999 }
}, FullName

function Get-CodeFenceLang([string]$Extension) {
    switch ($Extension.ToLowerInvariant()) {
        '.js'   { return 'javascript' }
        '.json' { return 'json' }
        '.html' { return 'html' }
        '.css'  { return 'css' }
        '.md'   { return 'markdown' }
        '.ps1'  { return 'powershell' }
        default { return 'text' }
    }
}

$Sb = [System.Text.StringBuilder]::new()
[void]$Sb.AppendLine("================================================================================")
[void]$Sb.AppendLine("# PROJEKT-GESAMTDOKUMENTATION: VLCopyCat / CopyCat Media Player")
[void]$Sb.AppendLine("Aktualisiert am: $(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss')")
[void]$Sb.AppendLine("Enthaltene Dateien ($($Files.Count)):")
foreach ($F in $Files) {
    $Rel = './' + ($F.FullName.Substring($ProjectRoot.Path.Length).TrimStart('\', '/') -replace '\\', '/')
    [void]$Sb.AppendLine("  - $Rel")
}
[void]$Sb.AppendLine("================================================================================")
[void]$Sb.AppendLine("")

foreach ($File in $Files) {
    $RelPath = './' + ($File.FullName.Substring($ProjectRoot.Path.Length).TrimStart('\', '/') -replace '\\', '/')
    $Lang    = Get-CodeFenceLang $File.Extension
    $RawContent = Get-Content -LiteralPath $File.FullName -Raw -Encoding UTF8 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($RawContent)) {
        continue
    }

    $CleanedContent = ($RawContent -replace "`r`n", "`n").Trim()
    $CleanedContent = [regex]::Replace($CleanedContent, "`n{3,}", "`n`n")

    $Fence   = if ($File.Extension.ToLowerInvariant() -eq '.md') { '````````' } else { '``````' }

    [void]$Sb.AppendLine("################################################################################")
    [void]$Sb.AppendLine("## DATEI: $RelPath")
    [void]$Sb.AppendLine("################################################################################")
    [void]$Sb.AppendLine("")
    [void]$Sb.AppendLine("$Fence$Lang")
    [void]$Sb.AppendLine($CleanedContent)
    [void]$Sb.AppendLine("$Fence")
    [void]$Sb.AppendLine("")
}

$Utf8NoBom = [System.Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false)
[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($OutputFile, $Sb.ToString(), $Utf8NoBom)

Write-Output "Aktualisiert: ./CopyCat-Gesamtprojekt.txt ($($Files.Count) Dateien)"

# Automatische Aktualisierung des Konversationsprotokolls
$convScript = Join-Path $ProjectRoot 'export_conversation.py'
if (Test-Path $convScript) {
    try {
        & python $convScript
    } catch {
        Write-Warning "Konversationsprotokoll konnte nicht automatisch aktualisiert werden: $_"
    }
}
``````

``````javascript
// main.js - VLCopyCat / CopyCat Media Player Main Process
// Modular Architecture with Dependency Injection:
//   - src/main/container.js      -> Lightweight IoC Service Container & WindowManager
//   - src/main/stream-manager.js  -> Dedicated stream & thumbnail process manager
//   - src/main/logger.js         -> Structured, prefixed logging
//   - src/main/session.js        -> Windows OS integrations & AI sandbox breakout
//   - src/main/updater.js        -> GitHub Releases auto-updater & asset downloader
//   - src/main/media-resolver.js -> Media detection, sniffer, probing & folder indexing
//   - src/main/ipc.js            -> Declarative IPC registry with injected services
//   - src/main/server.js         -> Local HTTP AI control server with dynamic command registry

const { app, BrowserWindow, protocol, net, Menu, shell, session } = require('electron');
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const { Readable } = require('stream');
const { pathToFileURL } = require('url');
const { parseByteRange } = require('./src/main/media-resolver');

// 1. Structured Logging Module
const { createLogger } = require('./src/main/logger');
const mainLogger = createLogger('Main');

// 2. Windows Session & AI Sandbox Breakout
const { handleAiSandboxBreakout } = require('./src/main/session');

// Trigger breakout if running inside AI agent terminal sandboxes
handleAiSandboxBreakout();

// 3. High-Performance Hardware Acceleration & Zero-Copy Compositing
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('enable-gpu-rasterization');
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('enable-zero-copy');
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('ignore-gpu-blocklist');
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('disable-background-timer-throttling');
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('enable-accelerated-video-decode');
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('enable-accelerated-mjpeg-decode');
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('enable-blink-features', 'AudioVideoTracks');

protocol.registerSchemesAsPrivileged([
  {
    scheme: 'media',
    privileges: {
      standard: true,
      secure: true,
      supportFetchAPI: true,
      corsEnabled: true,
      stream: true
    }
  }
]);

process.on('uncaughtException', (err) => {
  mainLogger.error('uncaughtException:', err.stack || err.message);
});
process.on('unhandledRejection', (reason) => {
  mainLogger.error('unhandledRejection:', reason?.stack || reason);
});

// 4. Initialize Dependency Injection Service Container
const { createAppContainer } = require('./src/main/container');
const container = createAppContainer();

const windowManager = container.get('window');
const streamManager = container.get('stream');
const mediaService = container.get('media');
const sessionService = container.get('session');
const updaterService = container.get('updater');

// 5. Connect Declarative IPC Registry with Injected Services
const { registerIpcRegistry } = require('./src/main/ipc');
registerIpcRegistry(container);

// 6. Start Local HTTP AI Control Server (Port 4500)
const { startAiControlServer } = require('./src/main/server');

// 7. Window Creation & Lifecycle Setup
function createMainWindow() {
  const icoPath = path.join(__dirname, 'assets', 'icon.ico');
  const iconPng = path.join(__dirname, 'assets', 'icon.png');
  const windowIcon = fs.existsSync(icoPath) ? icoPath : (fs.existsSync(iconPng) ? iconPng : undefined);

  const mainWindow = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 1320,
    height: 780,
    minWidth: 720,
    minHeight: 460,
    frame: false,
    backgroundColor: '#0A0A0A',
    title: 'CopyCat media player',
    icon: windowIcon,
    show: false,
    webPreferences: {
      preload: path.join(__dirname, 'preload.js'),
      contextIsolation: true,
      nodeIntegration: false,
      webSecurity: true,
      backgroundThrottling: false
    }
  });

  mainWindow.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, 'src', 'index.html'));

  mainWindow.webContents.on('console-message', (event, level, message, line, sourceId) => {
    if (level >= 2 || process.env.COPYCAT_DEBUG) {
      mainLogger.debug(`[Renderer ${level}] ${message} (${sourceId}:${line})`);
    }
  });

  // Native Context Menu for text inputs (Cut, Copy, Paste, Select All)
  mainWindow.webContents.on('context-menu', (event, params) => {
    if (params.isEditable) {
      const editMenu = Menu.buildFromTemplate([
        { role: 'undo', label: 'Undo' },
        { role: 'redo', label: 'Redo' },
        { type: 'separator' },
        { role: 'cut', label: 'Cut' },
        { role: 'copy', label: 'Copy' },
        { role: 'paste', label: 'Paste' },
        { type: 'separator' },
        { role: 'selectAll', label: 'Select All' }
      ]);
      editMenu.popup({ window: mainWindow });
    } else if (params.selectionText && params.selectionText.trim().length > 0) {
      const selectMenu = Menu.buildFromTemplate([
        { role: 'copy', label: 'Copy' },
        { role: 'selectAll', label: 'Select All' }
      ]);
      selectMenu.popup({ window: mainWindow });
    }
  });

  mainWindow.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {
    if (url.startsWith('https://') || url.startsWith('http://')) {
      shell.openExternal(url);
    }
    return { action: 'deny' };
  });

  // Attach all window lifecycle and state events cleanly via WindowManager
  windowManager.attachWindow(mainWindow);

  return mainWindow;
}

// 8. Single Instance Lock & App Initialization
const isSingleInstance = app.requestSingleInstanceLock();

if (!isSingleInstance) {
  app.quit();
} else {
  const initialMedia = mediaService.resolveCliFile(process.argv);
  if (initialMedia) {
    windowManager.setInitialMedia(initialMedia);
  }

  app.on('second-instance', (_event, commandLine) => {
    const win = windowManager.getWindow();
    if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
      windowManager.bringToFront(win);
      const payload = mediaService.resolveCliFile(commandLine);
      if (payload) {
        windowManager.setInitialMedia(payload);
        win.webContents.send('media:initial', payload);
      }
    }
  });

  app.whenReady().then(() => {
    Menu.setApplicationMenu(null);

    protocol.handle('media', (request) => {
      try {
        const parsed = new URL(request.url);
        let filePath = '';
        if (process.platform === 'win32' && /^[a-zA-Z]$/.test(parsed.host)) {
          filePath = parsed.host.toUpperCase() + ':' + decodeURIComponent(parsed.pathname);
        } else {
          let p = decodeURIComponent(parsed.pathname);
          if (process.platform === 'win32' && /^\/[a-zA-Z]:/.test(p)) {
            p = p.slice(1);
          }
          filePath = p;
        }
        filePath = path.resolve(filePath);

        if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
          return new Response('Media file not found', { status: 404 });
        }

        const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);
        const { isRange, start, end, chunkSize, total } = parseByteRange(request.headers.get('range'), stat.size);

        const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
        const mime = (ext === '.mp4' || ext === '.m4v') ? 'video/mp4' :
                     (ext === '.webm') ? 'video/webm' :
                     (ext === '.mkv') ? 'video/x-matroska' :
                     (ext === '.mp3') ? 'audio/mpeg' :
                     (ext === '.wav') ? 'audio/wav' :
                     (ext === '.flac') ? 'audio/flac' : 'application/octet-stream';

        // Large 2MB highWaterMark buffer eliminates any stream starvation while enabling instant byte-range seeking
        const stream = fs.createReadStream(filePath, { start, end, highWaterMark: 2 * 1024 * 1024 });

        if (isRange) {
          return new Response(Readable.toWeb(stream), {
            status: 206,
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': mime,
              'Accept-Ranges': 'bytes',
              'Content-Range': `bytes ${start}-${end}/${total}`,
              'Content-Length': String(chunkSize)
            }
          });
        }

        return new Response(Readable.toWeb(stream), {
          status: 200,
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': mime,
            'Accept-Ranges': 'bytes',
            'Content-Length': String(total)
          }
        });
      } catch (err) {
        mainLogger.warn('Media protocol error:', err.message);
        return new Response('Media protocol error', { status: 500 });
      }
    });

    // 4b. Stream Providers Header Rewriting (Bypasses anti-hotlink restrictions for providers like strm.cx/miruro/frostheron/coastowl/*.top)
    const { getRequiredHeadersForUrl, isStrmCdnUrl } = require('./src/main/stream-sniffer');
    session.defaultSession.webRequest.onBeforeSendHeaders((details, callback) => {
      const u = details.url;
      if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(u)) {
        const reqHeaders = getRequiredHeadersForUrl(u);
        if (isStrmCdnUrl(u) || reqHeaders.Referer === 'https://strm.cx/') {
          details.requestHeaders['Referer'] = reqHeaders.Referer;
          details.requestHeaders['Origin'] = reqHeaders.Origin;
        }
      }
      callback({ cancel: false, requestHeaders: details.requestHeaders });
    });

    // Scoped CORS headers on media & streaming resources so native hls.js MSE & Web Audio API work seamlessly
    const MEDIA_STREAM_REGEX = /\.(m3u8|mpd|ts|m4s|mp4|webm|aac|mp3|m4a|ogg|opus)(\?.*)?$/i;
    session.defaultSession.webRequest.onHeadersReceived((details, callback) => {
      const responseHeaders = { ...(details.responseHeaders || {}) };
      const u = details.url || '';
      if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(u) && (details.resourceType === 'media' || MEDIA_STREAM_REGEX.test(u) || u.includes(':4500/api/'))) {
        responseHeaders['Access-Control-Allow-Origin'] = ['*'];
        responseHeaders['Access-Control-Allow-Headers'] = ['*'];
        responseHeaders['Access-Control-Expose-Headers'] = ['Content-Length, Content-Range, Content-Type'];
      }
      callback({ responseHeaders });
    });

    startAiControlServer(4500, container);
    createMainWindow();

    setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const updateInfo = await updaterService.check();
        const win = windowManager.getWindow();
        if (updateInfo && updateInfo.updateAvailable && win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
          win.webContents.send('updater:available', updateInfo);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        mainLogger.debug('Update check skipped:', err.message);
      }
    }, 2500);
  });
}

app.on('before-quit', () => {
  streamManager.killAll();
});

app.on('window-all-closed', () => {
  streamManager.killAll();
  if (process.platform !== 'darwin') app.quit();
});
``````

``````javascript
const { contextBridge, ipcRenderer, webUtils } = require('electron');

let bufferedInitialMedia = null;
let initialMediaListener = null;

ipcRenderer.on('media:initial', (_e, data) => {
  bufferedInitialMedia = data;
  if (typeof initialMediaListener === 'function') {
    try {
      initialMediaListener(data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error in onInitialMedia callback:', err);
    }
  }
});

const copycatApi = {
  // Window controls
  minimize: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('win:minimize'),
  maximize: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('win:maximize'),
  toggleFullscreen: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('win:toggleFullscreen'),
  exitFullscreenToDrag: (data) => ipcRenderer.invoke('win:exitFullscreenToDrag', data),
  dragMove: (data) => ipcRenderer.send('win:dragMove', data),
  toggleAlwaysOnTop: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('win:toggleAlwaysOnTop'),
  setAlwaysOnTop: (flag) => ipcRenderer.invoke('win:setAlwaysOnTop', flag),
  enterPip: (opts) => ipcRenderer.invoke('win:enterPip', opts),
  leavePip: (opts) => ipcRenderer.invoke('win:leavePip', opts),
  close: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('win:close'),
  setWindowOpacity: (opacity) => ipcRenderer.invoke('win:setOpacity', opacity),
  copyImageToClipboard: (dataUrl) => ipcRenderer.invoke('clipboard:writeImage', dataUrl),
  getWindowState: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('win:getState'),

  // File & folder dialogs
  openFiles: (multi = false) => ipcRenderer.invoke('dialog:openFiles', multi),
  openFolder: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('dialog:openFolder'),
  openSubtitle: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('dialog:openSubtitle'),

  // Media probe & FFmpeg stream lifecycle
  probeMedia: (filePath) => ipcRenderer.invoke('media:probe', filePath),
  resolveAnimeChapters: (filePath, duration) => ipcRenderer.invoke('media:resolveAnimeChapters', { filePath, duration }),
  extractEmbeddedSubtitle: (filePath, trackIndex) => ipcRenderer.invoke('media:extractEmbeddedSubtitle', { filePath, trackIndex }),
  stopStream: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('media:stopStream'),

  // Media Editor & File Processing Tools
  probeMediaMetadata: (filePath) => ipcRenderer.invoke('media-editor:probe', filePath),
  convertMedia: (opts) => ipcRenderer.invoke('media-editor:convert', opts),
  cancelMediaJob: (jobId) => ipcRenderer.invoke('media-editor:cancel-job', jobId),
  searchSubtitles: (queryOpts) => ipcRenderer.invoke('media-editor:search-subtitles', queryOpts),
  downloadSubtitle: (url, targetPath) => ipcRenderer.invoke('media-editor:download-subtitle', url, targetPath),
  embedSubtitles: (opts) => ipcRenderer.invoke('media-editor:embed-subtitles', opts),
  applyMediaMetadata: (opts) => ipcRenderer.invoke('media-editor:apply-metadata', opts),
  openVideoFileDialog: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('dialog:open-video-file'),
  openSubtitleFileDialog: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('dialog:open-subtitle-file'),
  onMediaEditorProgress: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('media-editor:progress', (_e, data) => cb(data)),

  // Filesystem helpers
  getPathForFile: (file) => {
    try {
      return webUtils.getPathForFile(file);
    } catch {
      return file?.path || '';
    }
  },
  resolvePaths: (paths) => ipcRenderer.invoke('fs:resolvePaths', paths),
  scanFolder: (folderPath) => ipcRenderer.invoke('fs:scanFolder', folderPath),
  readSubtitle: (subPath) => ipcRenderer.invoke('fs:readSubtitle', subPath),
  getQuickFolders: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('fs:getQuickFolders'),
  saveSnapshot: (payload) => ipcRenderer.invoke('fs:saveSnapshot', payload),
  revealInExplorer: (filePath) => ipcRenderer.invoke('shell:reveal', filePath),
  openExternal: (url) => ipcRenderer.invoke('shell:openExternal', url),
  hasDesktopShortcut: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('shortcut:hasDesktop'),
  createDesktopShortcut: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('shortcut:createDesktop'),
  setDefaultPlayer: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('app:setDefaultPlayer'),
  installAiIntegration: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('app:installAiIntegration'),
  openDefaultAppsSettings: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('app:openDefaultAppsSettings'),

  // Native Stream & Downloader
  probeStreamUrl: (url) => ipcRenderer.invoke('stream:probe', url),
  startDownload: (payload) => ipcRenderer.invoke('download:start', payload),
  queueSeasonDownload: (payload) => ipcRenderer.invoke('download:queueSeason', payload),
  haltAllDownloads: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('download:haltAll'),
  retryDownload: (id) => ipcRenderer.invoke('download:retry', id),
  incrementEpisodeUrl: (url, step) => ipcRenderer.invoke('download:incrementUrl', { url, step }),
  cancelDownload: (id) => ipcRenderer.invoke('download:cancel', id),
  getDownloadQueue: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('download:getQueue'),
  getDownloadFolder: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('download:getFolder'),
  setDownloadFolder: (folder) => ipcRenderer.invoke('download:setFolder', folder),
  chooseDownloadFolder: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('download:chooseFolder'),
  openDownloadFolder: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('download:openFolder'),
  onDownloadProgress: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('download:progress', (_e, data) => cb(data)),
  onDownloadComplete: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('download:complete', (_e, data) => cb(data)),
  onDownloadError: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('download:error', (_e, data) => cb(data)),

  // Auto-Updater
  checkForUpdates: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('updater:check'),
  downloadAndInstallUpdate: (asset) => ipcRenderer.invoke('updater:downloadAndInstall', asset),
  onUpdateAvailable: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('updater:available', (_e, data) => cb(data)),
  onUpdateDownloadProgress: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('updater:downloadProgress', (_e, p) => cb(p)),
  onUpdateDownloadComplete: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('updater:downloadComplete', (_e, d) => cb(d)),
  onUpdateError: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('updater:error', (_e, err) => cb(err)),

  // Live telemetry + AI bridge
  reportStatus: (status) => ipcRenderer.send('player:statusUpdate', status),
  onWindowState: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('win:state', (_e, state) => cb(state)),
  onWindowMinimize: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('win:minimize', () => cb()),
  onWindowRestore: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('win:restore', () => cb()),
  onPipExitRequested: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('pip:exitRequested', () => cb()),
  onAiCommand: (cb) => ipcRenderer.on('ai:command', (_e, cmd) => cb(cmd)),
  onInitialMedia: (cb) => {
    initialMediaListener = cb;
    if (bufferedInitialMedia) {
      try {
        cb(bufferedInitialMedia);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error delivering buffered initial media:', err);
      }
    }
  },
  getInitialMedia: async () => {
    if (bufferedInitialMedia) return bufferedInitialMedia;
    try {
      const initial = await ipcRenderer.invoke('media:getInitial');
      if (initial) bufferedInitialMedia = initial;
      return initial;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }
};

contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('copycat', copycatApi);
contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('vlcopycat', copycatApi);
``````

## DATEI: ./C:/Users/Schual/Desktop/VLCopyCat-Master/README.md
################################################################################

````````markdown
<div align="center">

<img src="assets/icon.png" width="96" height="96" alt="CopyCat media player Logo" />

# CopyCat Media Player

**A modern, hardware-accelerated desktop media player for Windows.**  
*Re-imagining classic media playback through a refined glassmorphic interface, zero-copy Direct Composition rendering, Web Audio DSP amplification, and native system integration.*

[![Release](https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/georgschneider96-collab/CopyCat-media-player?color=E07A16&label=Release&style=flat-square)](https://github.com/georgschneider96-collab/CopyCat-media-player/releases/latest)
[![Platform](https://img.shields.io/badge/Platform-Windows%2010%20%7C%2011%20(x64)-blue?style=flat-square)](https://github.com/georgschneider96-collab/CopyCat-media-player)
[![Electron](https://img.shields.io/badge/Electron-35.0-47848F?style=flat-square&logo=electron&logoColor=white)](https://www.electronjs.org/)
[![License](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-green?style=flat-square)](LICENSE)

</div>

---

## Overview

**CopyCat Media Player** is a standalone, frameless desktop media player engineered for performance, precision, and modern design aesthetics. Built from the ground up on Electron, HTML5 Video/Canvas, and the Web Audio API, CopyCat brings Juxtopposed's viral *VLC After 25 Years* conceptual redesign to life with bespoke cat iconography branding, ultra-responsive gesture drawers, zero-latency local HTTP control, and native Windows shell integration.

---

## Key Features

### 🎨 Refined Glassmorphic Interface
- **Frameless Acrylic Canvas**: Custom title bar with window snapping, smooth maximize/restore transitions, and fluid drag-down gestures from fullscreen mode.
- **Dynamic Accent System**: 9 built-in theme presets (*Traffic Cone Orange, Amber, Lime, Emerald, Cyan, Blue, Purple, Magenta, Rose*) powering coherent accents and tinted seekbar fills.
- **Context-Aware Drawers**: Edge-gesture side panels—Left Library Navigator and Right Queue / Bookmarks Drawer—that glide smoothly into view and automatically defer rendering calculations when collapsed.
- **Unobtrusive Controls**: Bottom playback bar, volume popovers, and settings auto-hide during video playback and restore instantly on mouse interaction.

### ⚡ Hardware-Accelerated Video & Streaming Engine
- **Direct Composition Zero-Copy Passthrough**: Active video playback bypasses intermediate CSS filter graphs (`filter: none`), enabling Chromium's Direct Composition swapchain to feed hardware-decoded video surfaces directly to the display with minimal GPU memory transfers.
- **Chromium GPU Acceleration**: Configured with dedicated hardware decode switches (`enable-accelerated-video-decode`, `enable-accelerated-mjpeg-decode`) for smooth 4K/60fps and high-bitrate playback.
- **RFC 7233 Byte-Range Protocol**: Native `media:///` protocol handler with 2MB highWaterMark buffering for instant non-blocking seeks across local NVMe/SSD files.
- **Universal Format Demuxing**: Seamlessly plays standard HTML5 formats (`.mp4`, `.webm`) as well as extended media containers (`.mkv`, `.avi`, `.ts`, `.m2ts`, `.flv`, `.vob`, `.wmv`) through bundled background FFmpeg/FFprobe streaming services.

### 🔊 Studio-Grade Web Audio & Real-Time Visualizer
- **True 200% Volume Boost**: Powered by native Web Audio `GainNode` processing for authentic acoustic amplification beyond 100%, backed by a downstream `DynamicsCompressorNode` brickwall limiter (-0.5 dBFS) to prevent digital clipping.
- **Parametric Multi-Band Equalizer**: Built-in EQ presets (*Flat Studio, Bass Boost, Cinema Wide, Vocal Clarity, Night Softener*) with real-time biquad filtering.
- **60fps Zero-Allocation Visualizer**: Real-time frequency spectrum visualizer engineered with pre-allocated typed arrays, cached color tokens, and cached 2D canvas contexts for 0ms garbage collection pauses.

### 💬 Precision Subtitle & Dual-Audio Engine
- **Autoplay Subtitles Toggle**: Dedicated toggle under Settings → Playback & Files to automatically activate matching subtitles upon media load or require manual hotkey (`C`) activation.
- **O(log N) Binary Search Matching**: Instantaneous cue synchronization across thousands of subtitle lines, eliminating frame drops during dense dialogue sequences.
- **Cue Equality Memoization**: Guards against redundant DOM reflows by updating the subtitle overlay only when active cue content changes.
- **Native Dual Audio**: Automatic recognition of original and dub streams (`Japanese (Original)`, `English (Dub)`, or ISO language codes) in the audio drawer and keyboard hotkey cycling (`B`).
- **Interactive Dialogue Timeline**: Displays subtitle cue density directly on the seekbar and provides keyboard subtitle delay synchronization (`Shift + G` / `Shift + H`).

### 🎌 Anime Skip & Contiguous Chapter Generation
- **Dynamic Progression Bar Alignment**: The floating "Skip Segment [TAB]" prompt is dynamically positioned pixel-perfect to the horizontal start of the seekbar progression bar across all window sizes and layout changes.
- **AniList & AniSkip Integration**: Strictly verified OP/ED intervals queried asynchronously without stalling local playback starts.
- **Lossless Chapter Baking**: Generates contiguous, non-overlapping chapter markers (`Prologue`, `Opening`, `Episode`, `Ending`, `Preview`) and embeds them losslessly into MP4/MKV containers via FFmpeg stream-copy with safe backup rollback on NTFS.

### 📥 Next-Episode, Multi-Link & Season Batch Downloader
- **Zero-Dependency Stream Downloader**: Native HTTPS segment fetcher and bundled FFmpeg muxer producing single self-contained MP4 files with embedded subtitles and dual audio. Zero stray `.temp_*` or `.vtt` files.
- **Fast URL Incrementing (`+1 Ep`)**: Smart pattern detector that recognizes query strings (`?ep=01`) and path slugs (`/episode-1`), automatically incrementing to the next episode while strictly preserving zero-padding.
- **10-Episode Sliding Window Queue**: Download full seasons sequentially (1 active download at a time) with an in-memory backlog that feeds up to 10 active/pending items at a time without network saturation or bot detection.
- **Just-In-Time (JIT) Headless Sniffing**: Webpage URLs are sniffed only right before each episode begins downloading, keeping background CPU and RAM usage minimal.
- **Strict Option A Track Validation**: Enforces user language preferences (Japanese, English Dub, Dual Audio, specific subtitles). If an episode lacks the requested track, CopyCat flags it with a clear reason, skips downloading the wrong track, and seamlessly advances to the next episode.
- **One-Click Retry**: Easily re-queue any skipped or failed episode directly from the download card with a single click.
- **Dedicated Season Subfolders & Explorer View**: Season downloads automatically create and organize into `C:\Users\Schual\Downloads\CopyCat\<Series Name>\` with standardized filenames (`<Series Name> - Episode 01.mp4`), automatically configured with `desktop.ini` and Windows Explorer view settings to display as **Large Icons** (thumbnails) sorted in **Ascending** order without grouping.
- **Emergency Halt All**: Instant kill-switch that safely terminates active FFmpeg processes, closes open sniffer windows, purges the season backlog, and resets the taskbar progress bar.

### 🎯 Power-User Tools & Playback Controls
- **Live Hover Scrubbing**: Floating live thumbnail preview card with LRU frame caching for instant scrubbing precision.
- **Hold for 2.0x Speed**: Press and hold `Space` or long-press the video canvas to smoothly boost playback to 2.0x with an animated HUD badge; release to restore normal speed.
- **Auto-Pause When Minimized**: Automatically pauses playback upon minimizing to the taskbar and resumes upon restoring (with Picture-in-Picture immunity and pre-paused persistence).
- **Stop After Current Episode (`E`)**: Complete the current file cleanly and stop playback automatically without advancing down the queue.
- **Automated Sleep Timer**: Configurable graceful sleep timer (15m to 120m) with live status readout.
- **Instant Boss Key (`F12`)**: Silences audio, pauses playback, exits fullscreen/PiP, and minimizes the window instantly.
- **Always-on-Top Picture-in-Picture (`P`)**: Native Windows TopMost enforcement via compiled C# helper for guaranteed screen priority over other applications.
- **Watched Episode Tracking**: Session-aware checkmarks and hover tags in the queue list to track series progress.

### 🪟 Native Windows Integration & Non-Blocking Design
- **100% Non-Blocking Main Process**: Zero synchronous child process spawns (`spawnSync`) on startup or hot paths; PATH tool lookup is resolved in memory, and desktop shortcuts are created asynchronously.
- **Shell Associations**: Registers CopyCat as an official media viewer across 21 extensions (`.mp4`, `.mkv`, `.ts`, `.webm`, `.avi`, `.mov`, `.flac`, `.mp3`, `.opus`, etc.).
- **Windows Explorer Context Menu**: Right-click any media file or directory to "Play with CopyCat media player".
- **Taskbar Thumbnail Toolbar & SMTC**: Control playback directly from the Windows taskbar preview (Play/Pause, Next, Previous) and Windows System Media Transport Controls.

### 🤖 Local AI & Script Automation Bridge
- Hosts a high-performance local REST HTTP server on `127.0.0.1:4500` enabled with `TCP_NODELAY`.
- Strictly protected against browser CSRF attacks via loopback origin checks and `Sec-Fetch-Site: cross-site` blocking.
- Enables external scripts, CLI utilities, and AI assistants (Antigravity, Gemini, Copilot, Cursor) to inspect playback state and control the player programmatically via JSON payloads (`/api/status`, `/api/control`).

---

## Keyboard Shortcuts

| Shortcut | Action |
| :--- | :--- |
| `Space` / `K` | Toggle Play / Pause *(Hold Space >220ms for 2.0x Speed)* |
| `F` / Double-Click | Toggle Fullscreen |
| `M` | Toggle Audio Mute |
| `Z` / `A` | **Cycle Aspect Ratio** *(Contain, Cover, Stretch, 16:9, 4:3, 21:9)* |
| `B` | **Cycle Audio Track** *(Dual Audio switch between Original & Dub)* |
| `C` | Toggle Subtitles On / Off |
| `PageDown` / `Ctrl + Right` | Jump to Next Chapter / Anime Segment |
| `PageUp` / `Ctrl + Left` | Jump to Previous Chapter / Anime Segment |
| `←` / `→` | Seek -5s / +5s *(Shift for ±15s, coalesced multi-tap)* |
| `↑` / `↓` | Volume +5% / -5% *(Boost up to 200%)* |
| `[` / `]` | Playback Speed -0.25x / +0.25x |
| `Tab` | Skip Intro / Outro / Segment *(Full Binge Mode available)* |
| `E` | Toggle "Stop After Current Episode" |
| `P` | Toggle Always-on-Top Picture-in-Picture (PiP) |
| `Ctrl + B` | Add Timestamp Bookmark |
| `S` / `Shift + S` | Capture & Save High-Resolution Video Snapshot |
| `F12` | **Boss Key**: Instant Mute, Pause & Minimize |
| `Ctrl + O` | Open Media File |
| `Ctrl + F` | Open Media Folder |
| `Ctrl + P` | Open Preferences & Themes |
| `Escape` | Close Popovers / Exit Fullscreen |

---

## Installation

### Option 1: Standalone Installer or Portable App (Recommended)
Download the latest pre-compiled binaries from the [GitHub Releases](https://github.com/georgschneider96-collab/CopyCat-media-player/releases/latest) page:
- **`CopyCat-media-player-Setup-x.x.x.exe`**: Complete Windows installer with file associations and desktop shortcut.
- **`CopyCat-media-player-x.x.x-portable.exe`**: Zero-install standalone executable.

### Option 2: Run from Source

#### Prerequisites
- [Node.js](https://nodejs.org/) (v18.0.0 or higher recommended)
- npm (bundled with Node.js)
- Windows 10 or 11 (64-bit)

#### Setup & Execution
```powershell
# Clone the repository
git clone https://github.com/georgschneider96-collab/CopyCat-media-player.git
cd CopyCat-media-player

# Install dependencies
npm install

# Start CopyCat Media Player
npm start

# Run Automated Regression & Health Test Suite
npm test
```

#### Compiling Production Binaries
```powershell
# Package standard setup installer and portable executable to dist/
npm run dist

# Package portable executable only
npm run dist:portable
```

---

## Architecture

```text
CopyCat Media Player/
├── package.json              # App manifest, dependencies, and electron-builder packaging config
├── main.js                   # Application bootstrap, GPU acceleration flags, single-instance lock
├── preload.js                # Secure context bridge exposing window.copycat (and alias window.vlcopycat)
├── AI_CONTEXT.md             # Deep technical specification & AI control schema guide
├── README.md                 # Project documentation
├── assets/
│   ├── icon.ico              # Multi-resolution Windows application icon
│   ├── icon.png              # High-resolution raster logo
│   └── thumbar-*.png         # Windows taskbar thumbnail toolbar button icons
├── bin/
│   ├── ffmpeg.exe            # Bundled static FFmpeg media processor
│   ├── ffprobe.exe           # Bundled static FFprobe stream analyzer
│   ├── launcher.cs           # C# Win32 desktop session redirector source
│   ├── pip-helper.cs         # C# Win32 HWND TopMost enforcer source
│   ├── pip-helper.exe        # Compiled always-on-top PiP helper binary
│   ├── open_player.ps1       # PowerShell automation and session summoning script
│   └── register_associations.ps1 # Comprehensive Windows file type association setup
├── src/
│   ├── index.html            # DOM layout: Titlebar, Video Canvas, Drawers, Seekbar, Dialogs
│   ├── styles.css            # Glassmorphic CSS design system, CSS custom properties, animations
│   ├── icons.js              # Crisp inline SVG vector iconography
│   ├── app.js                # Core playback state machine, Web Audio DSP, subtitle engine & event orchestration
│   └── main/                 # Modular Main Process Services (IoC Architecture)
│       ├── container.js      # IoC Service Container & WindowManager
│       ├── downloader.js     # Native stream downloader, sliding-window season queue, JIT sniffer & Dual-Audio muxer
│       ├── stream-manager.js # FFmpeg live fragmented-MP4 transcoding & streaming process lifecycle
│       ├── stream-sniffer.js # Isolated headless stream sniffer & subtitle extractor
│       ├── media-resolver.js # Fast local ffprobe media analyzer, byte-range handler & subtitle extraction
│       ├── anime-skip-resolver.js # AniList & AniSkip integration, chapter generation & baking
│       ├── server.js         # Local HTTP REST automation bridge (port 4500) with origin security guards
│       ├── session.js        # Window bounds, volume, DRC, desktop shortcuts & state persistence
│       ├── updater.js        # HTTPS auto-update checking, SHA-256 verification, and atomic staging
│       ├── ipc.js            # Declarative IPC channel registration
│       └── logger.js         # Scoped structured file & console logging
└── tests/
    └── test-suite.js         # Automated regression test suite (syntax, IoC, security, semver, anime, range)
```
````````

``````javascript
    volumeStep: 5,        // 1% to 25%

    speedStep: 0.10       // 0.01x to 0.50x
  };

  function escapeHtml(str) {
    if (str === null || str === undefined) return '';
    return String(str)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
  }

  function stripLanguageTags(str) {
    if (!str || typeof str !== 'string') return '';
    return str
      .replace(/\s*[\(\[]\s*(?:English(?:\s*(?:Dub|Sub))?|Japanese(?:\s*(?:Original|Sub))?|Dual(?:\s*Audio)?|Dub|Sub|Eng|Jpn|Jap|Original)\s*[\)\]]/gi, '')
      .replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ')
      .trim();
  }

  function formatDoubleDigitEpisode(str) {
    if (!str || typeof str !== 'string') return '';
    let result = str.replace(/(\b(?:Episode|Ep\.?|E)\s*[-_]?\s*)(\d+)\b/gi, (match, prefix, num) => {
      return prefix + (num.length === 1 ? `0${num}` : num);
    });
    result = result.replace(/(\s*[-–—]\s*)(\d+)(\s*)$/, (match, prefix, num, suffix) => {
      return prefix + (num.length === 1 ? `0${num}` : num) + suffix;
    });
    return result;
  }

  // ==================== UNIFIED TIME HELPERS ====================
  function getDuration() {
    if (state.streamMode) {
      return state.streamDuration > 0 ? state.streamDuration : 0;
    }
    return Number.isFinite(el.video.duration) && el.video.duration > 0 ? el.video.duration : 0;
  }

  function getCurrentTime() {
    if (state.scrubPreviewTime !== null) {
      return state.scrubPreviewTime;
    }
    if (state.streamMode) {
      const t = state.streamOffset + (el.video.currentTime || 0);
      return state.streamDuration > 0 ? Math.min(state.streamDuration, t) : t;
    }
    return el.video.currentTime || 0;
  }

  // ==================== AUDIO ENGINE (200% CONE BOOST + EQ + ANALYSER) ====================
  let audioCtx = null;
  let gainNode = null;
  let limiterNode = null;
  let softClipperNode = null;
  let compressorNode = null;
  let delayNode = null;
  let analyserNode = null;
  let eqNodes = null;
  let visualizerRaf = null;

  function ensureAudioGraph() {
    if (audioCtx) {
      if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
        audioCtx.resume().catch(() => {});
      }
      return;
    }
    try {
      const Ctx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
      audioCtx = new Ctx();
      const source = audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(el.video);

      const bass = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
      bass.type = 'lowshelf';
      bass.frequency.value = 110;

      const mid = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
      mid.type = 'peaking';
      mid.Q.value = 1.0;
      mid.frequency.value = 1000;

      const treble = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
      treble.type = 'highshelf';
      treble.frequency.value = 8000;

      compressorNode = audioCtx.createDynamicsCompressor();
      delayNode = audioCtx.createDelay(2.0);

      analyserNode = audioCtx.createAnalyser();
      analyserNode.fftSize = 128;
      analyserNode.smoothingTimeConstant = 0.82;

      gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();

      // Downstream Safety Peak Limiter & Soft-Clipper downstream of the 200% Gain Boost:
      // 1. Fast peak compressor stage: Attenuates heavy gain overshoot with 20:1 ratio and -0.5 dBFS threshold
      limiterNode = audioCtx.createDynamicsCompressor();
      limiterNode.threshold.value = -0.5; // Peak ceiling in dBFS
      limiterNode.knee.value = 0.0;       // Hard knee for rapid response
      limiterNode.ratio.value = 20.0;     // 20:1 high compression ratio
      limiterNode.attack.value = 0.002;   // 2ms ultra-fast attack
      limiterNode.release.value = 0.05;   // 50ms fast recovery to prevent volume pumping

      // 2. Oversampled Soft-Clipper stage: Sigmoid tanh curve catches any instantaneous micro-transients
      // during the 2ms attack window to prevent harsh digital clipping on boosted audio
      softClipperNode = audioCtx.createWaveShaper();
      const clipSamples = 1024;
      const clipCurve = new Float32Array(clipSamples);
      const tanhNorm = Math.tanh(1.25);
      for (let i = 0; i < clipSamples; i++) {
        const x = (i * 2) / clipSamples - 1;
        clipCurve[i] = Math.tanh(x * 1.25) / tanhNorm;
      }
      softClipperNode.curve = clipCurve;
      softClipperNode.oversample = '2x';

      source.connect(bass);
      bass.connect(mid);
      mid.connect(treble);
      treble.connect(compressorNode);
      compressorNode.connect(delayNode);
      delayNode.connect(analyserNode);
      analyserNode.connect(gainNode);
      gainNode.connect(limiterNode);
      limiterNode.connect(softClipperNode);
      softClipperNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);

      eqNodes = [bass, mid, treble];
      applyEqualizer();
      applyNightModeAndDelay();
      applyVolumeToEngine();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('WebAudio fallback active:', err);
    }
  }

  function applyNightModeAndDelay() {
    if (!compressorNode || !delayNode || !audioCtx) return;
    try {
      if (state.nightMode) {
        compressorNode.threshold.setValueAtTime(-24, audioCtx.currentTime);
        compressorNode.knee.setValueAtTime(30, audioCtx.currentTime);
        compressorNode.ratio.setValueAtTime(12, audioCtx.currentTime);
        compressorNode.attack.setValueAtTime(0.003, audioCtx.currentTime);
        compressorNode.release.setValueAtTime(0.25, audioCtx.currentTime);
      } else {
        compressorNode.ratio.setValueAtTime(1, audioCtx.currentTime);
        compressorNode.threshold.setValueAtTime(0, audioCtx.currentTime);
      }
      const delaySec = Math.max(0, Math.min(2.0, (state.audioDelayMs || 0) / 1000));
      delayNode.delayTime.setValueAtTime(delaySec, audioCtx.currentTime);
    } catch {}
  }

  function applyEqualizer() {
    if (!eqNodes) return;
    const gains = EQ_PRESETS[state.eqPreset] || EQ_PRESETS.flat;
    eqNodes[0].gain.value = gains[0];
    eqNodes[1].gain.value = gains[1];
    eqNodes[2].gain.value = gains[2];
  }

  let lastMutedIconState = null;

  function applyVolumeToEngine() {
    const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(200, Math.round(state.volume)));
    state.volume = clamped;

    if (gainNode && audioCtx) {
      el.video.muted = false;
      el.video.volume = 1.0;
      try {
        gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(0);
      } catch {}
      gainNode.gain.value = state.muted ? 0 : clamped / 100;
    } else {
      el.video.muted = state.muted;
      el.video.volume = Math.min(1, clamped / 100);
    }

    // Update cone slider UI (0..200% maps to 0..100% width; 100% sits at 50% tick)
    const fillPct = state.muted ? 0 : clamped / 2;
    el.volConeFill.style.width = `${fillPct}%`;
    const isMuted = state.muted || clamped === 0;
    el.volConeSlider.classList.toggle('muted', isMuted);
    el.volConeSlider.title = `Cone Volume Boost: ${state.muted ? 'Muted' : clamped + '%'} (Up to 200%)`;
    if (isMuted !== lastMutedIconState) {
      el.btnMute.innerHTML = isMuted ? ICONS.speakerMute : ICONS.speaker;
      lastMutedIconState = isMuted;
    }

    el.rngVolumePop.value = String(clamped);
    el.lblVolumePop.textContent = state.muted ? `Muted (${clamped}%)` : `${clamped}%`;
  }

  // ==================== MULTI-TRACK AUDIO & DUAL AUDIO SWITCHER ====================
  const LANG_PATTERNS = {
    en: /en|eng|english/i,
    ja: /ja|jpn|japanese/i,
    de: /de|deu|ger|german|deutsch/i,
    hu: /hu|hun|hungarian|magyar/i,
    es: /es|spa|spanish|español/i,
    fr: /fr|fre|fra|french|français/i,
    it: /it|ita|italian|italiano/i,
    pt: /pt|por|portuguese|português/i,
    ru: /ru|rus|russian/i,
    zh: /zh|zho|chi|chinese/i,
    ko: /ko|kor|korean/i,
    in: /in|id|ind|indonesian/i,
    th: /th|tha|thai/i
  };

  function detectLanguageCodeFromTrack(trackOrObj) {
    if (!trackOrObj) return null;
    const str = typeof trackOrObj === 'string'
      ? trackOrObj
      : `${trackOrObj.lang || ''} ${trackOrObj.language || ''} ${trackOrObj.name || ''} ${trackOrObj.label || ''} ${trackOrObj.title || ''}`;
    for (const [code, pattern] of Object.entries(LANG_PATTERNS)) {
      if (pattern.test(str)) return code;
    }
    const rawLang = (typeof trackOrObj === 'object' ? (trackOrObj.lang || trackOrObj.language) : null);
    if (rawLang && typeof rawLang === 'string' && rawLang.trim().length >= 2 && rawLang.trim().toLowerCase() !== 'und') {
      return rawLang.trim().toLowerCase();
    }
    return null;
  }

  function normalizeLangCode(codeOrName) {
    if (!codeOrName || typeof codeOrName !== 'string') return null;
    const trimmed = codeOrName.trim().toLowerCase();
    for (const [code, pattern] of Object.entries(LANG_PATTERNS)) {
      if (pattern.test(trimmed)) return code;
    }
    return trimmed;
  }

  function matchesLanguage(track, langCode) {
    if (!track || !langCode || langCode === 'none') return false;
    const pattern = LANG_PATTERNS[langCode];
    const lang = (track.lang || track.language || '').toLowerCase();
    const name = (track.name || track.label || track.title || '').toLowerCase();
    if (pattern) {
      return pattern.test(lang) || pattern.test(name);
    }
    const target = langCode.toLowerCase();
    return lang.includes(target) || name.includes(target);
  }

  function getAudioTracksList() {
    if (state.hls && Array.isArray(state.hls.audioTracks) && state.hls.audioTracks.length > 0) {
      return state.hls.audioTracks.map((t, idx) => ({
        index: idx,
        name: t.name || (t.lang ? t.lang.toUpperCase() : `Audio Track ${idx + 1}`),
        lang: t.lang || '',
        enabled: state.hls.audioTrack === idx
      }));
    }

    const currentItem = getCurrentItem();
    const itemTitle = currentItem?.title || currentItem?.name || currentItem?.filePath || '';
    const isDub = /dub/i.test(itemTitle);
    const isAnime = /(?:\[.*?\]|\b(?:ep(?:isode)?\.?\s*\d+|s\d+\s*e\d+|season\s*\d+|owarimonogatari|monogatari|naruto|piece|bleach|jujutsu|frieren|knight|elaina|witch|slime|re:zero|titan|hero|dungeon|isekai|danmachi|sword|art|online|shield|solo|leveling)\b)/i.test(itemTitle)
      || (state.currentChapters && state.currentChapters.length > 0)
      || (state.currentSegments && state.currentSegments.length > 0)
      || (state.subCues && state.subCues.length > 0)
      || /copycat/i.test(itemTitle);

    const resolveTrackLabel = (rawName, langCode, idx, totalTracks) => {
      let name = rawName || '';
      const lang = (langCode || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      if (!name || name === 'SoundHandler' || /^audio\s*track/i.test(name) || /^track\s*\d+/i.test(name) || /^default/i.test(name)) {
        if (/jpn|ja/i.test(lang)) {
          return 'Japanese (Original)';
        } else if (/eng|en/i.test(lang)) {
          return isDub ? 'English (Dub)' : 'English';
        } else if (/ger|deu|de/i.test(lang)) {
          return 'German';
        } else if (/hungarian|magyar|hun|hu/i.test(lang)) {
          return 'Hungarian';
        } else if (/fra|fre|fr/i.test(lang)) {
          return 'French';
        } else if (/spa|es/i.test(lang)) {
          return 'Spanish';
        } else if (isAnime) {
          if (totalTracks > 1) {
            return idx === 0 ? (isDub ? 'English (Dub)' : 'Japanese (Original)') : (isDub ? 'Japanese (Original)' : 'English (Dub)');
          }
          return isDub ? 'English (Dub)' : 'Japanese (Original)';
        }
        return `Audio Track ${idx + 1}${lang ? ` [${lang.toUpperCase()}]` : ''}`;
      }
      if (/japanese|ja-jp|jpn/i.test(name)) return 'Japanese (Original)';
      if (/english|en-us|eng/i.test(name)) return 'English (Dub)';
      if (/hungarian|magyar|hun/i.test(name)) return 'Hungarian';
      return name;
    };

    if (el.video && el.video.audioTracks && el.video.audioTracks.length > 0) {
      const list = [];
      const totalTracks = el.video.audioTracks.length;
      for (let i = 0; i < totalTracks; i++) {
        const t = el.video.audioTracks[i];
        const probed = state.probedAudioTracks?.[i];
        const rawName = t.label || probed?.name;
        const langCode = t.language || probed?.lang || (isAnime ? (isDub ? (i === 0 ? 'eng' : 'jpn') : (i === 0 ? 'jpn' : 'eng')) : '');
        const name = resolveTrackLabel(rawName, langCode, i, totalTracks);
        list.push({
          index: i,
          name,
          lang: langCode,
          enabled: Boolean(t.enabled)
        });
      }
      return list;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(state.probedAudioTracks) && state.probedAudioTracks.length > 0) {
      const totalTracks = state.probedAudioTracks.length;
      return state.probedAudioTracks.map((p, idx) => {
        const langCode = p.lang || (isAnime ? (isDub ? (idx === 0 ? 'eng' : 'jpn') : (idx === 0 ? 'jpn' : 'eng')) : '');
        const name = resolveTrackLabel(p.name, langCode, idx, totalTracks);
        return {
          index: idx,
          name,
          lang: langCode,
          enabled: idx === 0
        };
      });
    }

    return [];
  }

  function syncAudioTracksDropdown() {
    if (!el.selAudioTrack) return;
    const tracks = getAudioTracksList();
    const currentItem = getCurrentItem();
    const itemTitle = currentItem?.title || currentItem?.name || currentItem?.filePath || '';
    const isDub = /dub/i.test(itemTitle);
    const isAnime = /(?:\[.*?\]|\b(?:ep(?:isode)?\.?\s*\d+|s\d+\s*e\d+|season\s*\d+|owarimonogatari|monogatari|naruto|piece|bleach|jujutsu|frieren|knight|elaina|witch|slime|re:zero|titan|hero|dungeon|isekai|danmachi|sword|art|online|shield|solo|leveling)\b)/i.test(itemTitle)
      || (state.currentChapters && state.currentChapters.length > 0)
      || (state.currentSegments && state.currentSegments.length > 0)
      || (state.subCues && state.subCues.length > 0)
      || /copycat/i.test(itemTitle);

    if (tracks.length <= 1) {
      let label = tracks.length === 1 ? tracks[0].name : '';
      if (!label && state.probedAudioTracks && state.probedAudioTracks.length > 0) {
        label = state.probedAudioTracks[0].name;
      }
      if (!label || /^audio\s*track/i.test(label) || /^default/i.test(label)) {
        label = isAnime ? (isDub ? 'English (Dub)' : 'Japanese (Original)') : 'Default Audio (Stereo)';
      }
      if (el.selAudioTrack.options.length !== 1 || el.selAudioTrack.options[0].textContent !== label) {
        el.selAudioTrack.innerHTML = `<option value="0" selected>${escapeHtml(label)}</option>`;
      }
      el.selAudioTrack.disabled = false;
      if (el.lblAudioTrackSub) el.lblAudioTrackSub.textContent = `${label} • Single audio track`;
      return;
    }

    el.selAudioTrack.disabled = false;
    if (el.lblAudioTrackSub) el.lblAudioTrackSub.textContent = `${tracks.length} audio tracks available (Dual Audio)`;
    const anyEnabled = tracks.some((t) => t.enabled);
    const activeTrack = tracks.find((t) => t.enabled) || tracks[0];

    // Rebuild options only when necessary, preserving focus & preventing dropdown flicker
    if (el.selAudioTrack.options.length !== tracks.length) {
      el.selAudioTrack.innerHTML = tracks.map((t, idx) => {
        let label = t.name;
        if (/japanese|ja/i.test(label) || /jpn/i.test(t.lang || '')) label = 'Japanese (Original)';
        else if (/english|en/i.test(label) || /eng/i.test(t.lang || '')) label = 'English (Dub)';
        const isSelected = anyEnabled ? t.enabled : (idx === 0);
        return `<option value="${t.index}" ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''}>${escapeHtml(label)}</option>`;
      }).join('');
    }

    if (activeTrack) {
      el.selAudioTrack.value = String(activeTrack.index);
    }
  }

  function setAudioTrack(trackIndex, isUserInitiated = false, notify = true) {
    const tracks = getAudioTracksList();
    if (tracks.length === 0) return;
    const targetIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(tracks.length - 1, trackIndex));

    const wasPlaying = !el.video.paused;
    const curTime = el.video.currentTime;

    if (state.hls && Array.isArray(state.hls.audioTracks)) {
      state.hls.audioTrack = targetIdx;
    } else if (el.video && el.video.audioTracks && el.video.audioTracks.length > 0) {
      for (let i = 0; i < el.video.audioTracks.length; i++) {
        el.video.audioTracks[i].enabled = (i === targetIdx);
      }

      // Automatically un-stall Chromium's media demuxer:
      // In Chromium, toggling AudioTrack.enabled on an MP4 leaves the demuxer waiting
      // for new audio packets, stalling video playback until an explicit seek occurs.
      // Performing an imperceptible 10ms micro-seek flushes the demuxer buffers and
      // binds the newly selected audio stream immediately, resuming playback seamlessly.
      const dur = getDuration();
      const nudge = (Number.isFinite(dur) && curTime + 0.01 < dur) ? curTime + 0.01 : Math.max(0, curTime - 0.01);
      try {
        el.video.currentTime = nudge;
      } catch {}

      if (wasPlaying) {
        el.video.play().catch(() => {});
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (wasPlaying && el.video.paused) {
            el.video.play().catch(() => {});
          }
        }, 60);
      }
    }

    // Ensure AudioContext is active and Web Audio gainNode is not muted
    if (audioCtx) {
      if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
        audioCtx.resume().catch(() => {});
      }
    }
    applyVolumeToEngine();

    syncAudioTracksDropdown();
    const active = tracks[targetIdx];
    let label = active?.name || `Track ${targetIdx + 1}`;
    if (/japanese|ja/i.test(label) || /jpn/i.test(active?.lang || '')) label = 'Japanese';
    else if (/english|en/i.test(label) || /eng/i.test(active?.lang || '')) label = 'English';
    else if (/hungarian|magyar|hun|hu/i.test(label) || /hun|hu/i.test(active?.lang || '')) label = 'Hungarian';

    if (isUserInitiated && active) {
      const detectedLang = detectLanguageCodeFromTrack(active);
      if (detectedLang) {
        state.activeSessionAudioLang = detectedLang;
      }
      state.activeSessionAudioTrackName = active.name;
    }

    if (notify) {
      showToast(`Audio Track: ${label} (${targetIdx + 1}/${tracks.length})`);
    }
  }

  function cycleAudioTrack() {
    const tracks = getAudioTracksList();
    if (tracks.length <= 1) {
      showToast(tracks.length === 1 ? `Audio: ${tracks[0].name} (Single Track)` : 'No alternate audio track found');
      return;
    }
    const currentIdx = tracks.findIndex((t) => t.enabled);
    const nextIdx = (currentIdx + 1) % tracks.length;
    setAudioTrack(nextIdx, true, true);
  }

  function detectClientSubtitleLanguage(text) {
    if (!text || typeof text !== 'string') return null;
    const clean = text
      .replace(/^WEBVTT[^\r\n]*\r?\n+/i, '')
      .replace(/\d\d:\d\d(?::\d\d)?(?:\.\d+)?\s*-->\s*\d\d:\d\d(?::\d\d)?(?:\.\d+)?/g, '')
      .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '')
      .slice(0, 15000);

    if (/[\u0e00-\u0e7f]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Thai', lang: 'tha' };
    if (/[\u3040-\u30ff]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Japanese', lang: 'jpn' };
    if (/[\uac00-\ud7af]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Korean', lang: 'kor' };
    if (/[\u0400-\u04ff]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Russian', lang: 'rus' };
    if (/[\u0600-\u06ff]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Arabic', lang: 'ara' };
    if (/[\u4e00-\u9fa5]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Chinese', lang: 'chi' };

    const huMatches = (clean.match(/\b(hogy|nem|egy|van|volt|lesz|mert|csak|még|már|vagy|mint|kell|tudom|lehet|ebben|abban|akkor|most|mindig|nagyon|után|senki|semmi)\b/gi) || []).length;
    const idMatches = (clean.match(/\b(yang|dan|di|ini|itu|tidak|ada|dari|untuk|kamu|aku|dia|kita|mereka|bisa|sudah|dengan|akan|apa|pada|saya|kau|kami|jangan|bukan)\b/gi) || []).length;
    const deMatches = (clean.match(/\b(und|der|die|das|ich|nicht|sie|ein|eine|ist|wir|haben|mit|dem|den|aber|wie|dass|sich|oder|wenn)\b/gi) || []).length;
    const esMatches = (clean.match(/\b(que|de|no|el|la|es|un|una|los|las|por|con|para|yo|pero|está|como|más|este|esta)\b/gi) || []).length;
    const frMatches = (clean.match(/\b(que|de|ne|pas|le|la|les|un|une|est|et|des|en|du|pour|avec|dans|mais|elle|nous)\b/gi) || []).length;
    const itMatches = (clean.match(/\b(che|di|non|il|la|un|una|per|con|sono|ma|questo|questa|come|cosa|tutto|anche)\b/gi) || []).length;
    const ptMatches = (clean.match(/\b(que|de|não|o|a|os|as|um|uma|com|para|por|em|este|esta|você|isso|mas)\b/gi) || []).length;
    const enMatches = (clean.match(/\b(the|and|you|that|was|for|are|with|his|they|this|have|from|one|had|not|but|what|all|were|when|your|can|there|out|about)\b/gi) || []).length;

    const scores = [
      { name: 'Hungarian', lang: 'hun', score: huMatches },
      { name: 'English', lang: 'eng', score: enMatches },
      { name: 'Indonesian', lang: 'ind', score: idMatches },
      { name: 'German', lang: 'ger', score: deMatches },
      { name: 'Spanish', lang: 'spa', score: esMatches },
      { name: 'French', lang: 'fra', score: frMatches },
      { name: 'Italian', lang: 'ita', score: itMatches },
      { name: 'Portuguese', lang: 'por', score: ptMatches }
    ].sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);

    if (scores[0] && scores[0].score >= 2) {
      return { name: scores[0].name, lang: scores[0].lang };
    }
    return null;
  }

  function syncSubtitleTracksDropdown() {
    if (!el.selSubTrack || !el.rowSubTrack) return;
    const subTracks = state.probedSubtitleTracks || [];
    if (subTracks.length === 0) {
      if (state.subCues && state.subCues.length > 0) {
        el.rowSubTrack.style.display = 'flex';
        if (el.lblSubTrackSub) {
          el.lblSubTrackSub.textContent = '1 subtitle track loaded';
        }
        const langLabel = state.subTrackLanguage
          ? `${state.subTrackLanguage} (Original)`
          : (state.subTrackName || 'Active Subtitles');
        const isOff = !state.subEnabled;
        el.selSubTrack.innerHTML = `
          <option value="0" ${!isOff ? 'selected' : ''} style="background:#181818;color:#fff;">${escapeHtml(langLabel)}</option>
          <option value="-1" ${isOff ? 'selected' : ''} style="background:#181818;color:#aaa;">Off</option>
        `;
        el.selSubTrack.value = isOff ? '-1' : '0';
        return;
      }
      el.rowSubTrack.style.display = 'none';
      return;
    }

    el.rowSubTrack.style.display = 'flex';
    if (el.lblSubTrackSub) {
      el.lblSubTrackSub.textContent = `${subTracks.length} embedded track${subTracks.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} available`;
    }

    const currentIdx = state.activeEmbeddedSubTrackIndex ?? -1;
    let optsHtml = `<option value="-1" ${currentIdx === -1 ? 'selected' : ''} style="background:#181818;color:#aaa;">Off / External</option>`;
    subTracks.forEach((st, idx) => {
      let label = st.name || `Subtitle Track ${idx + 1}`;
      if (st.lang && st.lang !== 'und' && !label.toLowerCase().includes(st.lang.toLowerCase())) {
        label += ` [${st.lang.toUpperCase()}]`;
      }
      optsHtml += `<option value="${idx}" ${currentIdx === idx ? 'selected' : ''} style="background:#181818;color:#fff;">${escapeHtml(label)}</option>`;
    });
    el.selSubTrack.innerHTML = optsHtml;
    el.selSubTrack.value = String(currentIdx);
  }

  async function selectEmbeddedSubtitleTrack(trackIdx, isUserInitiated = false, notify = true) {
    if (!state.currentItem || !state.currentItem.filePath || !api.extractEmbeddedSubtitle) return;
    const subTracks = state.probedSubtitleTracks || [];
    const track = subTracks[trackIdx];
    if (!track) return;

    try {
      if (notify) showToast(`Loading ${track.name || 'embedded'} subtitle...`);
      const res = await api.extractEmbeddedSubtitle(state.currentItem.filePath, track.index !== undefined ? track.index : trackIdx);
      if (res && res.ok && res.content) {
        state.activeEmbeddedSubTrackIndex = trackIdx;
        state.subEnabled = true;
        if (isUserInitiated) {
          state.userExplicitSubState = true;
        }
        if (!track.name || track.name.startsWith('Subtitle Track') || !track.lang || track.lang === 'und') {
          const detected = detectClientSubtitleLanguage(res.content);
          if (detected) {
            track.name = detected.name;
            track.lang = detected.lang;
          }
        }
        const detectedCode = detectLanguageCodeFromTrack(track) || (track.lang ? normalizeLangCode(track.lang) : null);
        if (isUserInitiated && detectedCode) {
          state.activeSessionSubLang = detectedCode;
        }
        loadSubtitleData({
          name: `${track.name || 'Embedded'} Subtitle`,
          content: res.content
        }, notify, isUserInitiated);
        if (el.swSubEnabled) el.swSubEnabled.classList.add('on');
        if (el.subtitleOverlay) el.subtitleOverlay.style.display = 'block';
        syncSubtitleTracksDropdown();
      } else if (notify) {
        showToast('Failed to extract subtitle track');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Failed to extract subtitle track:', err);
      if (notify) showToast('Error extracting subtitle');
    }
  }

  // ==================== PREFERRED LANGUAGE & SESSION SUBTITLES ====================
  let languagePreferenceAppliedAudio = false;
  let languagePreferenceAppliedSub = false;

  async function applyLanguagePreferencesOnLoad(probe = null) {
    if (!state.currentItem) return;

    // 1. Audio Track Preference
    if (!languagePreferenceAppliedAudio) {
      const prefAudio = state.activeSessionAudioLang || state.preferredAudioLang || 'en';
      if (prefAudio !== 'none') {
        const audioTracks = getAudioTracksList();
        if (audioTracks.length > 0) {
          let matchIdx = -1;
          if (state.activeSessionAudioTrackName) {
            matchIdx = audioTracks.findIndex((t) => t.name && t.name.toLowerCase() === state.activeSessionAudioTrackName.toLowerCase());
          }
          if (matchIdx < 0) {
            matchIdx = audioTracks.findIndex((t) => matchesLanguage(t, prefAudio));
          }
          if (matchIdx >= 0) {
            languagePreferenceAppliedAudio = true;
            const currentActive = audioTracks.findIndex((t) => t.enabled);
            if (currentActive !== matchIdx) {
              setAudioTrack(matchIdx, false, false);
            }
          } else if (audioTracks.length > 1) {
            languagePreferenceAppliedAudio = true;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. Subtitle Track & Visibility Preference
    if (!languagePreferenceAppliedSub) {
      const wantSubtitles = (state.userExplicitSubState !== null)
        ? Boolean(state.userExplicitSubState)
        : Boolean(state.autoPlaySubtitles);

      if (!wantSubtitles) {
        languagePreferenceAppliedSub = true;
        state.subEnabled = false;
        if (el.swSubEnabled) el.swSubEnabled.classList.remove('on');
        if (el.subtitleOverlay) el.subtitleOverlay.style.display = 'none';
        return;
      }

      state.subEnabled = true;
      if (el.swSubEnabled) el.swSubEnabled.classList.add('on');
      if (el.subtitleOverlay) el.subtitleOverlay.style.display = 'block';

      const targetSubLang = state.activeSessionSubLang
        || (state.preferredAudioLang !== 'none' ? state.preferredAudioLang : 'en');

      const subTracks = probe?.subtitleTracks || state.probedSubtitleTracks || [];

      // 2A. Embedded subtitle tracks
      if (subTracks.length > 0 && state.currentItem.filePath && api.extractEmbeddedSubtitle) {
        let matchSubIdx = -1;
        if (targetSubLang && targetSubLang !== 'none') {
          matchSubIdx = subTracks.findIndex((s) => matchesLanguage(s, targetSubLang));
        }
        if (matchSubIdx < 0) {
          matchSubIdx = subTracks.findIndex((s) => matchesLanguage(s, 'en'));
        }
        if (matchSubIdx < 0) {
          matchSubIdx = 0;
        }

        if (matchSubIdx >= 0) {
          languagePreferenceAppliedSub = true;
          await selectEmbeddedSubtitleTrack(matchSubIdx, false, false);
          return;
        }
      }

      // 2B. Companion subtitle (.srt / .vtt)
      if (state.currentItem.autoSubPath) {
        languagePreferenceAppliedSub = true;
        const subData = await api.readSubtitle(state.currentItem.autoSubPath);
        if (subData) {
          loadSubtitleData(subData, false, false);
          return;
        }
      }

      // 2C. Chromium native textTracks
      if (el.video && el.video.textTracks && el.video.textTracks.length > 0) {
        let matchTtIdx = -1;
        if (targetSubLang && targetSubLang !== 'none') {
          for (let i = 0; i < el.video.textTracks.length; i++) {
            if (matchesLanguage(el.video.textTracks[i], targetSubLang)) {
              matchTtIdx = i;
              break;
            }
          }
        }
        if (matchTtIdx < 0) matchTtIdx = 0;
        if (matchTtIdx >= 0) {
          languagePreferenceAppliedSub = true;
          for (let j = 0; j < el.video.textTracks.length; j++) {
            el.video.textTracks[j].mode = (j === matchTtIdx) ? 'showing' : 'disabled';
          }
          return;
        }
      }

      if (probe) {
        languagePreferenceAppliedSub = true;
      }
    }
  }

  // ==================== HOVER THUMBNAIL ENGINE (NATIVE + FFMPEG STREAM) ====================
  const thumbVideo = document.createElement('video');
  thumbVideo.muted = true;
  thumbVideo.preload = 'auto';
  thumbVideo.playsInline = true;
  let thumbSeeking = false;
  let pendingThumbTime = null;

  const streamThumbImg = new Image();
  streamThumbImg.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
  let streamThumbLoading = false;
  let pendingStreamThumbSec = null;
  let lastStreamThumbSec = -999;

  const hoverCtx = el.hoverPreviewCanvas.getContext('2d');

  thumbVideo.addEventListener('seeked', () => {
    drawFittedSourceToHover(thumbVideo, thumbVideo.videoWidth, thumbVideo.videoHeight);
    if (pendingThumbTime !== null && Math.abs(pendingThumbTime - thumbVideo.currentTime) > 0.15) {
      const nextT = pendingThumbTime;
      pendingThumbTime = null;
      try {
        thumbVideo.currentTime = nextT;
      } catch {
        thumbSeeking = false;
      }
    } else {
      pendingThumbTime = null;
      thumbSeeking = false;
    }
  });

  streamThumbImg.addEventListener('load', () => {
    drawFittedSourceToHover(streamThumbImg, streamThumbImg.naturalWidth, streamThumbImg.naturalHeight);
    if (pendingStreamThumbSec !== null && Math.abs(pendingStreamThumbSec - lastStreamThumbSec) >= 1.0) {
      const nextSec = pendingStreamThumbSec;
      pendingStreamThumbSec = null;
      triggerStreamThumbFetch(nextSec);
    } else {
      pendingStreamThumbSec = null;
      streamThumbLoading = false;
    }
  });

  streamThumbImg.addEventListener('error', () => {
    streamThumbLoading = false;
    pendingStreamThumbSec = null;
  });

  function triggerStreamThumbFetch(sec) {
    const item = getCurrentItem();
    if (!item) return;
    streamThumbLoading = true;
    lastStreamThumbSec = sec;
    streamThumbImg.src =
      `http://127.0.0.1:${state.aiPort}/api/thumb?path=${encodeURIComponent(item.filePath)}&t=${sec.toFixed(1)}`;
  }

  function requestThumbAt(seconds) {
    const currentItem = getCurrentItem();
    if (!currentItem || currentItem.isAudio || !state.showHoverThumb) {
      drawPlaceholderThumb(currentItem?.isAudio ? 'AUDIO TRACK' : 'PREVIEW');
      return;
    }

    if (state.streamMode) {
      const rounded = Math.round(seconds * 2) / 2;
      if (Math.abs(rounded - lastStreamThumbSec) < 0.5) return;
      if (!streamThumbLoading) {
        triggerStreamThumbFetch(rounded);
      } else {
        pendingStreamThumbSec = rounded;
      }
      return;
    }

    if (!thumbVideo.src || thumbVideo.readyState < 1) {
      drawPlaceholderThumb('LOADING...');
      return;
    }
``````
