``````javascript
                  <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-muted);">${sub.downloads ? sub.downloads.toLocaleString() + ' DLs' : ''} ${sub.format ? '• ' + sub.format.toUpperCase() : ''}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-left:8px;">
                <button class="ghost-pill-btn btn-dl-sub" style="padding:3px 8px;font-size:11px;" type="button">Download</button>
                <button class="stream-probe-btn btn-use-sub" style="padding:3px 10px;font-size:11px;border-radius:5px;font-weight:600;background:rgba(var(--accent-rgb),0.18);color:var(--accent);" type="button">Use &amp; Embed</button>
              </div>
            `;

            // Download handler
            row.querySelector('.btn-dl-sub').addEventListener('click', async () => {
              if (!sub.downloadUrl || !api.downloadSubtitle) return;
              showToast('Downloading subtitle...');
              const dlRes = await api.downloadSubtitle(sub.downloadUrl);
              const targetPath = (typeof dlRes === 'string') ? dlRes : (dlRes?.targetPath || dlRes?.outputPath || (dlRes?.success ? dlRes.targetPath : null));
              if (targetPath) {
                showToast('Subtitle saved: ' + targetPath);
                if (api.revealInExplorer) api.revealInExplorer(targetPath);
              } else {
                showToast('Failed to download subtitle');
              }
            });

            // Use & Embed handler
            row.querySelector('.btn-use-sub').addEventListener('click', async () => {
              if (!sub.downloadUrl || !api.downloadSubtitle) return;
              showToast('Downloading subtitle for embedder...');
              const dlRes = await api.downloadSubtitle(sub.downloadUrl);
              const targetPath = (typeof dlRes === 'string') ? dlRes : (dlRes?.targetPath || dlRes?.outputPath || (dlRes?.success ? dlRes.targetPath : null));
              if (targetPath) {
                if (el.subEmbedFile) el.subEmbedFile.value = targetPath;
                if (el.subEmbedLang) el.subEmbedLang.value = sub.lang || 'eng';
                if (el.subEmbedTitle) el.subEmbedTitle.value = `${sub.lang?.toUpperCase() || 'Online'} Subtitles`;
                showToast('Subtitle ready for embedding!');
              } else {
                showToast('Failed to fetch subtitle');
              }
            });

            el.subResultsContainer.appendChild(row);
          });
        }
      }

      if (el.btnSubSearchOnline) {
        el.btnSubSearchOnline.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const query = el.subSearchQuery?.value?.trim() || '';
          const lang = el.subSearchLang?.value || 'eng';
          if (!query) {
            showToast('Please enter movie or series name');
            return;
          }
          executeSubtitleSearch(query, lang);
        });
      }

      if (el.btnSubDetectLocal) {
        el.btnSubDetectLocal.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const filePath = el.editorSourcePath?.value?.trim();
          if (!filePath) {
            showToast('Select a video file to look for local companion subtitles');
            return;
          }
          executeSubtitleSearch('', 'all');
        });
      }

      if (el.btnSubBrowseLocal) {
        el.btnSubBrowseLocal.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          if (!api.openSubtitleFileDialog) return;
          const subPath = await api.openSubtitleFileDialog();
          if (subPath && el.subEmbedFile) {
            el.subEmbedFile.value = subPath;
            showToast('Selected subtitle: ' + subPath.split(/[/\\]/).pop());
          }
        });
      }

      // Embed subtitle into video
      if (el.btnStartSubEmbed) {
        el.btnStartSubEmbed.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          const inputPath = el.editorSourcePath?.value?.trim();
          const subPath = el.subEmbedFile?.value?.trim();
          if (!inputPath) {
            showToast('Please select a target video file');
            return;
          }
          if (!subPath) {
            showToast('Please select a subtitle file (.srt, .vtt, .ass) to embed');
            return;
          }

          const mode = el.subEmbedMode?.value || 'soft';
          const lang = el.subEmbedLang?.value?.trim() || 'eng';
          const title = el.subEmbedTitle?.value?.trim() || 'English Subtitles';

          activeSubJobId = 'sub_' + Date.now();

          if (el.subEmbedProgressBox) el.subEmbedProgressBox.style.display = 'block';
          if (el.subEmbedProgressBar) el.subEmbedProgressBar.style.width = '0%';
          if (el.subEmbedPercentLabel) el.subEmbedPercentLabel.textContent = '0%';
          if (el.subEmbedStatusLabel) el.subEmbedStatusLabel.textContent = mode === 'soft' ? 'Remuxing subtitle track...' : 'Burning subtitles into video...';
          if (el.subEmbedCompleteBanner) el.subEmbedCompleteBanner.style.display = 'none';

          try {
            const res = await api.embedSubtitles({
              jobId: activeSubJobId,
              inputPath,
              subPath,
              lang,
              title,
              mode
            });

            if (res && res.success) {
              lastEmbeddedPath = res.outputPath;
              if (el.subEmbedProgressBox) el.subEmbedProgressBox.style.display = 'none';
              if (el.subEmbedCompleteBanner) el.subEmbedCompleteBanner.style.display = 'block';
              showToast('Subtitles embedded successfully!');
              playDownloadCompletionChime();
            } else {
              showToast(res?.error || 'Failed to embed subtitles');
              if (el.subEmbedProgressBox) el.subEmbedProgressBox.style.display = 'none';
            }
          } catch (err) {
            showToast('Embed error: ' + (err.message || err));
            if (el.subEmbedProgressBox) el.subEmbedProgressBox.style.display = 'none';
          } finally {
            activeSubJobId = null;
          }
        });
      }

      if (el.btnSubEmbedCancel) {
        el.btnSubEmbedCancel.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (activeSubJobId && api.cancelMediaJob) {
            api.cancelMediaJob(activeSubJobId);
            showToast('Subtitle embedding canceled');
            if (el.subEmbedProgressBox) el.subEmbedProgressBox.style.display = 'none';
            activeSubJobId = null;
          }
        });
      }

      if (el.btnSubEmbedPlayResult) {
        el.btnSubEmbedPlayResult.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          if (!lastEmbeddedPath) return;
          const bundle = await api.resolvePaths([lastEmbeddedPath]);
          if (bundle) ingestOpenedBundle(bundle, true, { append: false });
          toggleSettingsModal(false);
        });
      }
      if (el.btnSubEmbedShowResult) {
        el.btnSubEmbedShowResult.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (lastEmbeddedPath && api.revealInExplorer) api.revealInExplorer(lastEmbeddedPath);
        });
      }

      // Metadata & Tags Editor
      if (el.btnMetaPull) {
        el.btnMetaPull.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const inputPath = el.editorSourcePath?.value?.trim();
          if (!inputPath) {
            showToast('Select a video file first');
            return;
          }
          inspectTargetFile(inputPath);
          showToast('Pulled latest file metadata tags');
        });
      }

      if (el.btnMetaDetectFn) {
        el.btnMetaDetectFn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const inputPath = el.editorSourcePath?.value?.trim();
          if (!inputPath) {
            showToast('Select a video file first');
            return;
          }
          const fn = inputPath.split(/[/\\]/).pop();
          const parsed = extractMetadataFromFilename(fn, {});
          populateMetadataFields({ filename: fn, tags: {} }, parsed);
          showToast('Parsed show, season & episode from filename');
        });
      }

      if (el.btnSaveMetadata) {
        el.btnSaveMetadata.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          const inputPath = el.editorSourcePath?.value?.trim();
          if (!inputPath) {
            showToast('Please select a target video file first');
            return;
          }
          if (!api.applyMediaMetadata) return;

          const tags = {
            title: el.metaFieldTitle?.value?.trim(),
            show: el.metaFieldShow?.value?.trim(),
            season: el.metaFieldSeason?.value?.trim(),
            episode: el.metaFieldEpisode?.value?.trim(),
            year: el.metaFieldYear?.value?.trim(),
            artist: el.metaFieldArtist?.value?.trim(),
            comment: el.metaFieldComment?.value?.trim(),
            audioLang: el.metaFieldAudioLang?.value?.trim()
          };

          if (el.metaSaveFeedback) el.metaSaveFeedback.textContent = 'Saving metadata tags losslessly...';

          try {
            const res = await api.applyMediaMetadata({ inputPath, tags });
            if (res && res.success) {
              if (el.metaSaveFeedback) el.metaSaveFeedback.textContent = 'Metadata written and integrated successfully!';
              showToast('Metadata saved into video file!');
            } else {
              if (el.metaSaveFeedback) el.metaSaveFeedback.textContent = 'Failed: ' + (res?.error || 'Unknown error');
              showToast('Metadata save error: ' + (res?.error || 'Unknown error'));
            }
          } catch (err) {
            if (el.metaSaveFeedback) el.metaSaveFeedback.textContent = 'Error: ' + err.message;
            showToast('Metadata save error: ' + err.message);
          }
        });
      }

      // Media Editor Progress Listener
      if (api.onMediaEditorProgress) {
        api.onMediaEditorProgress((data) => {
          if (!data || !data.jobId) return;
          if (activeConvertJobId && data.jobId === activeConvertJobId) {
            const pct = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, data.percent || 0));
            if (el.converterProgressBar) el.converterProgressBar.style.width = pct + '%';
            if (el.converterPercentLabel) el.converterPercentLabel.textContent = Math.round(pct) + '%';
            if (el.converterStatsLabel) {
              el.converterStatsLabel.textContent = `Time: ${data.time || '00:00'} | Speed: ${data.speed || '1x'} | ${data.fps || 0} fps`;
            }
          } else if (activeSubJobId && data.jobId === activeSubJobId) {
            const pct = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, data.percent || 0));
            if (el.subEmbedProgressBar) el.subEmbedProgressBar.style.width = pct + '%';
            if (el.subEmbedPercentLabel) el.subEmbedPercentLabel.textContent = Math.round(pct) + '%';
            if (el.subEmbedStatsLabel) {
              el.subEmbedStatsLabel.textContent = `Time: ${data.time || '00:00'} | Speed: ${data.speed || '1x'}`;
            }
          }
        });
      }
    }

    // Periodic status sync for AI Control Bridge
    setInterval(pushPlayerStatus, 1000);
    renderLibraryList({ scrollToTop: true });
    renderRightDrawer();
    updateChromeSolidity();
    pushPlayerStatus();
    initDownloaderUI();
    initMediaEditorUI();

    // Check if desktop shortcut exists; if missing and not dismissed, show prompt banner
    setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        if (!state.dismissShortcutPrompt && !state.currentItem && api.hasDesktopShortcut) {
          const has = await api.hasDesktopShortcut();
          if (!has && el.shortcutBanner && !state.currentItem) {
            el.shortcutBanner.classList.add('visible');
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Desktop shortcut check:', err);
      }
    }, 600);
  }

  init();
})();
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/anime-skip-resolver.js
// Automated MyAnimeList & AniSkip Integration for CopyCat Media Player
// Resolves anime title -> MAL ID via AniList GraphQL, queries AniSkip for exact OP/ED intervals,
// and bakes contiguous chapter atoms directly into downloaded or local MP4/MKV files via FFmpeg stream-copy.

const https = require('https');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const os = require('os');
const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');

const logger = createLogger('AnimeSkipResolver');

// In-memory caches to prevent redundant network requests
const malIdCache = new Map(); // seriesTitle.toLowerCase() -> malId
const skipTimesCache = new Map(); // `${malId}_${episode}` -> skipData

/**
 * Perform a resilient HTTPS request with strict timeout
 */
function requestJson(url, options = {}, postData = null, timeoutMs = 4500) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let parsedUrl;
    try {
      parsedUrl = new URL(url);
    } catch (e) {
      return reject(e);
    }

    const reqOptions = {
      hostname: parsedUrl.hostname,
      port: parsedUrl.port || 443,
      path: parsedUrl.pathname + parsedUrl.search,
      method: options.method || (postData ? 'POST' : 'GET'),
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/128.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
        'Accept': 'application/json',
        ...(postData ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Content-Length': Buffer.byteLength(postData) } : {}),
        ...(options.headers || {})
      },
      timeout: timeoutMs
    };

    const req = https.request(reqOptions, (res) => {
      let data = '';
      res.on('data', (chunk) => { data += chunk; });
      res.on('end', () => {
        try {
          const json = JSON.parse(data);
          resolve({ status: res.statusCode, json, raw: data });
        } catch {
          resolve({ status: res.statusCode, json: null, raw: data });
        }
      });
    });

    req.on('timeout', () => {
      req.destroy();
      reject(new Error(`Request timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms: ${url}`));
    });

    req.on('error', (err) => {
      reject(err);
    });

    if (postData) {
      req.write(postData);
    }
    req.end();
  });
}

/**
 * Extract series title and integer episode number from raw filename or stream title
 * Handles:
 *  - "The Exiled Heavy Knight Knows How to Game the System - Episode 21"
 *  - "The Exiled Heavy Knight Knows How to Game the System - 21"
 *  - "Solo Leveling S01E08 (1080p)"
 *  - "[Erai-raws] DanMachi Season 5 - Episode 03 [1080p][Multiple Subtitle].mp4"
 */
function parseAnimeTitleAndEpisode(rawTitle) {
  if (!rawTitle || typeof rawTitle !== 'string') return null;

  let clean = rawTitle.replace(/\.(mp4|mkv|webm|avi|ts|mov)$/i, '').trim();

  // Strip leading/trailing release group or resolution brackets e.g. [Erai-raws], [1080p]
  clean = clean.replace(/^\[[^\]]+\]\s*/, '').replace(/\s*\[[^\]]+\]$/, '');
  clean = clean.replace(/\s*\([^)]*(1080p|720p|480p|x264|x265|hevc|sub|dub|multi)[^)]*\)/gi, '');
  clean = clean.replace(/\s*\[[^\]]*(1080p|720p|480p|x264|x265|hevc|sub|dub|multi)[^\]]*\]/gi, '');

  let title = '';
  let episode = null;

  // Pattern 1: Title - Episode 21 or Title Ep. 21
  const epWordMatch = clean.match(/^(.*?)(?:[\s\-_]+(?:episode|ep\.?|folge)[\s\-_]*(\d+))(?:\s*\(?.*)?$/i);
  if (epWordMatch) {
    title = epWordMatch[1].trim();
    episode = parseInt(epWordMatch[2], 10);
  }

  // Pattern 2: Title S01E21 or Title S1E21
  if (!episode) {
    const sEpMatch = clean.match(/^(.*?)(?:[\s\-_]+s\d+[\s\-_]*e(\d+))(?:\s*\(?.*)?$/i);
    if (sEpMatch) {
      title = sEpMatch[1].trim();
      episode = parseInt(sEpMatch[2], 10);
    }
  }

  // Pattern 3: Title - 21 (Isolated episode number following a hyphen or separator)
  if (!episode) {
    const hyphenNumMatch = clean.match(/^(.*?)\s*-\s*(\d{1,4})(?:\s*\(?.*)?$/);
    if (hyphenNumMatch) {
      title = hyphenNumMatch[1].trim();
      episode = parseInt(hyphenNumMatch[2], 10);
    }
  }

  // Clean trailing hyphens, colons or spaces from title
  title = title.replace(/[\s\-_:]+$/, '').trim();

  if (title && Number.isInteger(episode) && episode > 0) {
    return { title, episode };
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Resolve series title to MyAnimeList ID via AniList GraphQL
 * Fast, free, robust, and requires zero API keys.
 */
async function resolveMalId(seriesTitle) {
  if (!seriesTitle || typeof seriesTitle !== 'string') return null;
  const key = seriesTitle.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (malIdCache.has(key)) {
    return malIdCache.get(key);
  }

  try {
    const query = `
      query ($search: String) {
        Media(search: $search, type: ANIME) {
          id
          idMal
          title {
            english
            romaji
          }
        }
      }
    `;
    const payload = JSON.stringify({
      query,
      variables: { search: seriesTitle.trim() }
    });

    const res = await requestJson('https://graphql.anilist.co', {}, payload, 4000);
    if (res.status === 200 && res.json && res.json.data && res.json.data.Media) {
      const media = res.json.data.Media;
      const malId = media.idMal;
      if (malId) {
        malIdCache.set(key, malId);
        logger.info(`Resolved "${seriesTitle}" -> MAL ID ${malId} ("${media.title.english || media.title.romaji}")`);
        return malId;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug(`AniList resolution failed for "${seriesTitle}":`, err.message);
  }

  malIdCache.set(key, null);
  return null;
}

/**
 * Low-level query to AniSkip API for raw skip intervals
 */
async function fetchAniSkipRaw(malId, episodeNumber, durationSec = 1420) {
  try {
    const durParam = Math.round(durationSec > 0 ? durationSec : 1420);
    const url = `https://api.aniskip.com/v2/skip-times/${malId}/${episodeNumber}?types=op&types=ed&types=mixed-op&types=mixed-ed&types=recap&episodeLength=${durParam}`;
    const res = await requestJson(url, {}, null, 3500);

    if (res.status === 200 && res.json && res.json.found && Array.isArray(res.json.results)) {
      let op = null;
      let ed = null;

      for (const item of res.json.results) {
        if (!item || !item.interval) continue;
        const start = parseFloat(item.interval.startTime);
        const end = parseFloat(item.interval.endTime);
        if (!Number.isFinite(start) || !Number.isFinite(end) || end <= start) continue;

        if (!op && (item.skipType === 'op' || item.skipType === 'mixed-op')) {
          op = { start, end };
        } else if (!ed && (item.skipType === 'ed' || item.skipType === 'mixed-ed')) {
          ed = { start, end };
        }
      }
      return { op, ed };
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug(`AniSkip raw fetch failed for MAL ID ${malId} ep ${episodeNumber}:`, err.message);
  }
  return { op: null, ed: null };
}

/**
 * Fetch opening and ending skip intervals from AniSkip API
 * Strictly returns verified community intervals for the requested episode without guessing.
 * @param {number} malId MyAnimeList ID
 * @param {number} episodeNumber Episode number
 * @param {number} durationSec Media duration in seconds (for episode boundary validation)
 * @returns {Promise<{ op: { start: number, end: number } | null, ed: { start: number, end: number } | null } | null>}
 */
async function fetchAnimeSkipTimes(malId, episodeNumber, durationSec = 1420) {
  if (!malId || !episodeNumber) return null;
  const cacheKey = `${malId}_${episodeNumber}`;
  if (skipTimesCache.has(cacheKey)) {
    return skipTimesCache.get(cacheKey);
  }

  const dur = durationSec > 0 ? durationSec : 1420;
  const current = await fetchAniSkipRaw(malId, episodeNumber, dur);
  const op = current.op;
  const ed = current.ed;

  if (op || ed) {
    const result = { op, ed };
    skipTimesCache.set(cacheKey, result);
    logger.info(`Anime skip times resolved for MAL ID ${malId} ep ${episodeNumber}: OP=${JSON.stringify(op)}, ED=${JSON.stringify(ed)}`);
    return result;
  }

  skipTimesCache.set(cacheKey, null);
  return null;
}

/**
 * Build a contiguous, gap-free list of chapters across the full video duration
 * Strictly maps only verified AniSkip intervals (no guessing).
 * Clamps all bounds within [0, totalDurationSec]
 */
function buildContiguousChapters(skipData, totalDurationSec) {
  if (!skipData || (!skipData.op && !skipData.ed)) return [];
  const dur = Math.max(10, totalDurationSec || 1420);
  const chapters = [];

  let curTime = 0;

  // 1. Opening segment
  if (skipData.op && skipData.op.start < dur) {
    const opStart = Math.max(0, Math.min(dur - 5, skipData.op.start));
    const opEnd = Math.max(opStart + 5, Math.min(dur, skipData.op.end));

    if (opStart > 1.5) {
      chapters.push({
        start: 0,
        end: opStart,
        title: 'Prologue'
      });
    }

    chapters.push({
      start: opStart,
      end: opEnd,
      title: 'Opening'
    });
    curTime = opEnd;
  }

  // 2. Main Episode content & Ending (strictly if verified ending was returned)
  if (skipData.ed && skipData.ed.start < dur) {
    const edStart = Math.max(curTime + 5, Math.min(dur - 5, skipData.ed.start));
    const edEnd = Math.max(edStart + 5, Math.min(dur, skipData.ed.end));

    if (edStart > curTime) {
      chapters.push({
        start: curTime,
        end: edStart,
        title: 'Episode'
      });
    }

    chapters.push({
      start: edStart,
      end: edEnd,
      title: 'Ending'
    });
    curTime = edEnd;
  }

  // 3. Remainder / Post-Credits / Preview
  if (curTime < dur - 1.0) {
    const isEndingPresent = chapters.some((c) => c.title === 'Ending');
    chapters.push({
      start: curTime,
      end: dur,
      title: isEndingPresent ? 'Preview' : 'Episode'
    });
  }

  return chapters;
}

/**
 * Format chapters array into standard FFmpeg ffmetadata text
 */
function generateFfmetadataText(chapters, title = '') {
  let out = ';FFMETADATA1\n';
  if (title) {
    out += `title=${title.replace(/[=;#]/g, '')}\n`;
  }
  out += '\n';

  for (const ch of chapters) {
    const startMs = Math.round(ch.start * 1000);
    const endMs = Math.round(ch.end * 1000);
    if (endMs <= startMs) continue;

    out += '[CHAPTER]\n';
    out += 'TIMEBASE=1/1000\n';
    out += `START=${startMs}\n`;
    out += `END=${endMs}\n`;
    out += `title=${(ch.title || 'Chapter').replace(/[=;#]/g, '')}\n\n`;
  }

  return out;
}

/**
 * Embed chapter metadata into an existing media file via FFmpeg stream-copy (-c copy)
 * Fast (~0.2s), lossless, and preserves all audio/video/subtitles and faststart.
 * Features resilient backup staging, safe rollback, and guaranteed temp file cleanup.
 */
async function embedChaptersIntoFile(filePath, chapters, title = '', ffmpegPath = null) {
  try {
    await fs.promises.access(filePath, fs.constants.F_OK);
  } catch {
    throw new Error(`Target media file not found: ${filePath}`);
  }

  const ffmpeg = ffmpegPath || require('./media-resolver').FFMPEG_PATH;
  if (!ffmpeg) {
    throw new Error('Bundled FFmpeg executable could not be resolved.');
  }
  try {
    await fs.promises.access(ffmpeg, fs.constants.F_OK);
  } catch {
    throw new Error('Bundled FFmpeg executable could not be resolved.');
  }

  const metaText = generateFfmetadataText(chapters, title);
  const tempMetaPath = path.join(os.tmpdir(), `copycat_meta_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}.txt`);
  const tempOutPath = path.join(path.dirname(filePath), `copycat_tmp_tagged_${Date.now()}_${path.basename(filePath)}`);
  let backupPath = null;

  const cleanupArtifacts = async () => {
    try {
      if (tempMetaPath) await fs.promises.unlink(tempMetaPath).catch(() => {});
    } catch {}
    try {
      if (tempOutPath) await fs.promises.unlink(tempOutPath).catch(() => {});
    } catch {}
    try {
      if (backupPath) await fs.promises.unlink(backupPath).catch(() => {});
    } catch {}
  };

  await fs.promises.writeFile(tempMetaPath, metaText, 'utf8');

  const args = [
    '-y', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error',
    '-i', filePath,
    '-i', tempMetaPath,
    '-map', '0',
    '-map_metadata', '0',
    '-map_chapters', '1',
    '-c', 'copy',
    '-movflags', '+faststart',
    tempOutPath
  ];

  try {
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const child = spawn(ffmpeg, args, {
        windowsHide: true,
        stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe']
      });

      let errText = '';
      child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { errText += d.toString(); });

      child.on('error', (err) => {
        reject(err);
      });

      child.on('close', async (code) => {
        if (code === 0 && fs.existsSync(tempOutPath)) {
          try {
            const stat = await fs.promises.stat(tempOutPath);
            if (stat.size < 1024) {
              return reject(new Error('Embedded output file is empty or corrupted. Original file preserved.'));
            }

            // Resilient file replacement with safe rollback on Windows NTFS
            backupPath = path.join(path.dirname(filePath), `.copycat_bak_${Date.now()}_${path.basename(filePath)}`);
            let replaced = false;

            try {
              // Stage 1: Safely rename original file to temporary backup
              await fs.promises.rename(filePath, backupPath);
            } catch (bkErr) {
              // Fallback: If renaming original directly threw (e.g. active read lock), try atomic copy
              try {
                await fs.promises.copyFile(tempOutPath, filePath);
                replaced = true;
              } catch (copyErr) {
                return reject(new Error(`Could not access or replace target media file: ${copyErr.message}`));
              }
            }

            if (!replaced) {
              // Stage 2: Move the new chapter-tagged file to target filePath
              try {
                await fs.promises.rename(tempOutPath, filePath);
                // Stage 3: Successfully placed! Clean up backup
                try {
                  if (backupPath && fs.existsSync(backupPath)) await fs.promises.unlink(backupPath);
                  backupPath = null;
                } catch {}
                replaced = true;
              } catch (mvErr) {
                // Rollback: Restore original from backup to prevent any data loss!
                try {
                  if (fs.existsSync(backupPath)) {
                    await fs.promises.rename(backupPath, filePath);
                  }
                } catch (restoreErr) {
                  try {
                    await fs.promises.copyFile(backupPath, filePath);
                    await fs.promises.unlink(backupPath);
                    backupPath = null;
                  } catch {}
                }
                return reject(new Error(`Failed to place chapter-tagged file: ${mvErr.message}`));
              }
            }

            logger.info(`Successfully embedded ${chapters.length} anime chapters into: ${filePath}`);
            resolve({ success: true, chapters });
          } catch (swapErr) {
            reject(new Error(`Chapter swap error: ${swapErr.message}`));
          }
        } else {
          reject(new Error(errText.trim() || `FFmpeg chapter embed exited with code ${code}`));
        }
      });
    });

    return { success: true, chapters };
  } finally {
    await cleanupArtifacts();
  }
}

/**
 * Asynchronously probe media file duration via ffprobe without blocking the main event loop
 */
function probeDurationAsync(filePath) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    try {
      const { FFPROBE_PATH } = require('./media-resolver');
      if (!FFPROBE_PATH) return resolve(0);
      fs.promises.access(FFPROBE_PATH, fs.constants.F_OK)
        .then(() => {
          const child = spawn(FFPROBE_PATH, [
            '-v', 'quiet', '-print_format', 'json', '-show_format', filePath
          ], { windowsHide: true });
          let out = '';
          child.stdout.on('data', (d) => { out += d.toString(); });
          child.on('close', (code) => {
            if (code === 0 && out.trim()) {
              try {
                const probeJson = JSON.parse(out);
                const dVal = parseFloat(probeJson.format?.duration || 0);
                return resolve(dVal || 0);
              } catch {}
            }
            resolve(0);
          });
          child.on('error', () => resolve(0));
        })
        .catch(() => resolve(0));
    } catch {
      resolve(0);
    }
  });
}

/**
 * Master automated handler:
 * Resolves anime title + episode number, queries MAL & AniSkip, and bakes chapters into the file.
 * Returns { success: boolean, chapters: Array, animeTitle: string, episode: number }
 */
async function autoTagAnimeFile(filePath, rawTitle = '', durationSec = 0) {
  const fallbackTitle = rawTitle || path.basename(filePath);
  const parsed = parseAnimeTitleAndEpisode(fallbackTitle);

  if (!parsed) {
    logger.debug(`File does not match anime episode naming pattern: "${fallbackTitle}"`);
    return { success: false, reason: 'not_anime_pattern' };
  }

  logger.info(`Detected anime: "${parsed.title}" Episode ${parsed.episode}`);

  // 1. Resolve MyAnimeList ID
  const malId = await resolveMalId(parsed.title);
  if (!malId) {
    logger.info(`No MyAnimeList match found for anime: "${parsed.title}"`);
    return { success: false, reason: 'no_mal_id' };
  }

  // 2. Fetch duration asynchronously if not provided (non-blocking)
  let dur = durationSec;
  if (!dur || dur <= 0) {
    dur = await probeDurationAsync(filePath);
  }
  if (!dur || dur <= 0) dur = 1420;

  // 3. Query AniSkip
  const skipData = await fetchAnimeSkipTimes(malId, parsed.episode, dur);
  if (!skipData || (!skipData.op && !skipData.ed)) {
    logger.info(`No community skip times found on AniSkip for "${parsed.title}" ep ${parsed.episode}`);
    return { success: false, reason: 'no_skip_times', malId };
  }

  // 4. Build contiguous chapters
  const chapters = buildContiguousChapters(skipData, dur);
  if (chapters.length === 0) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'empty_chapters' };
  }

  // 5. Bake chapters into media file
  await embedChaptersIntoFile(filePath, chapters, parsed.title);

  return {
    success: true,
    animeTitle: parsed.title,
    episode: parsed.episode,
    malId,
    chapters
  };
}

module.exports = {
  parseAnimeTitleAndEpisode,
  resolveMalId,
  fetchAnimeSkipTimes,
  buildContiguousChapters,
  generateFfmetadataText,
  embedChaptersIntoFile,
  autoTagAnimeFile
};
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/container.js
// Lightweight Service Container & Dependency Injection Registry for VLCopyCat

const { app } = require('electron');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');
const mediaResolver = require('./media-resolver');
const sessionModule = require('./session');
const updaterModule = require('./updater');
const { createStreamManager } = require('./stream-manager');
const { createDownloadManager } = require('./downloader');

const logger = createLogger('Container');

/**
 * Lightweight Service Container (IoC)
 */
class ServiceContainer {
  constructor() {
    this._services = new Map();
    this._factories = new Map();
  }

  register(name, instance) {
    this._services.set(name, instance);
    return this;
  }

  factory(name, factoryFn) {
    this._factories.set(name, factoryFn);
    return this;
  }

  get(name) {
    if (this._services.has(name)) {
      return this._services.get(name);
    }
    if (this._factories.has(name)) {
      const instance = this._factories.get(name)(this);
      this._services.set(name, instance);
      return instance;
    }
    throw new Error(`Service "${name}" is not registered in the ServiceContainer.`);
  }

  has(name) {
    return this._services.has(name) || this._factories.has(name);
  }

  getAll() {
    const all = {};
    for (const [key, value] of this._services.entries()) {
      all[key] = value;
    }
    for (const key of this._factories.keys()) {
      if (!all[key]) {
        all[key] = this.get(key);
      }
    }
    return all;
  }
}

/**
 * Window & Playback State Manager Service
 */
class WindowManager {
  constructor() {
    this.mainWindow = null;
    this.aiServerPort = 4500;
    this.userAlwaysOnTop = false;
    this.isInPipMode = false;
    this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = false;
    this.restoringFromPipLeave = false;
    this.lastPipBounds = null;
    this.pendingInitialMedia = null;

    this.latestPlayerStatus = {
      app: 'CopyCat media player',
      playing: false,
      title: null,
      filePath: null,
      currentTime: 0,
      duration: 0,
      volume: 100,
      muted: false,
      speed: 1,
      accent: 'orange',
      queueLength: 0,
      streamEngine: 'native'
    };
  }

  getWindow() {
    return this.mainWindow;
  }

  setWindow(win) {
    this.mainWindow = win;
  }

  isAvailable() {
    return Boolean(this.mainWindow && !this.mainWindow.isDestroyed());
  }

  /**
   * Bind all window lifecycle and state events (clean separation from main.js)
   * @param {BrowserWindow} win
   */
  attachWindow(win) {
    this.mainWindow = win;

    const forceShowTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.isAvailable() && !win.isVisible()) {
        this.bringToFront(win);
        this.broadcastState();
      }
    }, 1200);

    win.once('ready-to-show', () => {
      clearTimeout(forceShowTimer);
      this.bringToFront(win);
      this.broadcastState();
      this.updateThumbar(false);

      if (this.pendingInitialMedia) {
        win.webContents.send('media:initial', this.pendingInitialMedia);
      }
    });

    win.webContents.on('did-finish-load', () => {
      if (this.pendingInitialMedia) {
        win.webContents.send('media:initial', this.pendingInitialMedia);
      }
    });

    win.on('maximize', () => this.broadcastState());
    win.on('unmaximize', () => this.broadcastState());
    win.on('enter-full-screen', () => this.broadcastState());
    win.on('leave-full-screen', () => this.broadcastState());

    win.on('minimize', () => {
      this.broadcastState();
      if (this.isAvailable()) {
        win.webContents.send('win:minimize');
      }
    });

    win.on('restore', () => {
      this.broadcastState();
      if (this.isAvailable()) {
        win.webContents.send('win:restore');
      }
      if (this.isInPipMode && !this.restoringFromPipLeave && this.isAvailable()) {
        this.isInPipMode = false;
        win.webContents.send('pip:exitRequested');
        if (this.wasFullscreenBeforePip) {
          this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = false;
          win.setFullScreen(true);
        }
      }
    });

    logger.info('Window lifecycle events successfully attached.');
  }

  getPort() {
    return this.aiServerPort;
  }

  setPort(port) {
    this.aiServerPort = port;
  }

  getStatus() {
    return this.latestPlayerStatus;
  }

  setStatus(status) {
    if (status && typeof status === 'object') {
      this.latestPlayerStatus = { ...this.latestPlayerStatus, ...status };
    }
  }

  getInitialMedia() {
    return this.pendingInitialMedia;
  }

  consumeInitialMedia() {
    const initial = this.pendingInitialMedia;
    this.pendingInitialMedia = null;
    return initial;
  }

  setInitialMedia(payload) {
    this.pendingInitialMedia = payload;
  }

  getUserAlwaysOnTop() {
    return this.userAlwaysOnTop;
  }

  applyAlwaysOnTop(flag) {
    this.userAlwaysOnTop = Boolean(flag);
    if (this.isAvailable()) {
      try {
        this.mainWindow.setAlwaysOnTop(this.userAlwaysOnTop, 'screen-saver');
      } catch {
        this.mainWindow.setAlwaysOnTop(this.userAlwaysOnTop);
      }
    }
    this.broadcastState();
    return this.userAlwaysOnTop;
  }

  broadcastState() {
    if (!this.isAvailable()) return;
    this.mainWindow.webContents.send('win:state', {
      maximized: this.mainWindow.isMaximized(),
      fullscreen: this.mainWindow.isFullScreen(),
      minimized: this.mainWindow.isMinimized(),
      alwaysOnTop: this.userAlwaysOnTop,
      aiPort: this.aiServerPort,
      hasFfmpeg: mediaResolver.hasFfmpeg()
    });
  }

  async enterPip(aspect = 16 / 9) {
    if (!this.isAvailable()) return false;
    this.isInPipMode = true;

    if (this.mainWindow.isFullScreen()) {
      this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = true;
      this.mainWindow.setFullScreen(false);
    } else {
      this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = false;
    }

    if (!this.mainWindow.isMinimized()) {
      this.mainWindow.minimize();
    }

    const res = await sessionModule.resizePipWindow(this.mainWindow, aspect);
    if (res.bounds) {
      this.lastPipBounds = res.bounds;
    }

    setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.isInPipMode && this.isAvailable()) {
        sessionModule.resizePipWindow(this.mainWindow, aspect);
      }
    }, 120);

    this.broadcastState();
    return res.success;
  }

  leavePip(opts = {}) {
    if (!this.isAvailable()) return false;
    this.restoringFromPipLeave = true;
    const wasActive = this.isInPipMode;
    this.isInPipMode = false;
    this.lastPipBounds = null;
    const shouldRestore = opts?.restore !== false;

    if (wasActive && shouldRestore) {
      try {
        sessionModule.restoreMainWindowWin32(this.mainWindow);
        if (this.mainWindow.isMinimized()) this.mainWindow.restore();
        this.mainWindow.show();
        this.mainWindow.focus();
        if (this.wasFullscreenBeforePip) {
          this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = false;
          this.mainWindow.setFullScreen(true);
        }
      } finally {
        setTimeout(() => {
          this.restoringFromPipLeave = false;
          this.broadcastState();
        }, 220);
      }
    } else {
      this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = false;
    }

    this.broadcastState();
    return true;
  }

  bringToFront(win = this.mainWindow) {
    sessionModule.bringToFront(win, this.userAlwaysOnTop);
  }

  updateThumbar(isPlaying = false) {
    sessionModule.updateThumbarButtons(this.mainWindow, isPlaying);
  }

  quit() {
    app.quit();
  }
}

/**
 * Initialize and populate the global Dependency Injection Container
 * @returns {ServiceContainer}
 */
function createAppContainer() {
  const container = new ServiceContainer();

  // 1. Core Window & App State Manager
  const windowManager = new WindowManager();
  container.register('window', windowManager);

  // 2. Stream Process Manager (Dedicated process lifecycle for FFmpeg live streams & preview thumbnails)
  const streamManager = createStreamManager();
  container.register('stream', streamManager);

  // 3. Media Resolver Service
  const mediaService = {
    hasFfmpeg: mediaResolver.hasFfmpeg,
    hasFfprobe: mediaResolver.hasFfprobe,
    getFfmpegPath: () => mediaResolver.FFMPEG_PATH,
    getFfprobePath: () => mediaResolver.FFPROBE_PATH,
    probe: mediaResolver.probeMediaFile,
    probeMediaFile: mediaResolver.probeMediaFile,
    resolveAnimeChapters: mediaResolver.resolveAnimeChapters,
    buildItem: mediaResolver.buildMediaItem,
    buildMediaItem: mediaResolver.buildMediaItem,
    scanFolder: mediaResolver.scanMediaFolder,
    findAutoSubtitle: mediaResolver.findAutoSubtitle,
    extractEmbeddedSubtitleVtt: mediaResolver.extractEmbeddedSubtitleVtt,
    detectNeedsStream: mediaResolver.detectNeedsStream,
    parseByteRange: mediaResolver.parseByteRange,
    resolveCliFile: mediaResolver.resolveCliMediaFile,
    MEDIA_EXTENSIONS: mediaResolver.MEDIA_EXTENSIONS,
    SUB_EXTENSIONS: mediaResolver.SUB_EXTENSIONS
  };
  container.register('media', mediaService);

  // 4. Windows Session Service
  const sessionService = {
    hasShortcut: sessionModule.hasDesktopShortcut,
    createShortcut: sessionModule.createDesktopShortcut,
    registerAssociations: sessionModule.runAssociationsRegistration,
    handleBreakout: sessionModule.handleAiSandboxBreakout,
    bringToFront: sessionModule.bringToFront,
    updateThumbar: (isPlaying) => windowManager.updateThumbar(isPlaying)
  };
  container.register('session', sessionService);

  // 5. GitHub Updater Service
  const updaterService = {
    check: updaterModule.checkForUpdates,
    downloadAndInstall: (asset, win) => updaterModule.downloadAndInstallUpdate(asset, win || windowManager.getWindow())
  };
  container.register('updater', updaterService);

  // 6. Native Downloader Service
  const downloadManager = createDownloadManager({ windowManager });
  container.register('downloader', downloadManager);

  // 7. Structured Logger Service
  container.register('logger', {
    createLogger,
    mainLogger: createLogger('Main')
  });

  // 8. Media Editor Service (Converter, Subtitle Embedder, Metadata Editor)
  const { mediaEditor } = require('./media-editor');
  container.register('mediaEditor', mediaEditor);

  logger.info('Service Container initialized with services: window, stream, media, session, updater, downloader, logger, mediaEditor');
  return container;
}

module.exports = {
  ServiceContainer,
  WindowManager,
  createAppContainer
};
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/downloader.js
// Standalone Native Stream & Video Downloader Service for CopyCat Media Player
// Zero External Dependencies: Uses native Node.js HTTPS segment fetcher + bundled FFmpeg muxer.

const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const https = require('https');
const http = require('http');
const crypto = require('crypto');
const { spawn, exec } = require('child_process');
const { app, dialog, shell } = require('electron');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');
const mediaResolver = require('./media-resolver');
const animeSkipResolver = require('./anime-skip-resolver');
const { extractStreamFromWebpage, getRequiredHeadersForUrl } = require('./stream-sniffer');

const logger = createLogger('Downloader');

const httpsKeepAliveAgent = new https.Agent({ keepAlive: true, maxSockets: 16, keepAliveMsecs: 15000 });
const httpKeepAliveAgent = new http.Agent({ keepAlive: true, maxSockets: 16, keepAliveMsecs: 15000 });

/**
 * Strip language and dub/sub/audio indicators from titles, filenames, and folder names
 * Example: "Series Name - Episode 1 (English)" -> "Series Name - Episode 1"
 */
function stripLanguageTags(str) {
  if (!str || typeof str !== 'string') return '';
  return str
    .replace(/\s*[\(\[]\s*(?:English(?:\s*(?:Dub|Sub))?|Japanese(?:\s*(?:Original|Sub))?|Dual(?:\s*Audio)?|Dub|Sub|Eng|Jpn|Jap|Original)\s*[\)\]]/gi, '')
    .replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ')
    .trim();
}

/**
 * Format episode numbers in titles and filenames with at least two digits (e.g. Episode 01, 02)
 * Handles: "Episode 1" -> "Episode 01", "Ep 2" -> "Ep 02", "Ep. 3" -> "Ep. 03", "E4" -> "E04", " - 5" -> " - 05"
 */
function formatDoubleDigitEpisode(str) {
  if (!str || typeof str !== 'string') return '';
  let result = str.replace(/(\b(?:Episode|Ep\.?|E)\s*[-_]?\s*)(\d+)\b/gi, (match, prefix, num) => {
    return prefix + (num.length === 1 ? `0${num}` : num);
  });
  result = result.replace(/(\s*[-–—]\s*)(\d+)(\s*)$/, (match, prefix, num, suffix) => {
    return prefix + (num.length === 1 ? `0${num}` : num) + suffix;
  });
  return result;
}

/**
 * Sanitize filename for safe Windows NTFS storage
 */
function sanitizeFilename(name) {
  let clean = formatDoubleDigitEpisode(stripLanguageTags(name || 'video'))
    .replace(/^Watch\s+/i, '')
    .replace(/\s*[-–—|·]\s*(Miruro|Watch Online|Full HD|JobbMintATv(?:\.pro)?|Indavideo|Online Ingyen Nézhető).*$/i, '')
    .replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '_')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
    .replace(/[.\s]+$/, '');
  return formatDoubleDigitEpisode(clean.slice(0, 180)) || (`video_${Date.now()}`);
}

/**
 * Convert seconds into HH:MM:SS or MM:SS format
 */
function formatDuration(sec) {
  if (!sec || isNaN(sec) || sec <= 0) return '00:00';
  const h = Math.floor(sec / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60);
  const s = Math.floor(sec % 60);
  if (h > 0) {
    return `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  }
  return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/**
 * Format bytes into human readable string
 */
function formatBytes(bytes) {
  if (!bytes || isNaN(bytes) || bytes <= 0) return '0 B';
  const units = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
  let i = 0;
  let val = bytes;
  while (val >= 1024 && i < units.length - 1) {
    val /= 1024;
    i++;
  }
  return i === 0 ? `${Math.round(val)} B` : `${val.toFixed(1)} ${units[i]}`;
}

/**
 * Parse FFmpeg timestamp string (HH:MM:SS.ss) into seconds
 */
function parseTimestamp(timeStr) {
  if (!timeStr) return 0;
  const parts = timeStr.trim().split(':');
  if (parts.length === 3) {
    return (parseFloat(parts[0]) * 3600) + (parseFloat(parts[1]) * 60) + parseFloat(parts[2]);
  }
  if (parts.length === 2) {
    return (parseFloat(parts[0]) * 60) + parseFloat(parts[1]);
  }
  return parseFloat(timeStr) || 0;
}

/**
 * Build standard request headers for stream CDNs (uses dynamic host header registry + strm.cx pattern matching)
 */
function getStreamHeaders(targetUrl, customHeaders = {}) {
  return getRequiredHeadersForUrl(targetUrl, customHeaders);
}

function normalizeIsoLang(lang, fallbackName = '') {
  const l = (lang || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const n = (fallbackName || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (/^ja/i.test(l) || /japanese|jpn/i.test(n)) return 'jpn';
  if (/^en/i.test(l) || /english|eng/i.test(n)) return 'eng';
  if (/^(in|id)/i.test(l) || /indonesian|indo/i.test(n)) return 'ind';
  if (/^th/i.test(l) || /thai/i.test(n)) return 'tha';
  if (/^(de|ger)/i.test(l) || /german|deutsch/i.test(n)) return 'ger';
  if (/^(es|spa)/i.test(l) || /spanish|espanol/i.test(n)) return 'spa';
  if (/^(fr|fra|fre)/i.test(l) || /french|francais/i.test(n)) return 'fra';
  if (/^(it|ita)/i.test(l) || /italian/i.test(n)) return 'ita';
  if (/^(ko|kor)/i.test(l) || /korean/i.test(n)) return 'kor';
  if (/^(zh|chi|zho)/i.test(l) || /chinese/i.test(n)) return 'chi';
  if (/^(pt|por)/i.test(l) || /portuguese/i.test(n)) return 'por';
  if (/^(ru|rus)/i.test(l) || /russian/i.test(n)) return 'rus';
  if (/^(ar|ara)/i.test(l) || /arabic/i.test(n)) return 'ara';
  if (/^(vi|vie)/i.test(l) || /vietnamese/i.test(n)) return 'vie';
  return l.length === 3 ? l : (l || 'und');
}

/**
 * Parse episode number, padding, and surrounding pattern from a streaming/webpage URL
 */
function parseEpisodeFromUrl(url) {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return null;
  const patterns = [
    { regex: /([?&](?:ep|episode|e)=)(\d+)/i, type: 'query' },
    { regex: /((?:[/-]|^)(?:episode|ep)[/-])(\d+)/i, type: 'slug' },
    { regex: /((?:[/-]|^)e)(\d+)(?=[/-]|\.|$)/i, type: 'short' },
    { regex: /([-_.])(\d+)(?=(?:\.[a-z0-9]+|\/)?$)/i, type: 'tail' }
  ];
  for (const { regex, type } of patterns) {
    const m = url.match(regex);
    if (m) {
      return {
        prefix: m[1],
        numStr: m[2],
        num: parseInt(m[2], 10),
        padLength: m[2].length,
        index: m.index,
        fullMatch: m[0],
        type
      };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Increment episode number in URL, preserving zero-padding
 */
function incrementEpisodeUrl(url, step = 1) {
  const info = parseEpisodeFromUrl(url);
  if (!info) return null;
  const nextNum = Math.max(1, info.num + step);
  const nextNumStr = String(nextNum).padStart(info.padLength, '0');
  const replacement = info.prefix + nextNumStr;
  return url.replace(info.fullMatch, replacement);
}

/**
 * Replace episode number in URL with a target number, preserving padding
 */
function replaceEpisodeInUrl(url, targetNum) {
  const info = parseEpisodeFromUrl(url);
  if (!info) return url;
  const numStr = String(targetNum).padStart(info.padLength, '0');
  const replacement = info.prefix + numStr;
  return url.replace(info.fullMatch, replacement);
}

/**
 * Extract a reasonable Series Title guess from URL path or user filename
 */
function extractSeriesTitleGuess(url, rawTitle = '') {
  if (rawTitle && rawTitle.trim()) {
    let clean = stripLanguageTags(rawTitle.trim())
      .replace(/\s*[-_]\s*episode\s*\d+/i, '')
      .replace(/\s*[-_]\s*ep\s*\d+/i, '')
      .replace(/\s*E\d+\b/i, '')
      .trim();
    if (clean) return clean;
  }
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(url);
    const pathParts = parsed.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
    for (let i = pathParts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      let segment = pathParts[i]
        .replace(/\.(html|php|mp4|m3u8)$/i, '')
        .replace(/[-_]?(?:episode|ep)[-_]?\d+/i, '')
        .replace(/[-_]?e\d+/i, '')
        .replace(/[-_]\d+$/, '')
        .replace(/[-_]+/g, ' ')
        .trim();
      if (segment && !/^(watch|video|play|anime|stream)$/i.test(segment)) {
        segment = stripLanguageTags(segment);
        return segment.replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase());
      }
    }
  } catch {}
  return 'Anime Series';
}

/**
 * Core Download Manager Class
 */
class DownloadManager {
  constructor({ windowManager } = {}) {
    this.windowManager = windowManager;
    this.activeProcesses = new Map(); // id -> childProcess
    this.activeControllers = new Map(); // id -> { cancelled: boolean }
    this.queue = []; // array of task items
    this.history = [];
    this.seasonBacklog = []; // Backlog of season episodes waiting for sliding window
    this.activeTaskId = null; // Currently executing task id
    this._isProcessingQueue = false;
    const baseDownloads = app && typeof app.getPath === 'function'
      ? app.getPath('downloads')
      : path.join(os.homedir(), 'Downloads');
    this.downloadFolder = path.join(baseDownloads, 'CopyCat');
    this.ensureFolder(this.downloadFolder);
  }

  ensureFolder(dir) {
    try {
      if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      }
      this.optimizeFolderView(dir);
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error('Failed to create download folder:', err.message);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Configure Windows Explorer view for downloads and season folders:
   * Sets folder template to 'Videos', displays items as Large Icons (big symbols),
   * sorts ascending by item name, and removes date grouping.
   */
  optimizeFolderView(folderPath, updateOpenExplorer = false) {
    if (!folderPath || typeof folderPath !== 'string') return;
    try {
      const resolved = path.resolve(folderPath);
      if (!fs.existsSync(resolved)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(resolved, { recursive: true });
      }

      if (process.platform === 'win32') {
        const iniPath = path.join(resolved, 'desktop.ini');
        let needsWrite = true;
        try {
          if (fs.existsSync(iniPath)) {
            const existing = fs.readFileSync(iniPath, 'utf8');
            if (existing.includes('FolderType=Videos') && existing.includes('Mode=1')) {
              needsWrite = false;
            }
          }
        } catch {}

        if (needsWrite) {
          const content = '[.ShellClassInfo]\r\nConfirmFileOp=0\r\n[ViewState]\r\nMode=1\r\nVid=\r\nFolderType=Videos\r\n';
          try {
            fs.writeFileSync(iniPath, content, 'utf8');
            exec(`attrib +h +s "${iniPath}" & attrib +r "${resolved}"`, { windowsHide: true }, () => {});
          } catch (writeErr) {
            logger.debug('desktop.ini write note:', writeErr.message);
          }
        }

        const applyExplorerView = () => {
          try {
            const escaped = resolved.replace(/'/g, "''");
            const psScript = `
              $ErrorActionPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
              $target = '${escaped}'
              $shell = New-Object -ComObject Shell.Application
              foreach ($w in $shell.Windows()) {
                try {
                  if ($w.Document.Folder.Self.Path -eq $target) {
                    $w.Document.GroupBy = 'System.Null'
                    $w.Document.CurrentViewMode = 1
                    $w.Document.IconSize = 96
                    $w.Document.SortColumns = 'prop:System.ItemNameDisplay;'
                  }
                } catch {}
              }
            `;
            const b64 = Buffer.from(psScript, 'utf16le').toString('base64');
            exec(`powershell.exe -NoProfile -NonInteractive -WindowStyle Hidden -EncodedCommand ${b64}`, { windowsHide: true }, () => {});
          } catch {}
        };

        applyExplorerView();
        if (updateOpenExplorer) {
          setTimeout(applyExplorerView, 400);
          setTimeout(applyExplorerView, 1200);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      logger.debug('optimizeFolderView error:', err.message);
    }
  }

  getDownloadFolder() {
    return this.downloadFolder;
  }

  setDownloadFolder(folder) {
    if (folder && typeof folder === 'string') {
      this.downloadFolder = folder.trim();
      this.ensureFolder(this.downloadFolder);
      return this.downloadFolder;
    }
    return this.downloadFolder;
  }

  async chooseDownloadFolder() {
    const win = this.windowManager?.getWindow();
    const result = await dialog.showOpenDialog(win || undefined, {
      title: 'Select CopyCat Downloads Folder',
      defaultPath: this.downloadFolder,
      properties: ['openDirectory', 'createDirectory']
    });

    if (!result.canceled && result.filePaths && result.filePaths.length > 0) {
      return this.setDownloadFolder(result.filePaths[0]);
    }
    return this.downloadFolder;
  }

  openDownloadFolder() {
    this.ensureFolder(this.downloadFolder);
    shell.openPath(this.downloadFolder);
    this.optimizeFolderView(this.downloadFolder, true);
  }

  /**
   * Resilient HTTP/HTTPS buffer fetcher with redirect following & HTTP 429 exponential backoff
   */
  _fetchBuffer(targetUrl, customHeaders = {}, retries = 6, maxRedirects = 5, cancelState = null) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const attempt = (urlToFetch, retriesLeft, redirectsLeft) => {
        if (cancelState?.cancelled) {
          return reject(new Error('Download cancelled'));
        }
        try {
          const parsed = new URL(urlToFetch);
          const isHttp = parsed.protocol === 'http:';
          const client = isHttp ? http : https;
          const agent = isHttp ? httpKeepAliveAgent : httpsKeepAliveAgent;

          const req = client.get(urlToFetch, {
            agent,
            headers: getStreamHeaders(urlToFetch, customHeaders),
            timeout: 18000
          }, (res) => {
            if (res.statusCode >= 300 && res.statusCode < 400 && res.headers.location && redirectsLeft > 0) {
              res.resume();
              const nextUrl = new URL(res.headers.location, urlToFetch).href;
              return attempt(nextUrl, retriesLeft, redirectsLeft - 1);
            }

            if ((res.statusCode === 429 || (res.statusCode >= 500 && res.statusCode < 600)) && retriesLeft > 0) {
              res.resume();
              const backoffMs = (7 - retriesLeft) * 1150;
              return setTimeout(() => attempt(urlToFetch, retriesLeft - 1, redirectsLeft), backoffMs);
            }

            if (res.statusCode !== 200 && res.statusCode !== 206) {
              res.resume();
              return reject(new Error(`HTTP ${res.statusCode}`));
            }

            const chunks = [];
            res.on('data', (c) => {
              if (cancelState?.cancelled) {
                req.destroy();
                return reject(new Error('Download cancelled'));
              }
              chunks.push(c);
            });
            res.on('end', () => resolve(Buffer.concat(chunks)));
            res.on('error', (err) => {
              if (retriesLeft > 0 && !cancelState?.cancelled) {
                return setTimeout(() => attempt(urlToFetch, retriesLeft - 1, redirectsLeft), 900);
              }
              reject(err);
            });
          });

          req.on('timeout', () => {
            req.destroy();
            if (retriesLeft > 0 && !cancelState?.cancelled) {
              return setTimeout(() => attempt(urlToFetch, retriesLeft - 1, redirectsLeft), 1000);
            }
            reject(new Error('Request timeout'));
          });

          req.on('error', (err) => {
            if (retriesLeft > 0 && !cancelState?.cancelled) {
              return setTimeout(() => attempt(urlToFetch, retriesLeft - 1, redirectsLeft), 900);
            }
            reject(err);
          });
        } catch (err) {
          reject(err);
        }
      };

      attempt(targetUrl, retries, maxRedirects);
    });
  }

  /**
   * Parse an HLS media playlist (.m3u8) into segments, duration, init map, and AES-128 key info
   */
  _parseMediaM3u8(playlistUrl, text) {
    const lines = text.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const segments = [];
    let durationSec = 0;
    let initUrl = null;
    let keyUrl = null;
    let keyIv = null;

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      const line = lines[i];
      if (line.startsWith('#EXTINF:')) {
        const dur = parseFloat(line.replace('#EXTINF:', '')) || 0;
        durationSec += dur;
      } else if (line.startsWith('#EXT-X-MAP:')) {
        const m = line.match(/URI=["']([^"']+)["']/);
        if (m && m[1]) {
          try { initUrl = new URL(m[1], playlistUrl).href; } catch {}
        }
      } else if (line.startsWith('#EXT-X-KEY:')) {
        const methodMatch = line.match(/METHOD=([^,\s]+)/);
        if (methodMatch && methodMatch[1] === 'AES-128') {
          const uriMatch = line.match(/URI=["']([^"']+)["']/);
          if (uriMatch && uriMatch[1]) {
            try { keyUrl = new URL(uriMatch[1], playlistUrl).href; } catch {}
          }
          const ivMatch = line.match(/IV=0x([0-9a-fA-F]+)/);
          if (ivMatch && ivMatch[1]) {
            keyIv = Buffer.from(ivMatch[1].padStart(32, '0'), 'hex');
          }
        }
      } else if (!line.startsWith('#')) {
        try {
          segments.push(new URL(line, playlistUrl).href);
        } catch {}
      }
    }

    return {
      playlistUrl,
      segments,
      durationSec,
      initUrl,
      keyUrl,
      keyIv
    };
  }

  /**
   * Resolve an HLS Master or Media playlist into video variants, audio renditions, and subtitle tracks
   */
  async resolveHlsManifest(m3u8Url, customHeaders = {}, cancelState = null, options = {}) {
    const masterBuf = await this._fetchBuffer(m3u8Url, customHeaders, 4, 5, cancelState);
    const masterText = masterBuf.toString('utf8');
    const lines = masterText.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);

    if (masterText.includes('#EXT-X-STREAM-INF')) {
      const audioTracks = [];
      const subtitleTracks = [];
      let bestAudioUrl = null;

      for (const line of lines) {
        if (line.startsWith('#EXT-X-MEDIA:')) {
          const uriMatch = line.match(/URI=["']([^"']+)["']/);
          const nameMatch = line.match(/NAME=["']([^"']+)["']/);
          const langMatch = line.match(/LANGUAGE=["']([^"']+)["']/);
          const isDefault = /DEFAULT=YES/i.test(line);

          if (uriMatch && uriMatch[1]) {
            try {
              const candidateUrl = new URL(uriMatch[1], m3u8Url).href;
              if (line.includes('TYPE=AUDIO')) {
                let trackName = (nameMatch && nameMatch[1]) || (langMatch && langMatch[1]) || `Audio ${audioTracks.length + 1}`;
                const trackLang = (langMatch && langMatch[1]) || 'und';
                if (/japanese|ja-jp|jpn/i.test(trackName) || /^ja/i.test(trackLang)) {
                  trackName = 'Japanese';
                } else if (/english|en-us|eng/i.test(trackName) || /^en/i.test(trackLang)) {
                  trackName = 'English';
                } else {
                  trackName = trackName.replace(/\s*\([^)]*\)/g, '').trim() || trackName;
                }
                audioTracks.push({
                  id: String(audioTracks.length),
                  name: trackName,
                  lang: trackLang,
                  isDefault,
                  url: candidateUrl
                });
                if (!bestAudioUrl || isDefault) {
                  bestAudioUrl = candidateUrl;
                }
              } else if (line.includes('TYPE=SUBTITLES')) {
                const subName = (nameMatch && nameMatch[1]) || (langMatch && langMatch[1]) || `Subtitles ${subtitleTracks.length + 1}`;
                const subLang = (langMatch && langMatch[1]) || 'und';
                subtitleTracks.push({
                  id: String(subtitleTracks.length),
                  label: subName,
                  lang: subLang,
                  url: candidateUrl
                });
              }
            } catch {}
          }
        }
      }

      const videoVariants = [];
      let bestVideoUrl = null;
      let maxBw = -1;
      for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        if (lines[i].startsWith('#EXT-X-STREAM-INF')) {
          const bwMatch = lines[i].match(/BANDWIDTH=(\d+)/);
          const resMatch = lines[i].match(/RESOLUTION=(\d+x\d+)/);
          const bw = bwMatch ? parseInt(bwMatch[1], 10) : 0;
          const resolution = resMatch ? resMatch[1] : '';
          const height = resolution ? parseInt(resolution.split('x')[1], 10) || 0 : 0;

          for (let j = i + 1; j < lines.length; j++) {
            if (!lines[j].startsWith('#')) {
              try {
                const variantUrl = new URL(lines[j], m3u8Url).href;
                const mbps = bw > 0 ? `${(bw / 1000000).toFixed(1)} Mbps` : '';
                const label = height > 0
                  ? `${height}p (${resolution})${mbps ? ` • ${mbps}` : ''}`
                  : (mbps || `Variant ${videoVariants.length + 1}`);
                videoVariants.push({
                  id: String(videoVariants.length),
                  label,
                  resolution,
                  height,
                  bandwidth: bw,
                  url: variantUrl
                });
                if (bw > maxBw || !bestVideoUrl) {
                  maxBw = bw;
                  bestVideoUrl = variantUrl;
                }
              } catch {}
              break;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      videoVariants.sort((a, b) => (b.bandwidth || 0) - (a.bandwidth || 0));

      // Validate that user-selected variant URLs actually belong to this master playlist
      let targetVideoUrl = bestVideoUrl || m3u8Url;
      if (options.selectedVideoUrl && videoVariants.some((v) => v.url === options.selectedVideoUrl)) {
        targetVideoUrl = options.selectedVideoUrl;
      }

      let targetAudioUrl = bestAudioUrl;
      if (options.selectedAudioUrl && audioTracks.some((a) => a.url === options.selectedAudioUrl)) {
        targetAudioUrl = options.selectedAudioUrl;
      } else if (options.audioPreference) {
        if (/eng|dub/i.test(options.audioPreference)) {
          const engTrack = audioTracks.find((a) => /english|en/i.test(a.name || '') || /^en/i.test(a.lang || ''));
          if (engTrack) targetAudioUrl = engTrack.url;
        } else if (/jpn|jap/i.test(options.audioPreference)) {
          const jpnTrack = audioTracks.find((a) => /japanese|ja/i.test(a.name || '') || /^ja/i.test(a.lang || ''));
          if (jpnTrack) targetAudioUrl = jpnTrack.url;
        }
      }

      const videoBuf = await this._fetchBuffer(targetVideoUrl, customHeaders, 4, 5, cancelState);
      const videoPlaylist = this._parseMediaM3u8(targetVideoUrl, videoBuf.toString('utf8'));

      let audioPlaylist = null;
      const allAudioPlaylists = [];

      if (options.dualAudio && audioTracks.length > 1) {
        for (const aTrack of audioTracks) {
          if (aTrack.url && aTrack.url !== targetVideoUrl) {
            try {
              const aBuf = await this._fetchBuffer(aTrack.url, customHeaders, 4, 5, cancelState);
              const parsedA = this._parseMediaM3u8(aTrack.url, aBuf.toString('utf8'));
              parsedA.name = aTrack.name;
              parsedA.lang = aTrack.lang;
              allAudioPlaylists.push(parsedA);
              if (!audioPlaylist) audioPlaylist = parsedA;
            } catch (err) {
              logger.warn(`Failed to fetch audio track ${aTrack.name}:`, err.message);
            }
          }
        }
      } else if (targetAudioUrl && targetAudioUrl !== targetVideoUrl) {
        try {
          const audioBuf = await this._fetchBuffer(targetAudioUrl, customHeaders, 4, 5, cancelState);
          audioPlaylist = this._parseMediaM3u8(targetAudioUrl, audioBuf.toString('utf8'));
          const matchedTrack = audioTracks.find((t) => t.url === targetAudioUrl);
          if (matchedTrack) {
            audioPlaylist.name = matchedTrack.name;
            audioPlaylist.lang = matchedTrack.lang;
          }
          allAudioPlaylists.push(audioPlaylist);
        } catch (err) {
          logger.warn('Failed to fetch separate audio playlist:', err.message);
        }
      }

      return {
        isMaster: true,
        masterUrl: m3u8Url,
        videoUrl: targetVideoUrl,
        audioUrl: audioPlaylist ? targetAudioUrl : null,
        durationSec: videoPlaylist.durationSec || audioPlaylist?.durationSec || 0,
        videoVariants,
        audioTracks,
        subtitleTracks,
        videoPlaylist,
        audioPlaylist,
        allAudioPlaylists
      };
    }

    const videoPlaylist = this._parseMediaM3u8(m3u8Url, masterText);
    return {
      isMaster: false,
      masterUrl: m3u8Url,
      videoUrl: m3u8Url,
      audioUrl: null,
      durationSec: videoPlaylist.durationSec || 0,
      videoVariants: [{ id: '0', label: 'Default Stream', resolution: '', height: 0, bandwidth: 0, url: m3u8Url }],
      audioTracks: [],
      subtitleTracks: [],
      videoPlaylist,
      audioPlaylist: null,
      allAudioPlaylists: []
    };
  }

  /**
   * Download and prepare subtitle track into a clean WebVTT file
   * Handles direct .vtt/.srt files and HLS multi-segment subtitle playlists (.m3u8)
   */
  async _downloadSubtitleTrack(sub, outVttPath, customHeaders = {}, cancelState = null) {
    if (!sub) return false;
    if (sub.content && typeof sub.content === 'string' && sub.content.length > 10) {
      await fs.promises.writeFile(outVttPath, sub.content, 'utf8');
      return true;
    }
    if (!sub.url || sub.url.startsWith('blob:')) return false;
    try {
      const subBuf = await this._fetchBuffer(sub.url, customHeaders, 5, 5, cancelState);
      const subText = subBuf.toString('utf8');
      const cleanText = subText.replace(/^\uFEFF/, '').trim();

      // Check if it is already direct WebVTT or SRT text
      if (cleanText.startsWith('WEBVTT') || /^\d+\r?\n\d\d:\d\d:/m.test(cleanText) || cleanText.includes('-->')) {
        let vttOut = cleanText;
        if (!vttOut.startsWith('WEBVTT')) {
          vttOut = `WEBVTT\n\n${vttOut}`;
        }
        await fs.promises.writeFile(outVttPath, vttOut, 'utf8');
        return true;
      }

      // Check for JSON timed text / cues format
      if (cleanText.startsWith('{') || cleanText.startsWith('[')) {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(cleanText);
          const cues = parsed.cues || parsed.events || (Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : null);
          if (Array.isArray(cues) && cues.length > 0) {
            const fmtTime = (sec) => {
              const h = Math.floor(sec / 3600);
              const m = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60);
              const sSec = Math.floor(sec % 60);
              const ms = Math.floor((sec % 1) * 1000);
              return `${String(h).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(sSec).padStart(2, '0')}.${String(ms).padStart(3, '0')}`;
            };
            let vtt = 'WEBVTT\n\n';
            for (const c of cues) {
              const s = typeof c.start === 'number' ? c.start : (c.startTime || 0);
              const e = typeof c.end === 'number' ? c.end : (c.endTime || s + 3);
              const txt = c.text || c.content || (c.segs ? c.segs.map((x) => x.utf8).join('') : '');
              if (txt) {
                vtt += `${fmtTime(s)} --> ${fmtTime(e)}\n${txt}\n\n`;
              }
            }
            await fs.promises.writeFile(outVttPath, vtt, 'utf8');
            return true;
          }
        } catch {}
      }

      // If it's an HLS subtitle playlist (#EXTM3U)
      if (subText.includes('#EXTM3U')) {
        const lines = subText.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        const segmentUrls = [];
        for (const line of lines) {
          if (!line.startsWith('#')) {
            try {
              segmentUrls.push(new URL(line, sub.url).href);
            } catch {
              segmentUrls.push(line);
            }
          }
        }

        if (segmentUrls.length === 0) return false;

        const vttChunks = [];
        for (const segUrl of segmentUrls) {
          if (cancelState?.cancelled) return false;
          try {
            const chunkBuf = await this._fetchBuffer(segUrl, customHeaders, 4, 3, cancelState);
            vttChunks.push(chunkBuf.toString('utf8'));
          } catch (err) {
            logger.debug('Failed to fetch subtitle segment:', err.message);
          }
        }

        if (vttChunks.length === 0) return false;

        // Merge WebVTT chunks into a single valid WebVTT document
        let mergedVtt = 'WEBVTT\n\n';
        for (const chunk of vttChunks) {
          const cleaned = chunk
            .replace(/^\uFEFF?WEBVTT[^\r\n]*\r?\n+/i, '')
            .replace(/^X-TIMESTAMP-MAP[^\r\n]*\r?\n+/im, '')
            .trim();
          if (cleaned) {
            mergedVtt += cleaned + '\n\n';
          }
        }

        await fs.promises.writeFile(outVttPath, mergedVtt, 'utf8');
        return true;
      }

      // Fallback: write buffer directly
      await fs.promises.writeFile(outVttPath, subBuf);
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      logger.warn('Failed to download subtitle track:', sub.label || sub.url, err.message);
      return false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Fast probe of stream URL headers & metadata with automatic redirect following & webpage sniffing
   */
  async probeUrl(rawUrl, maxRedirects = 5) {
    if (!rawUrl || typeof rawUrl !== 'string') {
      throw new Error('Invalid URL provided.');
    }

    const trimmed = rawUrl.trim();
    let isHls = false;
    let isDash = false;
    let contentType = '';
    let contentLength = 0;
    let derivedTitle = 'Stream Video';

    try {
      const parsed = new URL(trimmed);
      const lower = parsed.pathname.toLowerCase();
      if (lower.includes('.m3u8') || lower.includes('/m/')) isHls = true;
      if (lower.includes('.mpd')) isDash = true;

      const segments = parsed.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
      const lastSeg = segments[segments.length - 1] || '';
      if (lastSeg && !lastSeg.includes('master') && !lastSeg.includes('index') && lastSeg.length < 65) {
        const epParam = parsed.searchParams.get('ep');
        const baseName = lastSeg.replace(/\.(m3u8|mpd|mp4|webm|mkv|ts)$/i, '').replace(/-/g, ' ');
        const epFormatted = epParam ? (epParam.length === 1 ? `0${epParam}` : epParam) : null;
        derivedTitle = sanitizeFilename(epFormatted ? `${baseName} - Episode ${epFormatted}` : baseName);
      }
    } catch {
      throw new Error('Malformed stream URL.');
    }

    const enrichHlsProbe = async (baseResult, sniffedExtra = null) => {
      if (!baseResult.isHls) return baseResult;
      try {
        const manifest = await this.resolveHlsManifest(baseResult.url);
        let audioTracks = Array.isArray(manifest.audioTracks) ? [...manifest.audioTracks] : [];

        // If the webpage sniffer discovered a verified alternate Dub/Sub language stream
        if (
          sniffedExtra &&
          sniffedExtra.dubStreamUrl &&
          sniffedExtra.dubStreamUrl !== baseResult.url &&
          sniffedExtra.dubStreamUrl !== sniffedExtra.subStreamUrl
        ) {
          try {
            const dubManifest = await this.resolveHlsManifest(sniffedExtra.dubStreamUrl);
            const dubAudioTrack = dubManifest.audioTracks?.[0] || {
              id: 'dub',
              name: 'English',
              lang: 'en-US',
              url: dubManifest.audioUrl || sniffedExtra.dubStreamUrl,
              streamUrl: sniffedExtra.dubStreamUrl
            };
            dubAudioTrack.name = 'English';
            dubAudioTrack.streamUrl = sniffedExtra.dubStreamUrl;

            const hasEnglishTrack = audioTracks.some((a) => /english|en/i.test(a.name || '') || /en/i.test(a.lang || ''));
            if (!hasEnglishTrack) {
              const subAudioTrack = audioTracks[0] || {
                id: 'sub',
                name: 'Japanese',
                lang: 'ja-JP',
                isDefault: true,
                url: manifest.audioUrl || sniffedExtra.subStreamUrl || baseResult.url,
                streamUrl: sniffedExtra.subStreamUrl || baseResult.url
              };
              subAudioTrack.name = 'Japanese';
              subAudioTrack.streamUrl = sniffedExtra.subStreamUrl || baseResult.url;

              if (audioTracks.length <= 1) {
                audioTracks = [subAudioTrack, dubAudioTrack];
              } else {
                audioTracks.push(dubAudioTrack);
              }
            }
          } catch (err) {
            logger.debug('Dub stream manifest enrichment notice:', err.message);
          }
        }

        // If no explicit audio tracks were enumerated in manifest (e.g. single-stream TS with multiplexed audio)
        if (audioTracks.length === 0) {
          const titleCandidate = baseResult.title || derivedTitle || '';
          const isAnimeCandidate = animeSkipResolver.parseAnimeTitleAndEpisode(titleCandidate) || /(?:\[.*?\]|\b(?:ep(?:isode)?\.?\s*\d+|s\d+\s*e\d+|season\s*\d+|owarimonogatari|monogatari|naruto|piece|bleach|jujutsu|frieren|knight)\b)/i.test(titleCandidate) || (Array.isArray(baseResult.subtitles) && baseResult.subtitles.length > 0);
          const isDub = /dub/i.test(titleCandidate);
          if (isAnimeCandidate) {
            audioTracks.push({
              id: 'single_audio_0',
              name: isDub ? 'English (Dub)' : 'Japanese',
              lang: isDub ? 'eng' : 'jpn',
              isDefault: true,
              url: baseResult.url
            });
          }
        }

        const totalSegs = (manifest.videoPlaylist?.segments?.length || 0) + (manifest.audioPlaylist?.segments?.length || 0);
        const mergedSubtitles = [];
        const seenSubUrls = new Set();
        for (const s of [...(manifest.subtitleTracks || []), ...(baseResult.subtitles || [])]) {
          if (s && s.url && !seenSubUrls.has(s.url)) {
            seenSubUrls.add(s.url);
            mergedSubtitles.push(s);
          }
        }
        return {
          ...baseResult,
          masterUrl: manifest.masterUrl,
          videoUrl: manifest.videoUrl,
          audioUrl: manifest.audioUrl,
          videoVariants: manifest.videoVariants || [],
          audioTracks: audioTracks,
          subStreamUrl: sniffedExtra?.subStreamUrl || null,
          dubStreamUrl: sniffedExtra?.dubStreamUrl || null,
          subtitles: mergedSubtitles,
          durationSec: manifest.durationSec,
          durationFormatted: formatDuration(manifest.durationSec),
          segmentCount: totalSegs,
          sizeFormatted: manifest.durationSec > 0 ? `${formatDuration(manifest.durationSec)} (${totalSegs} segs)` : 'HLS Stream'
        };
      } catch {
        return baseResult;
      }
    };

    const fetchHeaders = (targetUrl, redirectsRemaining) => {
      return new Promise((resolve) => {
        try {
          const parsed = new URL(targetUrl);
          const client = parsed.protocol === 'http:' ? http : https;
          const req = client.request(targetUrl, {
            method: 'GET',
            headers: {
              ...getStreamHeaders(targetUrl),
              'Range': 'bytes=0-1024'
            },
            timeout: 7000
          }, (res) => {
            if (res.statusCode >= 300 && res.statusCode < 400 && res.headers.location && redirectsRemaining > 0) {
              req.destroy();
              const nextUrl = new URL(res.headers.location, targetUrl).href;
              return resolve(fetchHeaders(nextUrl, redirectsRemaining - 1));
            }

            contentType = (res.headers['content-type'] || '').toLowerCase();
            contentLength = parseInt(res.headers['content-length'] || '0', 10);
            const disp = res.headers['content-disposition'] || '';

            if (disp && disp.includes('filename=')) {
              const m = disp.match(/filename\*?=['"]?(?:UTF-\d['"]*)?([^;\r\n"']*)['"]?/i);
              if (m && m[1]) derivedTitle = sanitizeFilename(decodeURIComponent(m[1]));
            }

            if (contentType.includes('mpegurl') || targetUrl.includes('.m3u8') || targetUrl.includes('/m/')) isHls = true;
            if (contentType.includes('dash') || targetUrl.includes('.mpd')) isDash = true;

            const isHtml = contentType.includes('text/html') || contentType.includes('application/xhtml');

            req.destroy();

            if (isHtml && !isHls && !isDash) {
              logger.info(`Webpage detected for ${trimmed}, attempting headless stream sniffing...`);
              extractStreamFromWebpage(trimmed, 12000).then(async (sniffed) => {
                if (sniffed && sniffed.streamUrl) {
                  logger.info(`Successfully auto-extracted stream from webpage: ${sniffed.streamUrl}`);
                  let cleanTitle = sanitizeFilename(sniffed.title || derivedTitle);
                  try {
                    const ep = new URL(trimmed).searchParams.get('ep');
                    if (ep && !/ep(isode)?\s*\d+/i.test(cleanTitle)) {
                      const epFormatted = ep.length === 1 ? `0${ep}` : ep;
                      cleanTitle = sanitizeFilename(`${cleanTitle} - Episode ${epFormatted}`);
                    }
                  } catch {}

                  let sizeFormatted = 'Live Stream';
                  let sizeBytes = 0;
                  if (!sniffed.isHls && !sniffed.isDash && sniffed.streamUrl) {
                    try {
                      const clientMod = sniffed.streamUrl.startsWith('http:') ? http : https;
                      const headLen = await new Promise((r) => {
                        const hreq = clientMod.request(sniffed.streamUrl, {
                          method: 'HEAD',
                          headers: getStreamHeaders(sniffed.streamUrl),
                          timeout: 3000
                        }, (hres) => {
                          const len = parseInt(hres.headers['content-length'] || '0', 10);
                          r(len);
                        });
                        hreq.on('error', () => r(0));
                        hreq.end();
                      });
                      if (headLen > 0) {
                        sizeBytes = headLen;
                        sizeFormatted = formatBytes(headLen);
                      }
                    } catch {}
                  }

                  const extracted = await enrichHlsProbe({
                    url: sniffed.streamUrl,
                    originalUrl: trimmed,
                    title: cleanTitle,
                    isHls: sniffed.isHls,
                    isDash: sniffed.isDash,
                    subtitles: sniffed.subtitles || [],
                    isWebpage: false,
                    contentType: sniffed.isHls ? 'application/vnd.apple.mpegurl' : 'video/mp4',
                    format: sniffed.isHls ? 'HLS (1080p Stream)' : (sniffed.isDash ? 'DASH (mpd)' : 'MP4/Stream'),
                    sizeBytes,
                    sizeFormatted,
                    autoExtracted: true
                  }, sniffed);
                  return resolve(extracted);
                }

                resolve({
                  url: targetUrl,
                  originalUrl: trimmed,
                  title: derivedTitle,
                  isHls: false,
                  isDash: false,
                  isWebpage: true,
                  contentType,
                  format: 'Webpage (HTML)',
                  sizeBytes: 0,
                  sizeFormatted: 'Website Page',
``````
