``````javascript
// src/main/logger.js
// Structured, prefixed logging module for VLCopyCat Electron Main process

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

let cachedLogPath = null;
let logStream = null;

function getLogFilePath() {
  if (!cachedLogPath) {
    if (process.env.COPYCAT_TEST_SUITE === '1' || process.env.NODE_ENV === 'test') {
      cachedLogPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'tests', 'test-runs.log');
      return cachedLogPath;
    }
    try {
      const electron = require('electron');
      const app = electron.app;
      const baseDir = app && typeof app.getPath === 'function' ? app.getPath('userData') : path.join(__dirname, '..', '..');
      cachedLogPath = path.join(baseDir, 'copycat.log');
    } catch {
      cachedLogPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'copycat.log');
    }
  }
  return cachedLogPath;
}

function getLogStream() {
  if (!logStream) {
    try {
      logStream = fs.createWriteStream(getLogFilePath(), { flags: 'a', encoding: 'utf8' });
      logStream.on('error', () => { logStream = null; });
    } catch {}
  }
  return logStream;
}

class Logger {
  constructor(scope = 'Main') {
    this.scope = scope;
    this.prefix = `[VLCopyCat:${scope}]`;
  }

  static getLogFile() {
    return getLogFilePath();
  }

  static writeToFile(level, scope, message, meta) {
    try {
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString();
      const metaStr = meta !== undefined && meta !== null
        ? (typeof meta === 'object' ? ` ${JSON.stringify(meta)}` : ` ${meta}`)
        : '';
      const line = `[${stamp}] [${level.toUpperCase()}] [${scope}] ${message}${metaStr}\n`;
      const stream = getLogStream();
      if (stream && stream.writable) {
        stream.write(line);
      } else {
        fs.appendFile(getLogFilePath(), line, 'utf8', () => {});
      }
    } catch {
      // Safe fallback: never crash on file logging errors
    }
  }

  static formatTime() {
    const d = new Date();
    const h = String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
    const m = String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
    const s = String(d.getSeconds()).padStart(2, '0');
    const ms = String(d.getMilliseconds()).padStart(3, '0');
    return `${h}:${m}:${s}.${ms}`;
  }

  log(level, message, ...args) {
    const time = Logger.formatTime();
    const tag = `${time} ${this.prefix}`;
    const meta = args.length > 0 ? (args.length === 1 ? args[0] : args) : null;

    if (level === 'error') {
      console.error(tag, message, ...args);
      Logger.writeToFile('error', this.scope, message, meta);
    } else if (level === 'warn') {
      console.warn(tag, message, ...args);
      Logger.writeToFile('warn', this.scope, message, meta);
    } else if (level === 'debug') {
      if (process.env.COPYCAT_DEBUG) {
        console.debug(tag, message, ...args);
        Logger.writeToFile('debug', this.scope, message, meta);
      }
    } else {
      console.log(tag, message, ...args);
      if (process.env.COPYCAT_DEBUG) {
        Logger.writeToFile('info', this.scope, message, meta);
      }
    }
  }

  info(msg, ...args) {
    this.log('info', msg, ...args);
  }

  warn(msg, ...args) {
    this.log('warn', msg, ...args);
  }

  error(msg, ...args) {
    this.log('error', msg, ...args);
  }

  debug(msg, ...args) {
    this.log('debug', msg, ...args);
  }

  forScope(childScope) {
    return new Logger(childScope);
  }
}

const defaultLogger = new Logger('Main');

module.exports = {
  Logger,
  logger: defaultLogger,
  createLogger: (scope) => new Logger(scope)
};
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/media-editor.js
// Dedicated Media Tools Service: Video Converter, Subtitle Lookup & Embedder, Container Metadata Editor
// Uses bundled FFmpeg & FFprobe executables for fast, local, offline-first media processing.

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const https = require('https');
const http = require('http');
const zlib = require('zlib');
const { spawn, execFile } = require('child_process');
const mediaResolver = require('./media-resolver');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');

const logger = createLogger('MediaEditor');

function parseTimestamp(timeStr) {
  if (!timeStr) return 0;
  const parts = timeStr.trim().split(':');
  if (parts.length === 3) {
    return (parseFloat(parts[0]) * 3600) + (parseFloat(parts[1]) * 60) + parseFloat(parts[2]);
  }
  if (parts.length === 2) {
    return (parseFloat(parts[0]) * 60) + parseFloat(parts[1]);
  }
  return parseFloat(timeStr) || 0;
}

function formatBytes(bytes) {
  if (!bytes || isNaN(bytes) || bytes <= 0) return '0 B';
  const units = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
  let i = 0;
  let val = bytes;
  while (val >= 1024 && i < units.length - 1) {
    val /= 1024;
    i++;
  }
  return i === 0 ? `${Math.round(val)} B` : `${val.toFixed(1)} ${units[i]}`;
}

function formatDuration(sec) {
  if (!sec || isNaN(sec) || sec <= 0) return '00:00';
  const h = Math.floor(sec / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60);
  const s = Math.floor(sec % 60);
  if (h > 0) {
    return `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  }
  return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

class MediaEditor {
  constructor() {
    this.activeJobs = new Map(); // jobId -> childProcess
  }

  /**
   * Cancel an active conversion or embedding task
   */
  cancelJob(jobId) {
    const child = this.activeJobs.get(jobId);
    if (child) {
      try {
        if (process.platform === 'win32' && child.pid) {
          spawn('taskkill', ['/pid', String(child.pid), '/T', '/F'], { windowsHide: true });
        } else {
          child.kill('SIGKILL');
        }
      } catch {}
      this.activeJobs.delete(jobId);
      logger.info(`Cancelled media editor job [${jobId}]`);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * Read container metadata, stream details and tags using ffprobe
   */
  async probeMetadata(filePath) {
    filePath = String(filePath || '').trim().replace(/^["']+|["']+$/g, '').trim();
    if (!filePath || !fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
      throw new Error(`File does not exist: ${filePath}`);
    }

    const ffprobePath = mediaResolver.FFPROBE_PATH;
    if (!ffprobePath || !fs.existsSync(ffprobePath)) {
      throw new Error('Bundled FFprobe binary could not be found.');
    }

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const args = [
        '-v', 'quiet',
        '-print_format', 'json',
        '-show_format',
        '-show_streams',
        '-show_chapters',
        filePath
      ];

      execFile(ffprobePath, args, { windowsHide: true, maxBuffer: 10 * 1024 * 1024 }, (err, stdout) => {
        if (err) {
          return reject(new Error(`Failed to probe media metadata: ${err.message}`));
        }

        try {
          const data = JSON.parse(stdout || '{}');
          const fmt = data.format || {};
          const streams = data.streams || [];
          const tags = fmt.tags || {};

          const videoStreams = streams.filter((s) => s.codec_type === 'video');
          const audioStreams = streams.filter((s) => s.codec_type === 'audio');
          const subtitleStreams = streams.filter((s) => s.codec_type === 'subtitle');

          const primaryVideo = videoStreams[0] || {};
          const duration = parseFloat(fmt.duration || primaryVideo.duration || '0');
          const sizeBytes = parseInt(fmt.size || '0', 10) || (fs.existsSync(filePath) ? fs.statSync(filePath).size : 0);

          const result = {
            success: true,
            ok: true,
            filePath,
            fileName: path.basename(filePath),
            filename: path.basename(filePath),
            formatName: fmt.format_name || path.extname(filePath).replace('.', '').toLowerCase(),
            format: fmt.format_name || path.extname(filePath).replace('.', '').toLowerCase(),
            durationSec: duration,
            duration: duration,
            durationFormatted: formatDuration(duration),
            sizeBytes,
            size: sizeBytes,
            sizeFormatted: formatBytes(sizeBytes),
            bitRate: parseInt(fmt.bit_rate || '0', 10),
            chaptersCount: (data.chapters || []).length,
            tags: {
              title: tags.title || tags.TITLE || '',
              show: tags.show || tags.SHOW || tags.album || '',
              season: tags.season_number || tags.SEASON_NUMBER || tags.season || '',
              episode: tags.episode_sort || tags.EPISODE_SORT || tags.episode || '',
              artist: tags.artist || tags.ARTIST || tags.author || '',
              year: tags.date || tags.DATE || tags.year || tags.creation_time?.slice(0, 4) || '',
              description: tags.description || tags.comment || tags.COMMENT || ''
            },
            video: {
              codec: primaryVideo.codec_name || 'unknown',
              width: primaryVideo.width || 0,
              height: primaryVideo.height || 0,
              resolution: primaryVideo.width ? `${primaryVideo.width}x${primaryVideo.height}` : 'Unknown',
              fps: (() => {
                if (!primaryVideo.r_frame_rate) return 0;
                const parts = String(primaryVideo.r_frame_rate).split('/').map(Number);
                if (parts.length === 2 && parts[1] > 0) return Math.round(parts[0] / parts[1]);
                return Math.round(parts[0] || 0);
              })()
            },
            videoStream: primaryVideo,
            audioTracks: audioStreams.map((a, i) => ({
              index: a.index,
              trackNumber: i + 1,
              codec: a.codec_name || 'unknown',
              channels: a.channels || 2,
              language: a.tags?.language || a.tags?.lang || 'und',
              title: a.tags?.title || a.tags?.handler_name || `Audio Track ${i + 1}`,
              isDefault: Boolean(a.disposition?.default)
            })),
            audioStreams: audioStreams.map((a, i) => ({
              index: a.index,
              trackNumber: i + 1,
              codec: a.codec_name || 'unknown',
              channels: a.channels || 2,
              language: a.tags?.language || a.tags?.lang || 'und',
              title: a.tags?.title || a.tags?.handler_name || `Audio Track ${i + 1}`,
              isDefault: Boolean(a.disposition?.default)
            })),
            subtitleTracks: subtitleStreams.map((s, i) => ({
              index: s.index,
              trackNumber: i + 1,
              codec: s.codec_name || 'unknown',
              language: s.tags?.language || s.tags?.lang || 'und',
              title: s.tags?.title || s.tags?.handler_name || `Subtitle ${i + 1}`,
              isDefault: Boolean(s.disposition?.default)
            })),
            subtitleStreams: subtitleStreams.map((s, i) => ({
              index: s.index,
              trackNumber: i + 1,
              codec: s.codec_name || 'unknown',
              language: s.tags?.language || s.tags?.lang || 'und',
              title: s.tags?.title || s.tags?.handler_name || `Subtitle ${i + 1}`,
              isDefault: Boolean(s.disposition?.default)
            }))
          };

          resolve(result);
        } catch (parseErr) {
          reject(new Error(`Failed to parse probe JSON output: ${parseErr.message}`));
        }
      });
    });
  }

  /**
   * Fast metadata rewrite using FFmpeg lossless container remux
   */
  async applyMetadata({ inputPath, outputPath, tags, audioTracks = [] }, onProgress = null) {
    if (!inputPath || !fs.existsSync(inputPath)) {
      throw new Error(`Input file does not exist: ${inputPath}`);
    }

    const ffmpegPath = mediaResolver.FFMPEG_PATH;
    if (!ffmpegPath || !fs.existsSync(ffmpegPath)) {
      throw new Error('Bundled FFmpeg executable could not be resolved.');
    }

    const outPath = outputPath || path.join(
      path.dirname(inputPath),
      `${path.basename(inputPath, path.extname(inputPath))}_tagged${path.extname(inputPath)}`
    );

    const args = ['-y', '-i', inputPath, '-map', '0', '-c', 'copy'];

    if (tags) {
      if (tags.title) args.push('-metadata', `title=${tags.title}`);
      if (tags.show) args.push('-metadata', `show=${tags.show}`, '-metadata', `album=${tags.show}`);
      if (tags.season) args.push('-metadata', `season_number=${tags.season}`);
      if (tags.episode) args.push('-metadata', `episode_sort=${tags.episode}`);
      if (tags.artist) args.push('-metadata', `artist=${tags.artist}`);
      if (tags.year) args.push('-metadata', `date=${tags.year}`);
      if (tags.description) args.push('-metadata', `description=${tags.description}`, '-metadata', `comment=${tags.description}`);
    }

    if (Array.isArray(audioTracks)) {
      audioTracks.forEach((tr, i) => {
        if (tr.language) args.push(`-metadata:s:a:${i}`, `language=${tr.language}`);
        if (tr.title) args.push(`-metadata:s:a:${i}`, `title=${tr.title}`);
      });
    }

    if (outPath.toLowerCase().endsWith('.mp4')) {
      args.push('-movflags', '+faststart');
    }

    args.push(outPath);

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const jobId = `meta_${Date.now()}`;
      logger.info(`Applying metadata [${jobId}]: ${inputPath} -> ${outPath}`);

      const child = spawn(ffmpegPath, args, { windowsHide: true, stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'] });
      this.activeJobs.set(jobId, child);

      let errText = '';
      child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { errText += d.toString(); });
      child.on('error', (err) => {
        this.activeJobs.delete(jobId);
        reject(err);
      });

      child.on('close', (code) => {
        this.activeJobs.delete(jobId);
        if (code === 0 && fs.existsSync(outPath)) {
          resolve({ success: true, ok: true, outputPath: outPath });
        } else {
          reject(new Error(errText.trim() || `FFmpeg exited with code ${code}`));
        }
      });
    });
  }

  /**
   * Convert video/audio file to a different format or container
   */
  async convertMedia(options, onProgress = null) {
    const {
      inputPath,
      outputPath,
      targetFormat = 'mp4',
      mode = 'copy', // 'copy' (fast remux) or 'transcode' (re-encode)
      crf = 20,
      preset = 'medium',
      audioOnly = false
    } = options;

    if (!inputPath || !fs.existsSync(inputPath)) {
      throw new Error(`Input file does not exist: ${inputPath}`);
    }

    const ffmpegPath = mediaResolver.FFMPEG_PATH;
    if (!ffmpegPath || !fs.existsSync(ffmpegPath)) {
      throw new Error('Bundled FFmpeg executable could not be resolved.');
    }

    const ext = audioOnly ? (targetFormat === 'mp3' ? '.mp3' : '.aac') : `.${targetFormat.toLowerCase().replace('.', '')}`;
    const defaultOut = path.join(
      path.dirname(inputPath),
      `${path.basename(inputPath, path.extname(inputPath))}_converted${ext}`
    );
    const finalOut = outputPath || defaultOut;

    const args = ['-y', '-i', inputPath];

    if (audioOnly) {
      args.push('-vn');
      if (ext === '.mp3') {
        args.push('-c:a', 'libmp3lame', '-b:a', '256k');
      } else {
        args.push('-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '256k');
      }
    } else if (mode === 'copy') {
      args.push('-c', 'copy');
      if (ext === '.mp4') {
        args.push('-bsf:a', 'aac_adtstoasc', '-movflags', '+faststart');
      }
    } else {
      // Re-encode video
      if (ext === '.webm') {
        args.push('-c:v', 'libvpx-vp9', '-crf', String(crf), '-b:v', '0', '-c:a', 'libopus', '-b:a', '128k');
      } else {
        args.push('-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', preset, '-crf', String(crf), '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '192k');
        if (ext === '.mp4') {
          args.push('-movflags', '+faststart');
        }
      }
    }

    args.push(finalOut);

    const jobId = `conv_${Date.now()}`;
    logger.info(`Starting media conversion [${jobId}]: ${inputPath} -> ${finalOut} (${mode})`);

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const child = spawn(ffmpegPath, args, { windowsHide: true, stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'] });
      this.activeJobs.set(jobId, child);

      let totalDuration = 0;
      let lastReport = 0;
      let lastStderr = '';

      child.stderr.on('data', (chunk) => {
        const text = chunk.toString();
        lastStderr = (lastStderr + text).slice(-3000);

        if (!totalDuration) {
          const durMatch = text.match(/Duration:\s*(\d\d:\d\d:\d\d\.\d\d)/);
          if (durMatch && durMatch[1]) {
            totalDuration = parseTimestamp(durMatch[1]);
          }
        }

        const timeMatch = text.match(/time=(\d\d:\d\d:\d\d\.\d\d)/);
        if (timeMatch && timeMatch[1]) {
          const currentTime = parseTimestamp(timeMatch[1]);
          const percent = totalDuration > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((currentTime / totalDuration) * 100)) : 0;
          const speedMatch = text.match(/speed=\s*([\d\.]+)x/);
          const fpsMatch = text.match(/fps=\s*([\d\.]+)/);

          const now = Date.now();
          if (now - lastReport > 250 && typeof onProgress === 'function') {
            lastReport = now;
            onProgress({
              jobId,
              percent,
              currentTimeFormatted: formatDuration(currentTime),
              totalDurationFormatted: formatDuration(totalDuration),
              speed: speedMatch ? `${speedMatch[1]}x` : '',
              fps: fpsMatch ? fpsMatch[1] : ''
            });
          }
        }
      });

      child.on('error', (err) => {
        this.activeJobs.delete(jobId);
        reject(err);
      });

      child.on('close', (code) => {
        this.activeJobs.delete(jobId);
        if (code === 0 && fs.existsSync(finalOut)) {
          if (typeof onProgress === 'function') {
            onProgress({ jobId, percent: 100, status: 'completed' });
          }
          resolve({ success: true, ok: true, jobId, outputPath: finalOut });
        } else {
          reject(new Error(lastStderr.trim() || `Conversion process exited with code ${code}`));
        }
      });
    });
  }

  _detectSubtitleLanguage(filename) {
    const f = String(filename || '').toLowerCase();
    if (/(?:^|[._ -])(hu|hun|magyar)(?:[._ -]|$)/i.test(f)) return 'hun';
    if (/(?:^|[._ -])(ja|jp|jpn|japanese)(?:[._ -]|$)/i.test(f)) return 'jpn';
    if (/(?:^|[._ -])(de|ger|deutsch|german)(?:[._ -]|$)/i.test(f)) return 'ger';
    if (/(?:^|[._ -])(en|eng|english)(?:[._ -]|$)/i.test(f)) return 'eng';
    if (/(?:^|[._ -])(es|spa|spanish|espanol)(?:[._ -]|$)/i.test(f)) return 'spa';
    if (/(?:^|[._ -])(fr|fre|fra|french|francais)(?:[._ -]|$)/i.test(f)) return 'fre';
    if (/(?:^|[._ -])(it|ita|italian|italiano)(?:[._ -]|$)/i.test(f)) return 'ita';
    return 'eng';
  }

  /**
   * Search for companion subtitles locally and via online OpenSubtitles REST API
   */
  async searchSubtitles({ query, lang = 'eng', filePath = null }) {
    const localResults = [];
    const onlineResults = [];

    // 1. Search local folder companion files if filePath is provided
    if (filePath && fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
      try {
        const dir = path.dirname(filePath);
        const baseName = path.basename(filePath, path.extname(filePath)).toLowerCase();
        const files = fs.readdirSync(dir);

        for (const f of files) {
          const ext = path.extname(f).toLowerCase();
          if (['.srt', '.vtt', '.ass', '.sub'].includes(ext)) {
            const fLower = f.toLowerCase();
            if (fLower.includes(baseName) || baseName.includes(path.basename(f, ext).toLowerCase())) {
              localResults.push({
                id: `local_${localResults.length}`,
                source: 'local',
                title: f,
                fileName: f,
                filePath: path.join(dir, f),
                localPath: path.join(dir, f),
                lang: this._detectSubtitleLanguage(f),
                format: ext.replace('.', '').toUpperCase(),
                downloadUrl: null
              });
            }
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.debug('Local subtitle search notice:', err.message);
      }
    }

    // 2. Search OpenSubtitles REST API
    const searchQuery = query || (filePath ? path.basename(filePath, path.extname(filePath)).replace(/[-_.]+/g, ' ') : '');
    if (searchQuery && searchQuery.trim().length > 1) {
      try {
        const subLang = lang === 'hu' ? 'hun' : (lang === 'de' ? 'ger' : (lang === 'ja' ? 'jpn' : lang));
        const qTrim = searchQuery.trim();
        const queriesToRun = [qTrim];

        if (filePath) {
          const rawBase = path.basename(filePath, path.extname(filePath)).replace(/[-_.]+/g, ' ').trim();
          if (rawBase && rawBase.toLowerCase() !== qTrim.toLowerCase()) {
            queriesToRun.unshift(rawBase);
          }
        }

        const seenIds = new Set();
        for (const q of queriesToRun) {
          const osList = await this._queryOpenSubtitles(q, subLang);
          for (const item of osList) {
            if (!seenIds.has(item.id)) {
              seenIds.add(item.id);
              onlineResults.push(item);
            }
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.debug('Online subtitle search notice:', err.message);
      }
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      results: [...localResults, ...onlineResults],
      localResults,
      onlineResults
    };
  }

  _queryOpenSubtitles(query, lang = 'eng') {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const url = `https://rest.opensubtitles.org/search/query-${encodeURIComponent(query)}/sublanguageid-${lang}`;
      const fetchJson = (targetUrl, redirectsRemaining = 4) => {
        const req = https.get(targetUrl, {
          headers: { 'User-Agent': 'TemporaryUserAgent' },
          timeout: 6000
        }, (res) => {
          if (res.statusCode >= 300 && res.statusCode < 400 && res.headers.location && redirectsRemaining > 0) {
            req.destroy();
            return fetchJson(res.headers.location, redirectsRemaining - 1);
          }
          let data = '';
          res.on('data', chunk => data += chunk);
          res.on('end', () => {
            try {
              const list = JSON.parse(data || '[]');
              if (Array.isArray(list)) {
                const mapped = list.slice(0, 15).map((item, idx) => ({
                  id: item.IDSubtitleFile || `os_${idx}`,
                  source: 'opensubtitles',
                  title: item.SubFileName || item.MovieName || `Subtitle ${idx + 1}`,
                  fileName: item.SubFileName || `sub_${idx + 1}.srt`,
                  lang: item.SubLanguageID || lang,
                  format: item.SubFormat ? item.SubFormat.toUpperCase() : 'SRT',
                  downloadUrl: item.SubDownloadLink || item.ZipDownloadLink,
                  rating: item.SubRating || '0'
                }));
                return resolve(mapped);
              }
            } catch {}
            resolve([]);
          });
        });
        req.on('error', () => resolve([]));
        req.on('timeout', () => { req.destroy(); resolve([]); });
      };

      fetchJson(url);
    });
  }

  /**
   * Download online subtitle file and gunzip if needed
   */
  async downloadSubtitle(downloadUrl, targetPath) {
    if (!downloadUrl) throw new Error('Download URL required.');
    const outPath = targetPath || path.join(
      process.env.TEMP || process.env.TMP || 'C:\\Windows\\Temp',
      `copycat_sub_${Date.now()}.srt`
    );

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const req = https.get(downloadUrl, { headers: { 'User-Agent': 'TemporaryUserAgent' }, timeout: 10000 }, (res) => {
        if (res.statusCode >= 300 && res.statusCode < 400 && res.headers.location) {
          req.destroy();
          return this.downloadSubtitle(res.headers.location, outPath).then(resolve).catch(reject);
        }

        const chunks = [];
        res.on('data', c => chunks.push(c));
        res.on('end', () => {
          const buf = Buffer.concat(chunks);
          // Check if gzip compressed (magic bytes 0x1F, 0x8B)
          if (buf.length > 2 && buf[0] === 0x1f && buf[1] === 0x8b) {
            zlib.gunzip(buf, (err, unzipped) => {
              if (err) return reject(err);
              fs.writeFileSync(outPath, unzipped);
              resolve({ success: true, ok: true, targetPath: outPath, outPath });
            });
          } else {
            fs.writeFileSync(outPath, buf);
            resolve({ success: true, ok: true, targetPath: outPath, outPath });
          }
        });
      });
      req.on('error', reject);
    });
  }

  /**
   * Embed subtitle file into video container (Soft subremux or Hardsub burn-in)
   */
  async embedSubtitles(options, onProgress = null) {
    const {
      inputPath,
      outputPath,
      subPath,
      lang = 'eng',
      title = 'Subtitles',
      isDefault = true,
      mode = 'soft' // 'soft' (lossless container stream) or 'burn' (hardsub)
    } = options;

    if (!inputPath || !fs.existsSync(inputPath)) {
      throw new Error(`Input video does not exist: ${inputPath}`);
    }
    if (!subPath || !fs.existsSync(subPath)) {
      throw new Error(`Subtitle file does not exist: ${subPath}`);
    }

    const ffmpegPath = mediaResolver.FFMPEG_PATH;
    if (!ffmpegPath || !fs.existsSync(ffmpegPath)) {
      throw new Error('Bundled FFmpeg executable could not be resolved.');
    }

    const ext = path.extname(inputPath).toLowerCase();
    const defaultOut = path.join(
      path.dirname(inputPath),
      `${path.basename(inputPath, ext)}_subbed${ext}`
    );
    const finalOut = outputPath || defaultOut;

    const args = ['-y', '-i', inputPath];

    if (mode === 'burn') {
      // Hardsub filter
      const escapedSub = subPath.replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/:/g, '\\:');
      args.push('-vf', `subtitles='${escapedSub}'`, '-c:a', 'copy', finalOut);
    } else {
      // Soft subtitle remux
      args.push('-i', subPath, '-map', '0', '-map', '1:0', '-c', 'copy');
      if (ext === '.mp4' || ext === '.m4v') {
        args.push('-c:s', 'mov_text');
      } else {
        args.push('-c:s', 'subrip');
      }
      args.push(
        '-metadata:s:s:0', `language=${lang}`,
        '-metadata:s:s:0', `title=${title}`
      );
      if (isDefault) {
        args.push('-disposition:s:s:0', 'default');
      }
      if (ext === '.mp4') {
        args.push('-movflags', '+faststart');
      }
      args.push(finalOut);
    }

    const jobId = `embed_${Date.now()}`;
    logger.info(`Embedding subtitles [${jobId}]: ${subPath} into ${inputPath} -> ${finalOut} (${mode})`);

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const child = spawn(ffmpegPath, args, { windowsHide: true, stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'] });
      this.activeJobs.set(jobId, child);

      let lastStderr = '';
      child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { lastStderr += d.toString(); });
      child.on('error', (err) => {
        this.activeJobs.delete(jobId);
        reject(err);
      });

      child.on('close', (code) => {
        this.activeJobs.delete(jobId);
        if (code === 0 && fs.existsSync(finalOut)) {
          resolve({ success: true, ok: true, jobId, outputPath: finalOut });
        } else {
          reject(new Error(lastStderr.trim() || `FFmpeg subtitle embed failed with code ${code}`));
        }
      });
    });
  }
}

const mediaEditorInstance = new MediaEditor();

module.exports = {
  MediaEditor,
  mediaEditor: mediaEditorInstance
};
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/media-resolver.js
// Media Detection, Header-Byte Sniffing, Codec Probing and File Indexing for VLCopyCat

const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const { pathToFileURL } = require('url');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');

const logger = createLogger('MediaResolver');

// Recognized media file extensions
const MEDIA_EXTENSIONS = new Set([
  '.mp4', '.mkv', '.webm', '.mov', '.avi', '.m4v', '.ogv',
  '.ts', '.m2ts', '.flv', '.wmv', '.mpg', '.mpeg', '.vob', '.3gp',
  '.mp3', '.flac', '.wav', '.ogg', '.m4a', '.aac', '.opus'
]);

// Dedicated audio formats
const AUDIO_EXTENSIONS = new Set([
  '.mp3', '.flac', '.wav', '.ogg', '.m4a', '.aac', '.opus'
]);

// Supported subtitle file formats
const SUB_EXTENSIONS = ['.srt', '.vtt'];

// Known ISO Base Media File Format box / atom types
const ISO_BMFF_ATOMS = new Set([
  'ftyp', 'moov', 'mdat', 'free', 'wide', 'skip', 'pnot', 'styp'
]);

const toolCache = new Map();

/**
 * Locate FFmpeg & FFprobe binaries in package, local bin or system PATH
 * Uses in-memory PATH scanning to completely eliminate synchronous child-process stalls.
 * @param {string} toolName 'ffmpeg' or 'ffprobe'
 * @returns {string|null} Resolved executable path or null
 */
function resolveMediaTool(toolName) {
  if (toolCache.has(toolName)) {
    return toolCache.get(toolName);
  }

  const exe = process.platform === 'win32' ? `${toolName}.exe` : toolName;
  const candidates = [
    path.join(process.resourcesPath || '', 'bin', exe),
    path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'bin', exe),
    path.join(process.resourcesPath || '', exe)
  ];

  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (c && fs.existsSync(c)) {
      toolCache.set(toolName, c);
      return c;
    }
  }

  // Fast in-memory PATH lookup without spawning child processes (avoids startup event-loop stalls)
  const pathDirs = (process.env.PATH || '').split(path.delimiter);
  for (const dir of pathDirs) {
    if (!dir) continue;
    try {
      const candidatePath = path.join(dir, exe);
      if (fs.existsSync(candidatePath)) {
        toolCache.set(toolName, candidatePath);
        return candidatePath;
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  toolCache.set(toolName, null);
  return null;
}

/**
 * Parse an HTTP Range header (RFC 7233) against total file size
 * @param {string|null} rangeHeader
 * @param {number} totalSize
 * @returns {{ isRange: boolean, start: number, end: number, chunkSize: number, total: number }}
 */
function parseByteRange(rangeHeader, totalSize) {
  const total = Math.max(0, totalSize || 0);
  let start = 0;
  let end = Math.max(0, total - 1);
  let isRange = false;

  if (rangeHeader && typeof rangeHeader === 'string') {
    const match = /^bytes=(\d*)-(\d*)$/i.exec(rangeHeader.trim());
    if (match) {
      isRange = true;
      const s = match[1] ? parseInt(match[1], 10) : NaN;
      const e = match[2] ? parseInt(match[2], 10) : NaN;
      if (!Number.isNaN(s) && !Number.isNaN(e)) {
        start = Math.max(0, Math.min(s, total - 1));
        end = Math.max(start, Math.min(e, total - 1));
      } else if (!Number.isNaN(s)) {
        start = Math.max(0, Math.min(s, total - 1));
        end = Math.max(start, total - 1);
      } else if (!Number.isNaN(e)) {
        start = Math.max(0, total - e);
        end = Math.max(start, total - 1);
      }
    }
  }

  const chunkSize = total === 0 ? 0 : Math.max(0, end - start + 1);
  return { isRange, start, end, chunkSize, total };
}

const FFMPEG_PATH = resolveMediaTool('ffmpeg');
const FFPROBE_PATH = resolveMediaTool('ffprobe');

function hasFfmpeg() {
  return Boolean(FFMPEG_PATH);
}

function hasFfprobe() {
  return Boolean(FFPROBE_PATH);
}

/**
 * Asynchronously checks if media file requires FFmpeg live remux / transcoding
 * @param {string} filePath Absolute path to media file
 * @param {string} ext Lowercase extension (.mp4, .mkv, etc.)
 * @returns {Promise<boolean>} True if FFmpeg transcoding/remux stream is required
 */
async function detectNeedsStreamAsync(filePath, ext) {
  if (!FFMPEG_PATH) return false;
  if (AUDIO_EXTENSIONS.has(ext)) return false;

  if (['.ts', '.m2ts', '.avi', '.flv', '.wmv', '.mpg', '.mpeg', '.vob'].includes(ext)) {
    return true;
  }

  let fh = null;
  try {
    fh = await fs.promises.open(filePath, 'r');
    const buf = Buffer.alloc(196);
    const { bytesRead } = await fh.read(buf, 0, 196, 0);
    if (bytesRead < 8) return false;

    if (buf[0] === 0x47 && (buf[188] === 0x47 || buf[192] === 0x47)) {
      logger.info(`Detected MPEG-TS stream disguised as "${path.extname(filePath)}" via byte sniffer`);
      return true;
    }

    const boxType = buf.toString('ascii', 4, 8);
    if (ISO_BMFF_ATOMS.has(boxType)) {
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug(`detectNeedsStreamAsync error on "${filePath}":`, err.message);
    return false;
  } finally {
    if (fh) {
      try { await fh.close(); } catch {}
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Fast 0.05ms header byte sniffer to catch MPEG-TS disguised as .mp4 and non-Chromium containers
 * @param {string} filePath Absolute path to media file
 * @param {string} ext Lowercase extension (.mp4, .mkv, etc.)
 * @returns {boolean} True if FFmpeg transcoding/remux stream is required
 */
function detectNeedsStream(filePath, ext) {
  if (!FFMPEG_PATH) return false;
  if (AUDIO_EXTENSIONS.has(ext)) return false;

  // Formats known to require remuxing/transcoding for Chromium video tag
  if (['.ts', '.m2ts', '.avi', '.flv', '.wmv', '.mpg', '.mpeg', '.vob'].includes(ext)) {
    return true;
  }

  let fd = null;
  try {
    fd = fs.openSync(filePath, 'r');
    const buf = Buffer.alloc(196);
    const bytesRead = fs.readSync(fd, buf, 0, 196, 0);
    if (bytesRead < 12) return false;

    // MPEG-TS sync byte 0x47 at offset 0 and offset 188 (common in HLS downloads saved as .mp4)
    if (buf[0] === 0x47 && (bytesRead < 189 || buf[188] === 0x47)) {
      return true;
    }

    // AVI ('RIFF'), FLV ('FLV')
    const head3 = buf.subarray(0, 3).toString('ascii');
    const head4 = buf.subarray(0, 4).toString('ascii');
    if (head4 === 'RIFF' || head3 === 'FLV') {
      return true;
    }

    // Verify ISO Base Media File Format atom for .mp4 / .m4v / .mov
    if (['.mp4', '.m4v', '.mov', '.3gp'].includes(ext)) {
      const atom = buf.subarray(4, 8).toString('ascii');
      if (!ISO_BMFF_ATOMS.has(atom)) {
        return true;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug(`detectNeedsStream error on "${filePath}":`, err.message);
    return false;
  } finally {
    if (fd !== null) {
      try {
        fs.closeSync(fd);
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * Heuristic subtitle language detector from WebVTT/SRT text samples
 * Supports Asian/Middle Eastern scripts and common Latin-script languages
 */
function detectSubtitleLanguage(text) {
  if (!text || typeof text !== 'string') return null;
  const clean = text
    .replace(/^WEBVTT[^\r\n]*\r?\n+/i, '')
    .replace(/\d\d:\d\d(?::\d\d)?(?:\.\d+)?\s*-->\s*\d\d:\d\d(?::\d\d)?(?:\.\d+)?/g, '')
    .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '')
    .slice(0, 15000);

  if (/[\u0e00-\u0e7f]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Thai', lang: 'tha' };
  if (/[\u3040-\u30ff]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Japanese', lang: 'jpn' };
  if (/[\uac00-\ud7af]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Korean', lang: 'kor' };
  if (/[\u0400-\u04ff]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Russian', lang: 'rus' };
  if (/[\u0600-\u06ff]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Arabic', lang: 'ara' };
  if (/[\u4e00-\u9fa5]/.test(clean)) return { name: 'Chinese', lang: 'chi' };

  const idMatches = (clean.match(/\b(yang|dan|di|ini|itu|tidak|ada|dari|untuk|kamu|aku|dia|kita|mereka|bisa|sudah|dengan|akan|apa|pada|saya|kau|kami|jangan|bukan)\b/gi) || []).length;
  const deMatches = (clean.match(/\b(und|der|die|das|ich|nicht|sie|ein|eine|ist|wir|haben|mit|dem|den|aber|wie|dass|sich|oder|wenn)\b/gi) || []).length;
  const esMatches = (clean.match(/\b(que|de|no|el|la|es|un|una|los|las|por|con|para|yo|pero|está|como|más|este|esta)\b/gi) || []).length;
  const frMatches = (clean.match(/\b(que|de|ne|pas|le|la|les|un|une|est|et|des|en|du|pour|avec|dans|mais|elle|nous)\b/gi) || []).length;
  const itMatches = (clean.match(/\b(che|di|non|il|la|un|una|per|con|sono|ma|questo|questa|come|cosa|tutto|anche)\b/gi) || []).length;
  const ptMatches = (clean.match(/\b(que|de|não|o|a|os|as|um|uma|com|para|por|em|este|esta|você|isso|mas)\b/gi) || []).length;
  const enMatches = (clean.match(/\b(the|and|you|that|was|for|are|with|his|they|this|have|from|one|had|not|but|what|all|were|when|your|can|there|out|about)\b/gi) || []).length;

  const scores = [
    { name: 'English', lang: 'eng', score: enMatches },
    { name: 'Indonesian', lang: 'ind', score: idMatches },
    { name: 'German', lang: 'ger', score: deMatches },
    { name: 'Spanish', lang: 'spa', score: esMatches },
    { name: 'French', lang: 'fra', score: frMatches },
    { name: 'Italian', lang: 'ita', score: itMatches },
    { name: 'Portuguese', lang: 'por', score: ptMatches }
  ].sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);

  if (scores[0] && scores[0].score >= 2) {
    return { name: scores[0].name, lang: scores[0].lang };
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Deep media probe using ffprobe
 * Extracts video/audio codec, container duration, dimensions, and chapter marks
 * @param {string} filePath
 * @returns {Promise<Object>}
 */
function probeMediaFile(filePath) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (!FFPROBE_PATH || !filePath) {
      return resolve({ ok: false, duration: 0, videoCodec: null, audioCodec: null, chapters: [] });
    }

    fs.promises.access(filePath, fs.constants.F_OK).then(() => {

    const args = [
      '-v', 'error',
      '-show_entries', 'format=format_name,duration',
      '-show_entries', 'stream=index,codec_type,codec_name,width,height,tags',
      '-show_chapters',
      '-of', 'json',
      filePath
    ];

    const proc = spawn(FFPROBE_PATH, args, { windowsHide: true });
    let out = '';

    proc.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => {
      out += chunk.toString();
    });

    proc.on('error', (err) => {
      logger.warn(`ffprobe execution error for "${filePath}":`, err.message);
      resolve({ ok: false, duration: 0, videoCodec: null, audioCodec: null, chapters: [] });
    });

    proc.on('close', async () => {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(out || '{}');
        const streams = parsed.streams || [];
        const vStream = streams.find((s) => s.codec_type === 'video');
        const aStream = streams.find((s) => s.codec_type === 'audio');
        const aStreams = streams.filter((s) => s.codec_type === 'audio');
        const animeResolver = require('./anime-skip-resolver');
        const animeParsed = animeResolver.parseAnimeTitleAndEpisode(path.basename(filePath));
        const isAnime = Boolean(animeParsed);
        const isDub = isAnime && /\b(dub|dubbed|english dub)\b/i.test(path.basename(filePath));

        const audioTracks = aStreams.map((s, idx) => {
          let name = (s.tags && (s.tags.title || s.tags.TITLE || s.tags.handler_name)) || '';
          let lang = (s.tags && (s.tags.language || s.tags.LANGUAGE)) || '';
          if (!name || name === 'SoundHandler' || /^Audio Track/i.test(name) || /^Default/i.test(name)) {
            if (/jpn|ja/i.test(lang)) {
              name = 'Japanese (Original)';
            } else if (/eng|en/i.test(lang)) {
              name = 'English (Dub)';
            } else if (isAnime && aStreams.length === 2) {
              name = idx === 0 ? (isDub ? 'English (Dub)' : 'Japanese (Original)') : (isDub ? 'Japanese (Original)' : 'English (Dub)');
              if (!lang) lang = idx === 0 ? (isDub ? 'en' : 'ja') : (isDub ? 'ja' : 'en');
            } else if (isAnime && aStreams.length === 1) {
              name = isDub ? 'English (Dub)' : 'Japanese (Original)';
              if (!lang) lang = isDub ? 'en' : 'ja';
            } else {
              name = `Audio Track ${idx + 1}${lang ? ` [${lang.toUpperCase()}]` : ''}`;
            }
          }
          return {
            index: idx,
            streamIndex: s.index,
            codec: s.codec_name,
            name,
            lang
          };
        });
        const duration = Number(parsed.format?.duration) || 0;
        const rawChapters = Array.isArray(parsed.chapters) ? parsed.chapters : [];
        let chapters = rawChapters.map((c, idx) => ({
          index: idx,
          start: Math.max(0, Number(c.start_time) || (Number(c.start) / 1000) || 0),
          end: Math.max(0, Number(c.end_time) || (Number(c.end) / 1000) || 0),
          title: (c.tags && (c.tags.title || c.tags.TITLE || c.tags.Title)) || ''
        })).filter((c) => c.end > c.start);

        const subStreams = streams.filter((s) => s.codec_type === 'subtitle');
        const subtitleTracks = subStreams.map((s, idx) => {
          let name = (s.tags && (s.tags.title || s.tags.TITLE || s.tags.handler_name)) || '';
          const lang = (s.tags && (s.tags.language || s.tags.LANGUAGE)) || '';
          if (!name || /^(SubtitleHandler|Timed Text Media Handler|Text|Subtitles|handler)$/i.test(name.trim())) {
            if (/jpn|ja/i.test(lang)) name = 'Japanese';
            else if (/eng|en/i.test(lang)) name = 'English';
            else if (/ger|deu|de/i.test(lang)) name = 'German';
            else if (/spa|es/i.test(lang)) name = 'Spanish';
            else if (/fre|fra|fr/i.test(lang)) name = 'French';
            else if (/ita|it/i.test(lang)) name = 'Italian';
            else if (/rus|ru/i.test(lang)) name = 'Russian';
            else if (/chi|zho|zh/i.test(lang)) name = 'Chinese';
            else if (/kor|ko/i.test(lang)) name = 'Korean';
            else if (/ind|in|id/i.test(lang)) name = 'Indonesian';
            else if (/tha|th/i.test(lang)) name = 'Thai';
            else if (/ara|ar/i.test(lang)) name = 'Arabic';
            else if (/por|pt/i.test(lang)) name = 'Portuguese';
            else if (/vie|vi/i.test(lang)) name = 'Vietnamese';
            else name = `Subtitle Track ${idx + 1}${lang ? ` [${lang}]` : ''}`;
          }
          return {
            index: idx,
            streamIndex: s.index,
            codec: s.codec_name,
            name,
            lang
          };
        });

        // If any subtitle track still has a generic name and unknown language, sample it to detect language
        for (const st of subtitleTracks) {
          if ((!st.name || st.name.startsWith('Subtitle Track')) && (!st.lang || st.lang === 'und')) {
            try {
              const sample = await extractEmbeddedSubtitleVtt(filePath, st.index);
              if (sample && sample.ok && sample.content) {
                const detected = detectSubtitleLanguage(sample.content);
                if (detected) {
                  st.name = detected.name;
                  st.lang = detected.lang;
                }
              }
            } catch {}
          }
        }

        resolve({
          ok: true,
          formatName: parsed.format?.format_name || '',
          duration,
          videoCodec: vStream?.codec_name || null,
          audioCodec: aStream?.codec_name || null,
          width: vStream?.width || 0,
          height: vStream?.height || 0,
          audioTracks,
          subtitleTracks,
          chapters
        });
      } catch (parseErr) {
        logger.warn(`ffprobe output JSON parse error:`, parseErr.message);
        resolve({ ok: false, duration: 0, videoCodec: null, audioCodec: null, chapters: [] });
      }
    });
    }).catch(() => {
      resolve({ ok: false, duration: 0, videoCodec: null, audioCodec: null, chapters: [] });
    });
  });
}

/**
 * Natural alphabetical + numeric sorting comparison
 */
function naturalCompare(a, b) {
  return a.localeCompare(b, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
}

/**
 * Automatically locate matching subtitle file in directory
 * @param {string} mediaFilePath
 * @param {Array|null} directoryEntries
 * @returns {string|null}
 */
function findAutoSubtitle(mediaFilePath, directoryEntries = null) {
  try {
    const dir = path.dirname(mediaFilePath);
    const base = path.basename(mediaFilePath, path.extname(mediaFilePath));

    for (const ext of SUB_EXTENSIONS) {
      const candidate = path.join(dir, base + ext);
      if (fs.existsSync(candidate)) {
        return candidate;
      }
    }

    const entries = Array.isArray(directoryEntries) ? directoryEntries : fs.readdirSync(dir);
    const baseLower = base.toLowerCase() + '.';
    const match = entries.find((f) => {
      const name = typeof f === 'string' ? f : f?.name;
      if (!name) return false;
      const lower = name.toLowerCase();
      return (
        lower.startsWith(baseLower) &&
        SUB_EXTENSIONS.some((ext) => lower.endsWith(ext))
      );
    });

    if (match) {
      const matchName = typeof match === 'string' ? match : match.name;
      return path.join(dir, matchName);
    }
    return null;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug(`findAutoSubtitle error:`, err.message);
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Generate privileged media:// custom protocol URL
 * Uses canonical Chromium URL parser for zero-loss encoding of +, %, #, spaces
 * @param {string} filePath
 * @returns {string}
 */
function toMediaUrl(filePath) {
  if (!filePath) return '';
  const resolved = path.resolve(filePath);
  return pathToFileURL(resolved).href.replace(/^file:\/\//i, 'media://');
}

/**
 * Construct fully resolved media item descriptor
 * @param {string} filePath
 * @param {Array|null} directoryEntries
 * @returns {Object}
 */
function buildMediaItem(filePath, directoryEntries = null) {
  const resolved = path.resolve(filePath);
  const ext = path.extname(resolved).toLowerCase();
  const isAudio = AUDIO_EXTENSIONS.has(ext);
  const folderPath = path.dirname(resolved);
  const autoSubPath = findAutoSubtitle(resolved, directoryEntries);
  const needsStream = detectNeedsStream(resolved, ext);

  return {
    filePath: resolved,
    fileUrl: toMediaUrl(resolved),
    rawFileUrl: pathToFileURL(resolved).href,
    name: path.basename(resolved),
    title: path.basename(resolved, path.extname(resolved)),
    ext,
    isAudio,
    needsStream,
    folderPath,
    folderName: path.basename(folderPath) || folderPath,
    autoSubPath
  };
}

/**
 * Scan directory recursively for media items and subdirectories
 * @param {string} folderPath
 * @returns {Object}
 */
function scanMediaFolder(folderPath) {
  try {
    const resolvedDir = path.resolve(folderPath);
    const entries = fs.readdirSync(resolvedDir, { withFileTypes: true });

    const subfolders = entries
      .filter((d) => d.isDirectory() && !d.name.startsWith('.') && !d.name.startsWith('$'))
      .map((d) => d.name)
      .sort(naturalCompare)
      .map((name) => ({
        name,
        folderPath: path.join(resolvedDir, name)
      }));

    const files = entries
      .filter((d) => d.isFile() && MEDIA_EXTENSIONS.has(path.extname(d.name).toLowerCase()))
      .map((d) => d.name)
      .sort(naturalCompare)
      .map((name) => buildMediaItem(path.join(resolvedDir, name), entries));

    const parentDir = path.dirname(resolvedDir);

    return {
      folderPath: resolvedDir,
      parentPath: parentDir !== resolvedDir ? parentDir : null,
      folderName: path.basename(resolvedDir) || resolvedDir,
      subfolders,
      items: files
    };
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn(`Failed to scan media folder "${folderPath}":`, err.message);
    return {
      folderPath,
      parentPath: null,
      folderName: path.basename(folderPath || ''),
      subfolders: [],
      items: [],
      error: err.message
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Extract media item and sibling folder from command line arguments
 * @param {Array} argv
 * @returns {Object|null}
 */
function resolveCliMediaFile(argv) {
  if (!Array.isArray(argv)) return null;
  for (let i = 1; i < argv.length; i++) {
    const raw = argv[i];
    if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'string') continue;
    const clean = raw.replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '').trim();
    if (!clean || clean.startsWith('-') || clean === '.') continue;
    const resolved = path.resolve(clean);
    const ext = path.extname(clean).toLowerCase() || path.extname(resolved).toLowerCase();
    if (MEDIA_EXTENSIONS.has(ext) && (fs.existsSync(clean) || fs.existsSync(resolved))) {
      const canonicalPath = fs.existsSync(resolved) ? resolved : clean;
      const item = buildMediaItem(canonicalPath);
      const sibling = scanMediaFolder(item.folderPath);
      return { item, folder: sibling };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Extract embedded subtitle stream from a media file directly to WebVTT string
 * Includes safety timeout to prevent hanging the media probe chain on corrupt files.
 */
function extractEmbeddedSubtitleVtt(filePath, trackIndex = 0, timeoutMs = 8000) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (!filePath || !hasFfmpeg()) {
      return resolve({ ok: false, content: null });
    }

    fs.promises.access(filePath, fs.constants.F_OK).then(() => {
      let finished = false;
      let proc = null;
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        if (!finished) {
          finished = true;
          try { if (proc) proc.kill(); } catch {}
          logger.warn(`Embedded subtitle extraction timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms: ${filePath}`);
          resolve({ ok: false, content: null });
        }
      }, timeoutMs);

      proc = spawn(FFMPEG_PATH, [
        '-nostdin',
        '-v', 'error',
        '-i', filePath,
        '-map', `0:s:${trackIndex}`,
        '-f', 'webvtt',
        '-'
      ], { windowsHide: true });

      let vtt = '';
      proc.stdout.on('data', (d) => { vtt += d.toString(); });
      proc.on('close', (code) => {
        if (finished) return;
        finished = true;
        clearTimeout(timer);
        if (code === 0 && vtt.trim().length > 0) {
          resolve({ ok: true, content: vtt });
        } else {
          resolve({ ok: false, content: null });
        }
      });
      proc.on('error', (err) => {
        if (finished) return;
        finished = true;
        clearTimeout(timer);
        logger.debug('Embedded subtitle extraction error:', err.message);
        resolve({ ok: false, content: null });
      });
    }).catch(() => {
      resolve({ ok: false, content: null });
    });
  });
}

/**
 * Asynchronously query AniList and AniSkip in the background for anime episodes
 * without blocking local media probing or video startup.
 * @param {string} filePath
 * @param {number} duration
 * @returns {Promise<Array>}
 */
async function resolveAnimeChapters(filePath, duration = 0) {
  if (!filePath || typeof filePath !== 'string' || duration < 60) return [];
  try {
    const animeResolver = require('./anime-skip-resolver');
    const animeInfo = animeResolver.parseAnimeTitleAndEpisode(path.basename(filePath));
    if (!animeInfo) return [];
    const malId = await animeResolver.resolveMalId(animeInfo.title);
    if (!malId) return [];
    const skipData = await animeResolver.fetchAnimeSkipTimes(malId, animeInfo.episode, duration);
    if (skipData && (skipData.op || skipData.ed)) {
      const genChapters = animeResolver.buildContiguousChapters(skipData, duration);
      return genChapters.map((gc, i) => ({
        index: i,
        start: gc.start,
        end: gc.end,
        title: gc.title
      }));
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug('Background anime chapter resolution notice:', err.message);
  }
  return [];
}

module.exports = {
  MEDIA_EXTENSIONS,
  AUDIO_EXTENSIONS,
  SUB_EXTENSIONS,
  ISO_BMFF_ATOMS,
  FFMPEG_PATH,
  FFPROBE_PATH,
  hasFfmpeg,
  hasFfprobe,
  resolveMediaTool,
  parseByteRange,
  detectNeedsStream,
  detectNeedsStreamAsync,
  probeMediaFile,
  resolveAnimeChapters,
  detectSubtitleLanguage,
  naturalCompare,
  findAutoSubtitle,
  extractEmbeddedSubtitleVtt,
  toMediaUrl,
  buildMediaItem,
  scanMediaFolder,
  resolveCliMediaFile
};

// src/main/server.js
// Local HTTP AI Control Server & Streaming Engine with Dynamic Command Registry

const http = require('http');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');

const logger = createLogger('Server');

let appVersion = '1.3.1';
try {
  const pkgPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'package.json');
  if (fs.existsSync(pkgPath)) {
    appVersion = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(pkgPath, 'utf8')).version || '1.3.1';
  }
} catch {}

/**
 * Robust HTTP Response Helper
 */
function sendJson(res, status, obj) {
  try {
    const body = JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2);
    res.writeHead(status, {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
      'Content-Length': Buffer.byteLength(body)
    });
    res.end(body);
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error('Failed to send JSON response:', err.message);
  }
}

/**
 * Safe Helper to forward commands to the Renderer Process
 */
function forwardToRenderer(window, payload) {
  const win = window.getWindow();
  if (!win || win.isDestroyed()) return;

  if (win.webContents.isLoading()) {
    win.webContents.once('did-finish-load', () => {
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (!win.isDestroyed()) {
          win.webContents.send('ai:command', payload);
        }
      }, 250);
    });
  } else {
    win.webContents.send('ai:command', payload);
  }
}

/**
 * Dynamic Command Registry (Modular Command Pattern for Port 4500)
 */
class CommandRegistry {
  constructor() {
    this._commands = new Map();
  }

  /**
   * Register an action handler
   * @param {string} action Command / action name (e.g. 'play', 'seek', 'openFile')
   * @param {Object} definition { description, validate?: fn, execute: fn }
   */
  register(action, definition) {
    this._commands.set(action, definition);
    return this;
  }

  has(action) {
    return this._commands.has(action);
  }

  get(action) {
    return this._commands.get(action);
  }

  getSupportedActions() {
    return Array.from(this._commands.keys());
  }

  getDescriptions() {
    const desc = {};
    for (const [action, def] of this._commands.entries()) {
      desc[action] = def.description || '';
    }
    return desc;
  }

  /**
   * Execute registered action with validation and error boundary
   */
  async execute(action, payload, services) {
    const command = this._commands.get(action);
    if (!command) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        status: 400,
        error: `Unknown action "${action}". Supported actions: ${this.getSupportedActions().join(', ')}`
      };
    }

    if (typeof command.validate === 'function') {
      const val = command.validate(payload, services);
      if (!val.valid) {
        return {
          ok: false,
          status: val.status || 400,
          error: val.error
        };
      }
    }

    try {
      const result = await command.execute(payload, services);
      return {
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        dispatched: action,
        ...(result || {})
      };
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error(`Error executing command "${action}":`, err.stack || err.message);
      return {
        ok: false,
        status: 500,
        error: err.message
      };
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Build the Default Command Registry for VLCopyCat
 * @returns {CommandRegistry}
 */
function createDefaultCommandRegistry() {
  const registry = new CommandRegistry();

  // ------------------------------------------
  // Application Lifecycle Commands
  // ------------------------------------------
  registry.register('quit', {
    description: 'Quit CopyCat media player application',
    execute: async (_payload, { window }) => {
      setTimeout(() => {
        const win = window.getWindow();
        if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) win.close();
        else window.quit();
      }, 100);
      return {};
    }
  });

  registry.register('close', {
    description: 'Close active window',
    execute: async (_payload, { window }) => {
      setTimeout(() => {
        const win = window.getWindow();
        if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) win.close();
        else window.quit();
      }, 100);
      return {};
    }
  });

  registry.register('bringToFront', {
    description: 'Bring player window to front and focus',
    execute: async (_payload, { window }) => {
      window.bringToFront();
      return {};
    }
  });

  registry.register('show', {
    description: 'Show and focus window (alias for bringToFront)',
    execute: async (_payload, { window }) => {
      window.bringToFront();
      return {};
    }
  });

  registry.register('focus', {
    description: 'Focus window (alias for bringToFront)',
    execute: async (_payload, { window }) => {
      window.bringToFront();
      return {};
    }
  });

  registry.register('minimize', {
    description: 'Minimize player window',
    execute: async (_payload, { window }) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
        if (win.isFullScreen()) win.setFullScreen(false);
        win.minimize();
      }
      return {};
    }
  });

  registry.register('restore', {
    description: 'Restore minimized player window',
    execute: async (_payload, { window }) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
        if (win.isMinimized()) win.restore();
        window.bringToFront(win);
        window.broadcastState();
      }
      return {};
    }
  });

  registry.register('togglePip', {
    description: 'Toggle Picture-in-Picture window mode',
    execute: async (_payload, { window }) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
        win.webContents.executeJavaScript('document.getElementById("btn-pip")?.click()', true).catch(() => {});
      }
      return {};
    }
  });

  registry.register('toggleFullscreen', {
    description: 'Toggle fullscreen playback mode',
    execute: async (payload, { window }) => {
      forwardToRenderer(window, payload);
      return {};
    }
  });

  // ------------------------------------------
  // Media Loading & Navigation Commands
  // ------------------------------------------
  registry.register('openFile', {
    description: 'Open and play a local media file (requires "filePath")',
    validate: (payload) => {
      if (!payload.filePath || typeof payload.filePath !== 'string') {
        return { valid: false, status: 400, error: 'Action "openFile" requires a string "filePath"' };
      }
      if (!fs.existsSync(payload.filePath)) {
        return { valid: false, status: 404, error: `File not found: ${payload.filePath}` };
      }
      return { valid: true };
    },
    execute: async (payload, { window, media }) => {
      const item = media.buildItem(payload.filePath);
      const folder = media.scanFolder(item.folderPath);
      payload.resolved = { item, folder };
      forwardToRenderer(window, payload);
      return { filePath: payload.filePath, title: item.title };
    }
  });

  registry.register('openFolder', {
    description: 'Open a local directory in library sidebar (requires "folderPath")',
    validate: (payload) => {
      if (!payload.folderPath || typeof payload.folderPath !== 'string') {
        return { valid: false, status: 400, error: 'Action "openFolder" requires a string "folderPath"' };
      }
      if (!fs.existsSync(payload.folderPath)) {
        return { valid: false, status: 404, error: `Folder not found: ${payload.folderPath}` };
      }
      return { valid: true };
    },
    execute: async (payload, { window, media }) => {
      payload.resolvedFolder = media.scanFolder(payload.folderPath);
      forwardToRenderer(window, payload);
      return { folderPath: payload.folderPath };
    }
  });

  registry.register('enqueue', {
    description: 'Enqueue one or more media files into the playlist (requires "filePath" or "filePaths")',
    validate: (payload, { media }) => {
      const rawList = Array.isArray(payload.filePaths)
        ? payload.filePaths
        : payload.filePath
          ? [payload.filePath]
          : [];
      const validItems = rawList
        .filter((p) => p && typeof p === 'string' && fs.existsSync(p))
        .map((p) => media.buildItem(p));
      if (!validItems.length) {
        return { valid: false, status: 404, error: 'No valid existing media files found to enqueue' };
      }
      payload.resolvedItems = validItems;
      return { valid: true };
    },
    execute: async (payload, { window }) => {
      forwardToRenderer(window, payload);
      return { count: payload.resolvedItems.length };
    }
  });

  // ------------------------------------------
  // Core Playback Controls
  // ------------------------------------------
  const simplePlaybackActions = ['play', 'pause', 'togglePlay', 'stop', 'next', 'prev', 'mute'];
  for (const act of simplePlaybackActions) {
    registry.register(act, {
      description: `Trigger playback action "${act}"`,
      execute: async (payload, { window }) => {
        forwardToRenderer(window, payload);
        return {};
      }
    });
  }

  registry.register('seek', {
    description: 'Seek playback position (value in seconds, or delta in seconds)',
    validate: (payload) => {
      if (payload.value !== undefined && Number.isNaN(Number(payload.value))) {
        return { valid: false, status: 400, error: 'Seek "value" must be a valid number (seconds)' };
      }
      if (payload.delta !== undefined && Number.isNaN(Number(payload.delta))) {
        return { valid: false, status: 400, error: 'Seek "delta" must be a valid number (seconds)' };
      }
      return { valid: true };
    },
    execute: async (payload, { window }) => {
      forwardToRenderer(window, payload);
      return {};
    }
  });

  registry.register('volume', {
    description: 'Set player volume 0..200 (supports VLC 200% gain boost)',
    validate: (payload) => {
      if (payload.value === undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(payload.value))) {
        return { valid: false, status: 400, error: 'Volume action requires numeric "value" (0..200)' };
      }
      payload.value = Math.max(0, Math.min(200, Number(payload.value)));
      return { valid: true };
    },
    execute: async (payload, { window }) => {
      forwardToRenderer(window, payload);
      return { volume: payload.value };
    }
  });

  registry.register('speed', {
    description: 'Set playback rate 0.25..4.0',
    validate: (payload) => {
      if (payload.value === undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(payload.value))) {
        return { valid: false, status: 400, error: 'Speed action requires numeric "value" (0.25..4.0)' };
      }
      payload.value = Math.max(0.25, Math.min(4.0, Number(payload.value)));
      return { valid: true };
    },
    execute: async (payload, { window }) => {
      forwardToRenderer(window, payload);
      return { speed: payload.value };
    }
  });

  registry.register('setAccent', {
    description: 'Switch accent theme color (orange, amber, lime, emerald, cyan, blue, purple, magenta, rose, or #hex)',
    validate: (payload) => {
      if (!payload.value || typeof payload.value !== 'string') {
        return { valid: false, status: 400, error: 'setAccent action requires a string "value"' };
      }
      return { valid: true };
    },
    execute: async (payload, { window }) => {
      forwardToRenderer(window, payload);
      return { accent: payload.value };
    }
  });

  return registry;
}

/**
 * Start the Local HTTP AI Control Server with Injected Services & Command Registry
 * @param {number} port
 * @param {Object|ServiceContainer} containerOrServices
 * @param {CommandRegistry|null} customRegistry Optional custom command registry
 */
function startAiControlServer(port = 4500, containerOrServices, customRegistry = null) {
  // Resolve injected services
  const services = {
    window: containerOrServices.has && containerOrServices.has('window')
      ? containerOrServices.get('window')
      : containerOrServices.window,
    stream: containerOrServices.has && containerOrServices.has('stream')
      ? containerOrServices.get('stream')
      : containerOrServices.stream,
    media: containerOrServices.has && containerOrServices.has('media')
      ? containerOrServices.get('media')
      : containerOrServices.media
  };

  const { window, stream, media } = services;
  const commandRegistry = customRegistry || createDefaultCommandRegistry();

  const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
    try {
      const currentPort = (window && typeof window.getPort === 'function') ? window.getPort() : 4500;
      let urlObj;
      try {
        urlObj = new URL(req.url || '/', `http://127.0.0.1:${currentPort}`);
        decodeURI(urlObj.pathname);
      } catch {
        res.writeHead(400, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });
        res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Malformed request URL' }));
        return;
      }
      const pathname = urlObj.pathname;

    // 0. Anti-DNS Rebinding Host Guard:
    // Ensure the Host header strictly targets local loopback. Rejects external domain rebinding to 127.0.0.1.
    const rawHost = (req.headers.host || '').trim();
    const hostHeader = rawHost.split(':')[0].toLowerCase();
    const allowedLoopbackHosts = ['127.0.0.1', 'localhost', '::1', '[::1]'];
    if (hostHeader && !allowedLoopbackHosts.includes(hostHeader)) {
      logger.warn(`Blocked potential DNS rebinding attack with Host: ${rawHost}`);
      res.writeHead(403, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });
      res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Forbidden Host header (DNS Rebinding Guard)' }));
      return;
    }

    // 1. Security Origin Guard: Protect against cross-site CSRF/rebinding attacks from external web browsers.
    // Media streaming routes (/api/stream, /api/thumb) are requested by Electron's file:/// renderer,
    // which Chromium network security tags as sec-fetch-site: cross-site.
    // We strictly protect API control and status endpoints from cross-site browser CSRF.
    const origin = req.headers.origin;
    const secFetchSite = req.headers['sec-fetch-site'];
    const isMediaRoute = pathname === '/api/stream' || pathname === '/api/thumb';

    if (secFetchSite === 'cross-site' && !isMediaRoute) {
      logger.warn(`Blocked cross-site browser request to ${pathname} from origin: ${origin || 'unknown'}`);
      res.writeHead(403, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });
      res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Cross-origin browser requests forbidden' }));
      return;
    }

    // Block untrusted browser-initiated null-origin requests on control endpoint
    if (origin === 'null' && pathname === '/api/control' && req.method === 'POST') {
      if (secFetchSite && secFetchSite !== 'none') {
        logger.warn(`Blocked untrusted null origin request on /api/control (Sec-Fetch-Site: ${secFetchSite})`);
        res.writeHead(403, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });
        res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Untrusted null origin on control endpoint' }));
        return;
      }
    }

    if (origin && origin !== 'null') {
      try {
        const parsedOrigin = new URL(origin);
        const allowedHosts = ['127.0.0.1', 'localhost', 'app', 'copycat'];
        if (!allowedHosts.includes(parsedOrigin.hostname)) {
          logger.warn(`Blocked unauthorized origin: ${origin}`);
          res.writeHead(403, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });
          res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Forbidden origin' }));
          return;
        }
      } catch {
        res.writeHead(403, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });
        res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Invalid origin header' }));
        return;
      }
    }

    const allowedOrigin = origin && origin !== 'null' ? origin : '*';
    res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', allowedOrigin);
    res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, OPTIONS');
    res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Content-Type, Authorization, Range');

    if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
      res.writeHead(204);
      res.end();
      return;
    }

    // 2. GET / (Documentation & Discovery)
    if (req.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/') {
      return sendJson(res, 200, {
        name: 'CopyCat media player Local AI Control Bridge',
        version: appVersion,
        port: currentPort,
        ffmpegEnabled: media.hasFfmpeg(),
        endpoints: {
          'GET /api/status': 'Inspect live playback state, title, timestamps, volume, speed, and theme',
          'GET /api/stream': 'Zero-disk FFmpeg fragmented MP4 live stream for MPEG-TS, MKV, AVI, AC3, HEVC, etc.',
          'GET /api/thumb': 'Instant seekbar hover thumbnail preview frame generator',
          'POST /api/control': {
            description: 'Control CopyCat media player from any AI assistant, CLI or local script',
            actions: commandRegistry.getSupportedActions()
          }
        },
        actions: commandRegistry.getDescriptions(),
        status: window.getStatus()
      });
    }

    // 3. GET /api/status or /status
    if (req.method === 'GET' && (pathname === '/api/status' || pathname === '/status')) {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      const isWinValid = Boolean(win && !win.isDestroyed());
      return sendJson(res, 200, {
        ok: true,
        ...window.getStatus(),
        minimized: isWinValid ? win.isMinimized() : false,
        pipActive: window.isInPipMode,
        pipBounds: window.isInPipMode ? window.lastPipBounds : null
      });
    }

    // 4. GET /api/stream (Delegated to dedicated StreamProcessManager)
    if (req.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/api/stream') {
      return stream.handleStreamRequest(urlObj, req, res, media);
    }

    // 5. GET /api/thumb (Delegated to dedicated StreamProcessManager)
    if (req.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/api/thumb') {
      return stream.handleThumbRequest(urlObj, req, res, media);
    }

    // 6. POST /api/control or /command (Executed via Modular Command Registry)
    if (req.method === 'POST' && (pathname === '/api/control' || pathname === '/command')) {
      let raw = '';
      req.on('data', (chunk) => {
        raw += chunk;
        if (raw.length > 1e6) {
          logger.warn('Incoming POST payload exceeded 1MB limit. Destroying connection.');
          req.destroy();
        }
      });

      req.on('end', async () => {
        let payload;
        try {
          payload = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
        } catch (jsonErr) {
          logger.warn('Invalid JSON payload received:', jsonErr.message);
          return sendJson(res, 400, { ok: false, error: 'Malformed JSON payload' });
        }

        // Normalize action / command field
        const action = payload.action || payload.command;
        if (!action || typeof action !== 'string') {
          return sendJson(res, 400, { ok: false, error: 'Missing required "action" field in payload' });
        }
        payload.action = action;

        // Dispatch via Command Registry
        const result = await commandRegistry.execute(action, payload, services);
        const httpStatus = result.status || (result.ok ? 200 : 400);

        logger.info(`Command dispatched: ${action} (HTTP ${httpStatus})`);
        return sendJson(res, httpStatus, result);
      });
      return;
    }

    // 7. Fallback: 404 Not Found
    return sendJson(res, 404, {
      ok: false,
      error: `Endpoint "${pathname}" not found. Visit GET / for full API documentation.`
    });
    } catch (unexpectedErr) {
      logger.error('Unexpected server error handling request:', unexpectedErr.message);
      if (!res.headersSent) {
        res.writeHead(500, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });
        res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Internal Server Error' }));
      }
    }
  });

  server.on('error', (err) => {
    if (err.code === 'EADDRINUSE' && port < 4510) {
      logger.warn(`Port ${port} in use, trying port ${port + 1}...`);
      startAiControlServer(port + 1, services, commandRegistry);
    } else {
      logger.error('HTTP Server error:', err.message);
    }
  });

  server.listen(port, '127.0.0.1', () => {
    window.setPort(port);
    logger.info(`Local AI Control Server running at http://127.0.0.1:${port}`);
    window.broadcastState();
  });

  server.commandRegistry = commandRegistry;
  return server;
}

module.exports = {
  CommandRegistry,
  createDefaultCommandRegistry,
  startAiControlServer
};
``````

// src/main/session.js
// Windows Session Integrations, Taskbar Thumbar Management, Desktop Shortcuts, PiP Helpers & AI Sandbox Breakout

const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const { spawn, execFile } = require('child_process');
const { app, nativeImage } = require('electron');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');

const logger = createLogger('Session');

/**
 * Auto-breakout for AI terminal sandboxes (Antigravity exebox, background runner sessions)
 * Ensures the Electron GUI spawns onto the physical user desktop rather than headless background tasks.
 */
function handleAiSandboxBreakout() {
  if (
    process.platform === 'win32' &&
    (process.env.ANTIGRAVITY_AGENT || process.env.AI_SANDBOX) &&
    !process.argv.includes('--desktop-spawned')
  ) {
    try {
      const isPackaged = !process.defaultApp && !/[\\/]electron[\\/]dist[\\/]electron\.exe$/i.test(process.execPath);
      const exe = process.execPath;
      const cleanArgs = process.argv
        .slice(1)
        .filter((a) => a !== '.' && !a.startsWith('--'))
        .map((a) => `\\"${a.replace(/"/g, '\\"')}\\"`)
        .join(' ');
      const safeExe = exe.replace(/'/g, "''");
      let cmd;
      if (isPackaged) {
        cmd = `$s = New-Object -ComObject Shell.Application; $s.ShellExecute('${safeExe}', ('--desktop-spawned ' + '${cleanArgs.replace(/'/g, "''")}').Trim(), '', 'open', 1)`;
      } else {
        const appRoot = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..').replace(/'/g, "''");
        cmd = `$s = New-Object -ComObject Shell.Application; $s.ShellExecute('${safeExe}', ('"${appRoot}" --desktop-spawned ' + '${cleanArgs.replace(/'/g, "''")}').Trim(), '', 'open', 1)`;
      }
      spawn('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-ExecutionPolicy', 'Bypass', '-Command', cmd], {
        detached: true,
        stdio: 'ignore',
        windowsHide: true
      }).unref();
      logger.info('Sandbox breakout triggered successfully.');
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error('Sandbox breakout failed:', err.message);
    }
    process.exit(0);
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// Windows Native PiP Helper Binary
// ----------------------------------------------------
const PIP_HELPER_PATH = (() => {
  const packaged = path.join(process.resourcesPath || '', 'bin', 'pip-helper.exe');
  if (fs.existsSync(packaged)) return packaged;
  return path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'bin', 'pip-helper.exe');
})();

function getMainHwndString(win) {
  if (!win || win.isDestroyed()) return '0';
  try {
    const buf = win.getNativeWindowHandle();
    if (buf.length >= 8) return buf.readBigUInt64LE(0).toString();
    if (buf.length >= 4) return String(buf.readUInt32LE(0));
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug('Failed to read native window handle:', err.message);
  }
  return '0';
}

function resizePipWindow(win, aspect = 16 / 9) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (process.platform !== 'win32' || !fs.existsSync(PIP_HELPER_PATH)) {
      resolve({ success: false, bounds: null });
      return;
    }
    const hwndStr = getMainHwndString(win);
    const args = [
      String(process.pid),
      hwndStr,
      Number(aspect || 1.7778).toFixed(4),
      '0.28'
    ];
    execFile(PIP_HELPER_PATH, args, { windowsHide: true, timeout: 1500 }, (err, stdout) => {
      const out = String(stdout || '').trim();
      if (!err && out.startsWith('OK ')) {
        const bounds = out.slice(3);
        resolve({ success: true, bounds });
      } else {
        const bounds = out || (err ? err.message : 'ERR');
        resolve({ success: false, bounds });
      }
    });
  });
}

function restoreMainWindowWin32(win) {
  if (process.platform !== 'win32' || !fs.existsSync(PIP_HELPER_PATH)) return;
  const hwnd = getMainHwndString(win);
  if (hwnd && hwnd !== '0') {
    execFile(PIP_HELPER_PATH, ['--restore', hwnd], { windowsHide: true, timeout: 1000 }, () => {});
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// Taskbar Thumbar Management
// ----------------------------------------------------
function updateThumbarButtons(mainWindow, isPlaying = false) {
  if (process.platform !== 'win32' || !mainWindow || mainWindow.isDestroyed()) return;
  const normalizedPlaying = Boolean(isPlaying);
  if (mainWindow._thumbarPlaying === normalizedPlaying) return;
  mainWindow._thumbarPlaying = normalizedPlaying;

  const assetsDir = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'assets');
  const prevIcon = nativeImage.createFromPath(path.join(assetsDir, 'thumbar-prev.png'));
  const playIcon = nativeImage.createFromPath(path.join(assetsDir, normalizedPlaying ? 'thumbar-pause.png' : 'thumbar-play.png'));
  const nextIcon = nativeImage.createFromPath(path.join(assetsDir, 'thumbar-next.png'));

  try {
    mainWindow.setThumbarButtons([
      {
        tooltip: 'Previous Track',
        icon: prevIcon,
        click() {
          if (mainWindow && !mainWindow.isDestroyed()) {
            mainWindow.webContents.send('ai:command', { action: 'prev' });
          }
        }
      },
      {
        tooltip: normalizedPlaying ? 'Pause' : 'Play',
        icon: playIcon,
        click() {
          if (mainWindow && !mainWindow.isDestroyed()) {
            mainWindow.webContents.send('ai:command', { action: 'togglePlay' });
          }
        }
      },
      {
        tooltip: 'Next Track',
        icon: nextIcon,
        click() {
          if (mainWindow && !mainWindow.isDestroyed()) {
            mainWindow.webContents.send('ai:command', { action: 'next' });
          }
        }
      }
    ]);
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug('Failed to set Thumbar buttons:', err.message);
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// Desktop Shortcut Management
