``````javascript
function getDesktopShortcutPath() {
  const desktopPath = (app && typeof app.getPath === 'function')
    ? app.getPath('desktop')
    : path.join(os.homedir(), 'Desktop');
  return path.join(desktopPath, 'CopyCat Media Player.lnk');
}

function hasDesktopShortcut() {
  try {
    return fs.existsSync(getDesktopShortcutPath());
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function createDesktopShortcut() {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    try {
      const rootDir = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..');
      const shortcutPath = getDesktopShortcutPath();
      const icoPath = path.join(rootDir, 'assets', 'icon.ico');
      const copycatExe = path.join(rootDir, 'CopyCat.exe');
      const exeLauncher = path.join(rootDir, 'Launch_CopyCat.exe');
      const isPackaged = Boolean(app && app.isPackaged);
      const targetPath = isPackaged 
        ? process.execPath 
        : (fs.existsSync(copycatExe) ? copycatExe : (fs.existsSync(exeLauncher) ? exeLauncher : path.join(rootDir, 'Launch_CopyCat.bat')));
      const workingDir = isPackaged ? path.dirname(process.execPath) : rootDir;
      const iconLocation = fs.existsSync(icoPath) ? `${icoPath},0` : `${targetPath},0`;

      const psScript = `
        $ws = New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell;
        $s = $ws.CreateShortcut('${shortcutPath.replace(/'/g, "''")}');
        $s.TargetPath = '${targetPath.replace(/'/g, "''")}';
        $s.WorkingDirectory = '${workingDir.replace(/'/g, "''")}';
        $s.IconLocation = '${iconLocation.replace(/'/g, "''")}';
        $s.Description = 'CopyCat Media Player';
        $s.Save();
      `;

      const child = spawn('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-NonInteractive', '-Command', psScript], {
        windowsHide: true
      });

      child.on('error', (err) => {
        logger.error('Failed to spawn PowerShell for desktop shortcut:', err.message);
        resolve({ success: false, ok: false, error: err.message });
      });

      child.on('close', () => {
        const created = fs.existsSync(shortcutPath);
        logger.info(`Desktop shortcut creation result: ${created}`);
        resolve({ success: created, ok: created, shortcutPath });
      });
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error('Failed to create desktop shortcut:', err.message);
      resolve({ success: false, ok: false, error: err.message });
    }
  });
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// Windows File Associations & AI Integration
// ----------------------------------------------------
function getAssociationsScriptPath() {
  const rootDir = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..');
  const candidates = [
    path.join(process.resourcesPath || '', 'bin', 'register_associations.ps1'),
    path.join(rootDir, 'bin', 'register_associations.ps1')
  ];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (c && fs.existsSync(c)) return c;
  }
  return null;
}

function runAssociationsRegistration(targetExe = process.execPath) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    try {
      const regScript = getAssociationsScriptPath();
      if (!regScript) {
        logger.warn('register_associations.ps1 script not found');
        return resolve({ ok: false, error: 'register_associations.ps1 script not found' });
      }
      const rootDir = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..');
      const copycatExe = path.join(rootDir, 'CopyCat.exe');
      const candidateExe = (targetExe && !targetExe.toLowerCase().includes('electron.exe') && fs.existsSync(targetExe))
        ? targetExe
        : (fs.existsSync(copycatExe) ? copycatExe : targetExe);

      const args = ['-NoProfile', '-ExecutionPolicy', 'Bypass', '-File', regScript];
      if (candidateExe && typeof candidateExe === 'string' && fs.existsSync(candidateExe)) {
        args.push('-TargetExe', candidateExe);
      }
      const child = spawn('powershell.exe', args, {
        windowsHide: true,
        stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe']
      });
      let stderr = '';
      child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { stderr += d.toString(); });
      child.on('close', (code) => {
        logger.info(`register_associations.ps1 finished with exit code ${code}`);
        resolve({ ok: code === 0, error: stderr });
      });
      child.on('error', (err) => {
        logger.error('Failed to run register_associations.ps1:', err.message);
        resolve({ ok: false, error: err.message });
      });
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error('Associations execution exception:', err.message);
      resolve({ ok: false, error: err.message });
    }
  });
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// Window Focus & Bring to Front Helper
// ----------------------------------------------------
function bringToFront(win, userAlwaysOnTop = false) {
  if (!win || win.isDestroyed()) return;
  try {
    if (win.isMinimized()) win.restore();
    win.show();
    win.focus();
    if (!userAlwaysOnTop) {
      try {
        win.setAlwaysOnTop(true, 'screen-saver');
      } catch {
        win.setAlwaysOnTop(true);
      }
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (win && !win.isDestroyed() && !userAlwaysOnTop) {
          win.setAlwaysOnTop(false);
        }
      }, 250);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug('bringToFront error:', err.message);
  }
}

module.exports = {
  handleAiSandboxBreakout,

  runAssociationsRegistration,

};
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/sniffers/base-sniffer.js
// Core Browser Engine, Dynamic CDN Header Registry & Shared Infrastructure for Sniffer Strategies

const { BrowserWindow, session } = require('electron');
const { createLogger } = require('../logger');

const logger = createLogger('StreamSniffer');

const SNIFF_CACHE_TTL_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;
const sniffCache = new Map(); // webpageUrl -> { timestamp, result }

// Dynamic registry mapping CDN hostnames (e.g. coralarch.coastowl.top, video4.videa.hu) -> { Referer, Origin }
const hostHeaderRegistry = new Map();

/**
 * Check if a URL matches the strm.cx / Miruro CDN pattern (*.top/m/*, *.top/a/*, etc.)
 */
function isStrmCdnUrl(targetUrl) {
  if (!targetUrl || typeof targetUrl !== 'string') return false;
  return /strm\.cx|miruro|frostheron|coastowl|keeply|\.top\/(m|a|s|sub)\/|subtitles/i.test(targetUrl);
}

/**
 * Register custom Referer / Origin headers for a discovered stream CDN hostname
 */
function registerStreamHostHeaders(targetUrl, headers = {}) {
  try {
    const host = new URL(targetUrl).hostname.toLowerCase();
    if (!host) return;
    const existing = hostHeaderRegistry.get(host) || {};
    hostHeaderRegistry.set(host, {
      Referer: headers.Referer || existing.Referer || 'https://strm.cx/',
      Origin: headers.Origin || existing.Origin || 'https://strm.cx'
    });
  } catch {}
}

/**
 * Resolve the required anti-hotlink Referer & Origin headers for any stream or segment URL
 */
function getRequiredHeadersForUrl(targetUrl, customHeaders = {}) {
  let parsed = null;
  try { parsed = new URL(targetUrl); } catch {}
  const host = parsed ? parsed.hostname.toLowerCase() : '';
  const defaultOrigin = parsed ? parsed.origin : '';

  const registered = host ? hostHeaderRegistry.get(host) : null;
  const isStrm = Boolean(registered || isStrmCdnUrl(targetUrl));

  const referer = customHeaders.Referer
    || registered?.Referer
    || (isStrm ? 'https://strm.cx/' : defaultOrigin);

  const origin = customHeaders.Origin
    || registered?.Origin
    || (isStrm ? 'https://strm.cx' : defaultOrigin);

  return {
    'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/128.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
    ...(referer ? { 'Referer': referer } : {}),
    ...(origin ? { 'Origin': origin } : {}),
    ...customHeaders
  };
}

/**
 * Run a headless sniffer session using a specialized Strategy object
 * @param {string} webpageUrl
 * @param {number} timeoutMs
 * @param {object} strategy
 */
function runSnifferSession(webpageUrl, timeoutMs, strategy) {
  const normalizedUrl = (webpageUrl || '').trim();

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    let finished = false;
    let snifferWin = null;
    let timer = null;
    let settleTimer = null;

    const capturedStreams = [];
    const capturedSubtitles = [];
    let activeLangCheck = null;
    let switchTriggered = false;
    let streamIndexAtSwitch = -1;

    let pageOrigin = '';
    try { pageOrigin = new URL(normalizedUrl).origin; } catch {}

    const collectInPageSubtitles = async () => {
      if (!snifferWin || snifferWin.isDestroyed()) return;
      try {
        const frames = [
          snifferWin.webContents.mainFrame,
          ...(snifferWin.webContents.mainFrame?.frames || [])
        ];
        for (const frame of frames) {
          if (!frame || frame.isDestroyed()) continue;
          try {
            const execPromise = frame.executeJavaScript(`
              (async () => {
                const results = [];
                const formatTime = (sec) => {
                  if (isNaN(sec) || sec < 0) sec = 0;
                  const h = Math.floor(sec / 3600);
                  const m = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60);
                  const s = Math.floor(sec % 60);
                  const ms = Math.floor((sec % 1) * 1000);
                  return String(h).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(m).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(s).padStart(2, '0') + '.' + String(ms).padStart(3, '0');
                };

                // 1. Vidstack or custom player textTracks (e.g. strm.cx, miruro, modern HTML5 players)
                const mp = document.querySelector('media-player');
                if (mp && mp.textTracks) {
                  for (let i = 0; i < mp.textTracks.length; i++) {
                    const t = mp.textTracks[i];
                    if (t.kind === 'subtitles' || t.kind === 'captions') {
                      try {
                        t.mode = 'showing';
                        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 150));
                        let content = '';
                        if (t.src) {
                          try {
                            content = await fetch(t.src).then(r => r.text()).catch(() => '');
                          } catch {}
                        }
                        if ((!content || content.length < 10) && t.cues && t.cues.length > 0) {
                          let vtt = 'WEBVTT\\n\\n';
                          for (let c = 0; c < t.cues.length; c++) {
                            const cue = t.cues[c];
                            vtt += formatTime(cue.startTime) + ' --> ' + formatTime(cue.endTime) + '\\n' + (cue.text || '') + '\\n\\n';
                          }
                          content = vtt;
                        }
                        results.push({
                          label: t.label || t.language || ('Track ' + (i + 1)),
                          lang: t.language || 'und',
                          url: t.src || '',
                          content: (content && content.length > 10) ? content : null
                        });
                      } catch {}
                    }
                  }
                }

                // 2. Standard HTML5 <video> and <track> elements
                const v = document.querySelector('video');
                if (v) {
                  const trackEls = Array.from(v.querySelectorAll('track'));
                  for (let i = 0; i < trackEls.length; i++) {
                    const t = trackEls[i];
                    const label = t.label || t.srclang || ('Track ' + (i + 1));
                    if (!results.some(s => s.label === label)) {
                      let content = '';
                      if (t.src) {
                        try {
                          content = await fetch(t.src).then(r => r.text()).catch(() => '');
                        } catch {}
                      }
                      if ((!content || content.length < 10) && t.track && t.track.cues && t.track.cues.length > 0) {
                        let vtt = 'WEBVTT\\n\\n';
                        for (let c = 0; c < t.track.cues.length; c++) {
                          const cue = t.track.cues[c];
                          vtt += formatTime(cue.startTime) + ' --> ' + formatTime(cue.endTime) + '\\n' + (cue.text || '') + '\\n\\n';
                        }
                        content = vtt;
                      }
                      results.push({
                        label,
                        lang: t.srclang || 'und',
                        url: t.src || '',
                        content: (content && content.length > 10) ? content : null
                      });
                    }
                  }
                }

                return results;
              })()
            `).catch(() => []);
            const timeoutPromise = new Promise((r) => setTimeout(() => r([]), 450));
            const list = await Promise.race([execPromise, timeoutPromise]);

            if (Array.isArray(list)) {
              for (const item of list) {
                if (item && item.label && (item.content || item.url)) {
                  if (!capturedSubtitles.some((s) => s.label === item.label)) {
                    capturedSubtitles.push(item);
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          } catch {}
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.debug('Error collecting in-page subtitles:', err.message);
      }
    };

    const finalize = async (result) => {
      if (finished) return;
      finished = true;
      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
      if (settleTimer) clearTimeout(settleTimer);

      if (snifferWin && !snifferWin.isDestroyed()) {
        try {
          await Promise.race([
            collectInPageSubtitles(),
            new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600))
          ]);
        } catch {}
        try {
          snifferWin.destroy();
        } catch {}
      }
      snifferWin = null;

      if (result) {
        result.subtitles = capturedSubtitles.slice(0, 8);
      }

      if (result && result.streamUrl) {
        sniffCache.set(normalizedUrl, {
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          result
        });
      }
      resolve(result);
    };

    const buildResultFromCaptured = (langCheck = null) => {
      if (capturedStreams.length === 0) return null;
      const primary = capturedStreams[0];
      let pageTitle = '';
      try {
        if (snifferWin && !snifferWin.isDestroyed()) pageTitle = snifferWin.webContents.getTitle();
      } catch {}
      const check = langCheck || activeLangCheck;

      let subStreamUrl = primary.url;
      let dubStreamUrl = null;

      // Only assign alternate language stream if an alternate language was explicitly verified
      // and a new stream was captured after the switch was triggered
      if (check && check.hasAlternate && streamIndexAtSwitch >= 0) {
        const alternateStreams = capturedStreams.slice(streamIndexAtSwitch);
        const altStream = alternateStreams.find((s) => s.url !== primary.url);
        if (altStream) {
          if (check.current === 'sub') {
            subStreamUrl = primary.url;
            dubStreamUrl = altStream.url;
          } else {
            dubStreamUrl = primary.url;
            subStreamUrl = altStream.url;
          }
        }
      }

      return {
        streamUrl: primary.url,
        subStreamUrl,
        dubStreamUrl,
        title: pageTitle || 'Extracted Video Stream',
        isHls: primary.isHls,
        isDash: primary.isDash,
        subtitles: capturedSubtitles.slice(0, 8),
        pageUrl: normalizedUrl,
        strategy: strategy.name
      };
    };

    timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      logger.debug(`[${strategy.name}] Sniffer timeout reached (${timeoutMs}ms)`);
      await finalize(buildResultFromCaptured());
    }, timeoutMs);

    try {
      if (!session || typeof session.fromPartition !== 'function' || !BrowserWindow) {
        logger.debug('Stream sniffer invoked outside Electron runtime; skipping browser sniffing.');
        return resolve(null);
      }
      const partition = `sniffer-${Date.now()}`;
      const sniffSession = session.fromPartition(partition);

      // Block all device permissions for the ephemeral sniffer session
      if (typeof sniffSession.setPermissionRequestHandler === 'function') {
        sniffSession.setPermissionRequestHandler((_webContents, _permission, callback) => callback(false));
      }

      sniffSession.webRequest.onBeforeSendHeaders((details, callback) => {
        const u = details.url;
        const lower = u.toLowerCase();

        // 1. Delegate to strategy-specific headers hook if available
        if (typeof strategy.onBeforeSendHeaders === 'function') {
          const handled = strategy.onBeforeSendHeaders(details, {
            registerStreamHostHeaders,
            pageOrigin,
            normalizedUrl
          });
          if (handled) {
            return callback({ cancel: false, requestHeaders: details.requestHeaders });
          }
        }

        // 2. Default dynamic CDN header registration
        const ref = details.requestHeaders['Referer'] || details.requestHeaders['referer'] || details.referrer || '';
        const orig = details.requestHeaders['Origin'] || details.requestHeaders['origin'] || '';
        if (ref || orig) {
          const effectiveRef = ref || (pageOrigin ? `${pageOrigin}/` : '');
          const effectiveOrig = orig || pageOrigin || '';
          if (effectiveRef && (/\.m3u8|\/m\/|\/a\/|\.mpd|\.mp4|\.vtt|\.srt|\/sub/i.test(u) || details.resourceType === 'media' || lower.includes('videa.hu') || lower.includes('indavideo.hu'))) {
            registerStreamHostHeaders(u, {
              Referer: effectiveRef,
              Origin: effectiveOrig
            });
          }
        }

        callback({ cancel: false, requestHeaders: details.requestHeaders });
      });

      sniffSession.webRequest.onBeforeRequest({ urls: ['*://*/*'] }, (details, callback) => {
        const u = details.url;
        const lower = u.toLowerCase();

        // Check if request is considered an ad
        const isAd = (
          lower.includes('google-analytics') ||
          lower.includes('doubleclick') ||
          lower.includes('adservice') ||
          lower.includes('popads') ||
          lower.includes('exoclick') ||
          lower.includes('adocean') ||
          lower.includes('2mdn.net') ||
          (typeof strategy.isAdUrl === 'function' && strategy.isAdUrl(u, details))
        );

        if (!isAd && !finished) {
          // 1. Check for subtitle tracks (.vtt / .srt or CDN /s/ /sub/ paths)
          if ((/\.(vtt|srt)(\?|$)/i.test(u) || lower.includes('/sub.vtt') || lower.includes('/subtitles/') || /\/s\/[a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+/i.test(u) || /\.top\/s\//i.test(u) || lower.includes('/sub/')) && !lower.includes('thumb') && !lower.includes('sprite')) {
            if (isStrmCdnUrl(u)) {
              registerStreamHostHeaders(u, {
                Referer: 'https://strm.cx/',
                Origin: 'https://strm.cx'
              });
            }
            if (!capturedSubtitles.some((s) => s.url === u)) {
              let subLabel = 'Subtitle';
              try {
                const seg = new URL(u).pathname.split('/').pop() || '';
                subLabel = seg.replace(/\.(vtt|srt)$/i, '') || `Track ${capturedSubtitles.length + 1}`;
              } catch {}
              capturedSubtitles.push({ url: u, label: subLabel });
            }
          }

          // 2. Check for media streams via strategy classifier or universal defaults
          let mediaCheck = null;
          if (typeof strategy.classifyMediaRequest === 'function') {
            mediaCheck = strategy.classifyMediaRequest(details, u, lower);
          }

          if (!mediaCheck) {
            const isStaticAsset = /\.(js|css|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|webp|svg|woff|woff2|ttf|eot|ico)(\?|$)/i.test(u);
            if (!isStaticAsset) {
              const isHls = lower.includes('.m3u8') || lower.includes('/m/') || lower.includes('/pl.');
              const isDash = lower.includes('.mpd');
              const isDirectVideo = details.resourceType === 'media' || /\.(mp4|webm|m4v|mkv)(\?|$)/i.test(u) || lower.includes('.mp4') || lower.includes('.webm') || (lower.includes('video') && /\/static\/[0-9]+p\//.test(lower));
              if (isHls || isDash || isDirectVideo) {
                mediaCheck = { isHls, isDash, isDirectVideo };
              }
            }
          }

          if (mediaCheck) {
            const { isHls, isDash } = mediaCheck;
            if (isStrmCdnUrl(u)) {
              registerStreamHostHeaders(u, {
                Referer: 'https://strm.cx/',
                Origin: 'https://strm.cx'
              });
            }
            if (!capturedStreams.some((s) => s.url === u)) {
              logger.info(`[${strategy.name}] Sniffer captured media stream:`, u);
              capturedStreams.push({ url: u, isHls, isDash });

              // If strategy provides a stream capture hook (e.g. Miruro sub/dub switch)
              if (typeof strategy.onStreamCaptured === 'function') {
                strategy.onStreamCaptured({
                  streamUrl: u,
                  capturedStreams,
                  snifferWin,
                  settleTimer,
                  resetSettleTimer: (ms, cb) => {
                    if (settleTimer) clearTimeout(settleTimer);
                    settleTimer = setTimeout(cb, ms);
                  },
                  finalizeWithResult: (check) => finalize(buildResultFromCaptured(check)),
                  onAlternateLanguageDetected: (check) => {
                    activeLangCheck = check;
                    switchTriggered = true;
                    streamIndexAtSwitch = capturedStreams.length;
                  }
                });
              } else if (!settleTimer) {
                settleTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                  finalize(buildResultFromCaptured());
                }, strategy.settleDelayMs || 850);
              }
            }
          }
        }

        callback({});
      });

      snifferWin = new BrowserWindow({
        show: false,
        width: 1280,
        height: 720,
        webPreferences: {
          session: sniffSession,
          webSecurity: false,
          nodeIntegration: false,
          contextIsolation: true
        }
      });

      snifferWin.webContents.setAudioMuted(true);

      // Hardened Security Isolation Boundary for Headless Sniffer Window:
      // 1. Block opening any popup or secondary window
      if (typeof snifferWin.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler === 'function') {
        snifferWin.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(() => ({ action: 'deny' }));
      }

      // 2. Allow only http: and https: protocols; block file://, javascript:, custom schemes
      snifferWin.webContents.on('will-navigate', (event, navUrl) => {
        try {
          const u = new URL(navUrl);
          if (u.protocol !== 'http:' && u.protocol !== 'https:') {
            event.preventDefault();
            logger.warn(`Blocked sniffer navigation to forbidden protocol: ${navUrl}`);
          }
        } catch {
          event.preventDefault();
        }
      });

      // 3. Block subframes from navigating to local loopback endpoints or local files
      snifferWin.webContents.on('will-frame-navigate', (event) => {
        try {
          const u = new URL(event.url);
          const h = u.hostname.toLowerCase();
          if (h === '127.0.0.1' || h === 'localhost' || h === '::1' || u.protocol === 'file:') {
            event.preventDefault();
            logger.warn(`Blocked sniffer frame navigation to local target: ${event.url}`);
          }
        } catch {}
      });

      const triggerPlayInAllFrames = () => {
        if (finished || !snifferWin || snifferWin.isDestroyed() || capturedStreams.length > 0) return;
        const frames = [
          snifferWin.webContents.mainFrame,
          ...(snifferWin.webContents.mainFrame?.frames || [])
        ];
        const extraSelectors = strategy.playSelectors ? `, ${strategy.playSelectors}` : '';
        for (const frame of frames) {
          if (!frame || frame.isDestroyed()) continue;
          frame.executeJavaScript(`
            try {
              const v = document.querySelector('video');
              if (v) { v.muted = true; v.play().catch(() => {}); }
              const playBtn = document.querySelector('#big_play, .bigplay, .play_button, [aria-label*="Play" i], .vds-play-button, .jw-icon-display, .plyr__control--overlaid, .vjs-big-play-button${extraSelectors}');
              if (playBtn) playBtn.click();
            } catch {}
          `).catch(() => {});
        }
      };

      snifferWin.webContents.on('did-finish-load', () => {
        setTimeout(triggerPlayInAllFrames, 500);
        setTimeout(triggerPlayInAllFrames, 1600);
        setTimeout(triggerPlayInAllFrames, 3200);
      });

      snifferWin.loadURL(normalizedUrl, {
        userAgent: strategy.userAgent || 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/128.0.0.0 Safari/537.36'
      }).catch((err) => {
        logger.debug(`[${strategy.name}] Sniffer page load notice:`, err.message);
      });
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error(`[${strategy.name}] Failed to launch stream sniffer:`, err.message);
      finalize(null);
    }
  });
}

module.exports = {
  isStrmCdnUrl,
  registerStreamHostHeaders,
  getRequiredHeadersForUrl,
  sniffCache,
  SNIFF_CACHE_TTL_MS,
  runSnifferSession,
  logger
};
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/sniffers/generic-sniffer.js
// Universal Fallback Sniffer Strategy for General Web Media & HTML5 Video Platforms

const genericSniffer = {
  name: 'GenericSniffer',

  /**
   * Universal catch-all fallback
   */
  matches(_url) {
    return true;
  },

  playSelectors: '#big_play, .bigplay, .play_button, [aria-label*="Play" i], .jw-icon-display, .plyr__control--overlaid',
  settleDelayMs: 850,

  /**
   * Classify universal media stream requests (.m3u8, .mpd, .mp4, .webm, or resourceType === 'media')
   */
  classifyMediaRequest(details, u, lower) {
    const isStaticAsset = /\.(js|css|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|webp|svg|woff|woff2|ttf|eot|ico)(\?|$)/i.test(u);
    if (isStaticAsset) return null;

    const isHls = lower.includes('.m3u8') || lower.includes('/m/') || lower.includes('/pl.');
    const isDash = lower.includes('.mpd');
    const isDirectVideo = (
      details.resourceType === 'media' ||
      /\.(mp4|webm|m4v|mkv)(\?|$)/i.test(u) ||
      lower.includes('.mp4') ||
      lower.includes('.webm')
    );

    if (isHls || isDash || isDirectVideo) {
      return { isHls, isDash, isDirectVideo };
    }
    return null;
  }
};

module.exports = genericSniffer;
``````

################################################################################

``````javascript
// src/main/sniffers/miruro-sniffer.js
// Dedicated Sniffer Strategy for Miruro & strm.cx Anime Streaming Platforms

const { isStrmCdnUrl, logger } = require('./base-sniffer');

const miruroSniffer = {
  name: 'MiruroSniffer',

  /**
   * Determine if this sniffer owns the given URL
   */
  matches(url) {
    if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return false;
    return isStrmCdnUrl(url) || /miruro|strm\.cx/i.test(url);
  },

  playSelectors: '.vds-play-button, media-player [aria-label*="Play" i]',
  settleDelayMs: 850,

  /**
   * Custom request header handler for Miruro & strm.cx CDN domains
   */
  onBeforeSendHeaders(details, { registerStreamHostHeaders }) {
    const u = details.url;
    if (isStrmCdnUrl(u)) {
      details.requestHeaders['Referer'] = 'https://strm.cx/';
      details.requestHeaders['Origin'] = 'https://strm.cx';
      registerStreamHostHeaders(u, {
        Referer: 'https://strm.cx/',
        Origin: 'https://strm.cx'
      });
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  },

  /**
   * Classify media stream requests (.m3u8, /m/ playlists, /a/ audio, DASH)
   */
  classifyMediaRequest(details, u, lower) {
    const isHls = lower.includes('.m3u8') || lower.includes('/m/') || lower.includes('/pl.');
    const isDash = lower.includes('.mpd');
    const isDirectVideo = /\.(mp4|webm)(\?|$)/i.test(u) || lower.includes('.mp4') || lower.includes('.webm');
    if (isHls || isDash || isDirectVideo) {
      return { isHls, isDash, isDirectVideo };
    }
    return null;
  },

  /**
   * Automated sub/dub alternate stream discovery on first captured rendition
   */
  onStreamCaptured({ capturedStreams, snifferWin, resetSettleTimer, finalizeWithResult, onAlternateLanguageDetected }) {
    if (capturedStreams.length === 1) {
      if (snifferWin && !snifferWin.isDestroyed()) {
        snifferWin.webContents.executeJavaScript(`
          new Promise((resolve) => {
            try {
              const langBtn = document.querySelector('button[aria-label="Language"]') || 
                Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button')).find(b => /^(sub|dub)$/i.test((b.innerText || '').trim()));
              if (!langBtn) return resolve({ hasAlternate: false });
              const currentText = (langBtn.innerText || '').trim().toLowerCase();
              const target = currentText === 'sub' ? 'dub' : 'sub';
              langBtn.click();
              setTimeout(() => {
                try {
                  const candidates = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(
                    '[role="menuitem"], [role="option"], [data-radix-collection-item], [role="menu"] *, button, div, span'
                  ));
                  const targetEl = candidates.find(el => (el.innerText || '').trim().toLowerCase() === target);
                  if (targetEl) {
                    targetEl.click();
                    return resolve({ hasAlternate: true, current: currentText, target });
                  }
                  langBtn.click();
                  return resolve({ hasAlternate: false, current: currentText });
                } catch {
                  return resolve({ hasAlternate: false });
                }
              }, 350);
            } catch {
              return resolve({ hasAlternate: false });
            }
          });
        `).then((langCheck) => {
          if (langCheck && langCheck.hasAlternate) {
            onAlternateLanguageDetected(langCheck);
            logger.info(`[MiruroSniffer] Detected alternate language track (${langCheck.target}), waiting for alternate stream...`);
            resetSettleTimer(2500, () => finalizeWithResult(langCheck));
          } else {
            resetSettleTimer(850, () => finalizeWithResult(langCheck));
          }
        }).catch(() => {
          resetSettleTimer(850, () => finalizeWithResult());
        });
      } else {
        resetSettleTimer(850, () => finalizeWithResult());
      }
    } else if (capturedStreams.length >= 2) {
      resetSettleTimer(750, () => finalizeWithResult());
    }
  }
};

module.exports = miruroSniffer;
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/sniffers/videa-sniffer.js
// Dedicated Sniffer Strategy for Hungarian Anime & Video Streaming Hosts (Videa, JobbMintATv, Indavideo)

const videaSniffer = {
  name: 'VideaSniffer',

  /**
   * Determine if this sniffer owns the given URL
   */
  matches(url) {
    if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return false;
    return /videa\.hu|jobbmintatv\.pro|indavideo\.hu/i.test(url);
  },

  playSelectors: '.vjs-big-play-button, #big_play, .play_button, .vjs-play-control',
  settleDelayMs: 650,

  /**
   * Filter out known video ad networks common on Hungarian aggregator sites
   */
  isAdUrl(url, _details) {
    const lower = url.toLowerCase();
    return (
      lower.includes('adocean') ||
      lower.includes('2mdn.net') ||
      lower.includes('indidata.com') ||
      lower.includes('gemius.pl') ||
      lower.includes('cfusion-internet.com')
    );
  },

  /**
   * Register host headers for Videa and Indavideo CDN edges
   */
  onBeforeSendHeaders(details, { registerStreamHostHeaders, pageOrigin }) {
    const u = details.url;
    const lower = u.toLowerCase();
    const ref = details.requestHeaders['Referer'] || details.requestHeaders['referer'] || details.referrer || '';
    const orig = details.requestHeaders['Origin'] || details.requestHeaders['origin'] || '';

    if (lower.includes('videa.hu')) {
      const effectiveRef = ref || 'https://videa.hu/';
      const effectiveOrig = orig || 'https://videa.hu';
      registerStreamHostHeaders(u, { Referer: effectiveRef, Origin: effectiveOrig });
      return true;
    }

    if (lower.includes('indavideo.hu')) {
      const effectiveRef = ref || 'https://embed.indavideo.hu/';
      const effectiveOrig = orig || 'https://embed.indavideo.hu';
      registerStreamHostHeaders(u, { Referer: effectiveRef, Origin: effectiveOrig });
      return true;
    }

    if (ref || orig) {
      registerStreamHostHeaders(u, {
        Referer: ref || (pageOrigin ? `${pageOrigin}/` : ''),
        Origin: orig || pageOrigin || ''
      });
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  },

  /**
   * Classify progressive MP4 media streams or HLS playlists from Videa & Indavideo
   */
  classifyMediaRequest(details, u, lower) {
    const isStaticAsset = /\.(js|css|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|webp|svg|woff|woff2|ttf|eot|ico)(\?|$)/i.test(u);
    if (isStaticAsset) return null;

    const isHls = lower.includes('.m3u8') || lower.includes('/m/');
    const isDash = lower.includes('.mpd');
    const isDirectVideo = (
      details.resourceType === 'media' ||
      /\.(mp4|webm|m4v)(\?|$)/i.test(u) ||
      lower.includes('.mp4') ||
      (lower.includes('video') && /\/static\/[0-9]+p\//.test(lower)) ||
      (lower.includes('indavideo.hu') && details.resourceType === 'media')
    );

    if (isHls || isDash || isDirectVideo) {
      return { isHls, isDash, isDirectVideo };
    }
    return null;
  }
};

module.exports = videaSniffer;
``````

## DATEI: ./C:/Users/Schual/Desktop/VLCopyCat-Master/src/main/stream-manager.js
################################################################################

``````javascript
// src/main/stream-manager.js
// Dedicated Stream & Thumbnail Worker Process Lifecycle Manager for VLCopyCat

const fs = require('fs');
const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');
const { getRequiredHeadersForUrl } = require('./stream-sniffer');

const logger = createLogger('StreamManager');
const MAX_THUMB_CACHE = 120;

class StreamProcessManager {
  constructor() {
    this.activeStreamProc = null;
    this.activeThumbProc = null;
    this.thumbCache = new Map();
  }

  /**
   * Safely terminate active FFmpeg live stream worker
   */
  killActiveStreamProc() {
    if (this.activeStreamProc) {
      const proc = this.activeStreamProc;
      this.activeStreamProc = null;
      try {
        if (process.platform === 'win32' && proc.pid) {
          try { proc.kill('SIGKILL'); } catch {}
          const killer = spawn('taskkill', ['/pid', String(proc.pid), '/T', '/F'], { windowsHide: true });
          killer.on('error', () => {});
          killer.unref();
        } else {
          proc.kill('SIGKILL');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.debug('Stream process kill notice:', err.message);
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Safely terminate active FFmpeg thumbnail worker
   */
  killActiveThumbProc() {
    if (this.activeThumbProc) {
      const proc = this.activeThumbProc;
      this.activeThumbProc = null;
      try {
        if (process.platform === 'win32' && proc.pid) {
          try { proc.kill('SIGKILL'); } catch {}
          const killer = spawn('taskkill', ['/pid', String(proc.pid), '/T', '/F'], { windowsHide: true });
          killer.on('error', () => {});
          killer.unref();
        } else {
          proc.kill('SIGKILL');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.debug('Thumbnail process kill notice:', err.message);
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Kill all background media worker processes
   */
  killAll() {
    this.killActiveStreamProc();
    this.killActiveThumbProc();
  }

  /**
   * Build anti-hotlink HTTP headers string for FFmpeg network inputs
   */
  _buildNetworkHeaders(targetUrl) {
    const hdrs = getRequiredHeadersForUrl(targetUrl);
    return Object.entries(hdrs).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join('\r\n') + '\r\n';
  }

  /**
   * Handle incoming HTTP /api/stream requests
   * Streams fragmented MP4 over HTTP using FFmpeg (supports local files and remote HLS/HTTP streams)
   * @param {URL} urlObj
   * @param {http.IncomingMessage} req
   * @param {http.ServerResponse} res
   * @param {Object} mediaService
   */
  handleStreamRequest(urlObj, req, res, mediaService) {
    const filePath = urlObj.searchParams.get('path') || '';
    const audioPath = urlObj.searchParams.get('audio') || '';
    const startSec = Math.max(0, Number(urlObj.searchParams.get('start')) || 0);
    const vcodec = (urlObj.searchParams.get('vcodec') || '').toLowerCase();
    const forceTranscode = urlObj.searchParams.get('transcode') === '1';

    const ffmpegPath = mediaService.getFfmpegPath();
    const isHttpUrl = /^https?:\/\//i.test(filePath);
    if (!ffmpegPath || !filePath || (!isHttpUrl && !fs.existsSync(filePath))) {
      res.writeHead(404, { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' });
      res.end('Media file or FFmpeg binary not found');
      return;
    }

    this.killActiveStreamProc();

    const canCopyVideo = !forceTranscode && (!vcodec || ['h264', 'avc1', 'mp4v'].includes(vcodec) || isHttpUrl);
    const args = ['-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error'];

    if (isHttpUrl) {
      args.push(
        '-reconnect', '1',
        '-reconnect_streamed', '1',
        '-reconnect_delay_max', '5',
        '-extension_picky', '0',
        '-allowed_segment_extensions', 'ALL',
        '-headers', this._buildNetworkHeaders(filePath)
      );
    }

    if (startSec > 0.05) {
      args.push('-ss', startSec.toFixed(3));
    }

    args.push('-i', filePath);

    if (audioPath && /^https?:\/\//i.test(audioPath)) {
      args.push(
        '-reconnect', '1',
        '-reconnect_streamed', '1',
        '-reconnect_delay_max', '5',
        '-extension_picky', '0',
        '-allowed_segment_extensions', 'ALL',
        '-headers', this._buildNetworkHeaders(audioPath)
      );
      if (startSec > 0.05) {
        args.push('-ss', startSec.toFixed(3));
      }
      args.push('-i', audioPath);
      args.push('-map', '0:v:0?', '-map', '1:a:0?');
    } else {
      args.push('-map', '0:v:0?', '-map', '0:a:0?');
    }

    if (canCopyVideo) {
      args.push('-c:v', 'copy');
    } else {
      args.push(
        '-c:v', 'libx264',
        '-preset', 'ultrafast',
        '-tune', 'zerolatency',
        '-crf', '20',
        '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p'
      );
    }

    args.push(
      '-c:a', 'aac',
      '-b:a', '192k',
      '-ac', '2',
      '-sn',
      '-dn',
      '-avoid_negative_ts', 'make_zero',
      '-threads', '0',
      '-f', 'mp4',
      '-movflags', 'frag_keyframe+empty_moov+default_base_moof',
      'pipe:1'
    );

    res.writeHead(200, {
      'Content-Type': 'video/mp4',
      'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
      'Cache-Control': 'no-store'
    });

    try {
      req.socket?.setNoDelay?.(true);
      res.socket?.setNoDelay?.(true);
    } catch {}

    const proc = spawn(ffmpegPath, args, { windowsHide: true });
    this.activeStreamProc = proc;

    proc.stdout.pipe(res);

    const cleanup = () => {
      if (this.activeStreamProc === proc) {
        this.activeStreamProc = null;
      }
      try {
        proc.kill('SIGKILL');
      } catch {}
    };

    req.on('close', cleanup);
    res.on('close', cleanup);
    proc.on('error', cleanup);
  }

  /**
   * Handle incoming HTTP /api/thumb requests
   * Extracts instant on-demand JPEG frame for timeline hover preview
   * @param {URL} urlObj
   * @param {http.IncomingMessage} req
   * @param {http.ServerResponse} res
   * @param {Object} mediaService
   */
  handleThumbRequest(urlObj, req, res, mediaService) {
    const filePath = urlObj.searchParams.get('path') || '';
    const timeSec = Math.max(0, Number(urlObj.searchParams.get('t')) || 0);

    const ffmpegPath = mediaService.getFfmpegPath();
    const isHttpUrl = /^https?:\/\//i.test(filePath);
    if (!ffmpegPath || !filePath || (!isHttpUrl && !fs.existsSync(filePath))) {
      res.writeHead(404);
      res.end();
      return;
    }

    const cacheKey = `${filePath}:${timeSec.toFixed(1)}`;
    if (this.thumbCache.has(cacheKey)) {
      const cachedBuf = this.thumbCache.get(cacheKey);
      this.thumbCache.delete(cacheKey);
      this.thumbCache.set(cacheKey, cachedBuf);

      res.writeHead(200, {
        'Content-Type': 'image/jpeg',
        'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
        'Cache-Control': 'max-age=180',
        'Content-Length': cachedBuf.length
      });
      res.end(cachedBuf);
      return;
    }

    this.killActiveThumbProc();

    const args = [
      '-hide_banner',
      '-loglevel', 'error'
    ];

    if (isHttpUrl) {
      args.push(
        '-extension_picky', '0',
        '-allowed_segment_extensions', 'ALL',
        '-headers', this._buildNetworkHeaders(filePath)
      );
    }

    args.push(
      '-ss', timeSec.toFixed(2),
      '-i', filePath,
      '-an',
      '-sn',
      '-dn',
      '-frames:v', '1',
      '-vf', 'scale=320:-2',
      '-q:v', '5',
      '-threads', '2',
      '-f', 'image2pipe',
      '-vcodec', 'mjpeg',
      'pipe:1'
    );

    res.writeHead(200, {
      'Content-Type': 'image/jpeg',
      'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
      'Cache-Control': 'max-age=180'
    });

    const proc = spawn(ffmpegPath, args, { windowsHide: true });
    this.activeThumbProc = proc;

    const chunks = [];
    proc.stdout.on('data', (c) => chunks.push(c));
    proc.stdout.pipe(res);

    const cleanup = () => {
      if (this.activeThumbProc === proc) {
        this.activeThumbProc = null;
      }
      try {
        proc.kill('SIGKILL');
      } catch {}
    };

    req.on('close', cleanup);
    proc.on('close', () => {
      if (this.activeThumbProc === proc) {
        this.activeThumbProc = null;
      }
      if (chunks.length > 0) {
        const fullBuf = Buffer.concat(chunks);
        if (fullBuf.length > 80) {
          if (this.thumbCache.size >= MAX_THUMB_CACHE) {
            const oldestKey = this.thumbCache.keys().next().value;
            this.thumbCache.delete(oldestKey);
          }
          this.thumbCache.set(cacheKey, fullBuf);
        }
      }
    });
    proc.on('error', cleanup);
  }
}

function createStreamManager() {
  return new StreamProcessManager();
}

module.exports = {
  StreamProcessManager,
  createStreamManager
};
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/stream-sniffer.js
// Modular Sniffer Router & Dispatcher for CopyCat Media Player
// Routes stream sniffing tasks to specialized strategies (Miruro, Videa, Generic HTML5)

const {
  isStrmCdnUrl,
  registerStreamHostHeaders,
  getRequiredHeadersForUrl,
  sniffCache,
  SNIFF_CACHE_TTL_MS,
  runSnifferSession,
  logger
} = require('./sniffers/base-sniffer');

const miruroSniffer = require('./sniffers/miruro-sniffer');
const videaSniffer = require('./sniffers/videa-sniffer');
const genericSniffer = require('./sniffers/generic-sniffer');

// Ordered list of specialized sniffer strategies (highest specificity to fallback)
const SNIFFER_STRATEGIES = [
  miruroSniffer,
  videaSniffer,
  genericSniffer
];

/**
 * Resolve the most appropriate sniffer strategy for a given URL
 * @param {string} url
 * @returns {object} The matching sniffer strategy
 */
function getSnifferStrategy(url) {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return genericSniffer;
  const trimmed = url.trim();
  const matched = SNIFFER_STRATEGIES.find((strategy) => {
    try {
      return typeof strategy.matches === 'function' && strategy.matches(trimmed);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  });
  return matched || genericSniffer;
}

/**
 * Sniff and extract the primary media stream and companion subtitles from any webpage URL
 * Automatically routes to the optimal sniffer strategy based on URL pattern and host.
 *
 * @param {string} webpageUrl
 * @param {number} timeoutMs
 * @returns {Promise<{ streamUrl: string, title: string, isHls: boolean, isDash: boolean, subtitles: Array<{url: string, label: string}>, pageUrl: string } | null>}
 */
function extractStreamFromWebpage(webpageUrl, timeoutMs = 9500) {
  const normalizedUrl = (webpageUrl || '').trim();

  // 1. Check in-memory 5-minute sniff cache for instant re-probes
  const cached = sniffCache.get(normalizedUrl);
  if (cached && (Date.now() - cached.timestamp < SNIFF_CACHE_TTL_MS)) {
    if (cached.result && Array.isArray(cached.result.subtitles) && cached.result.subtitles.length > 0) {
      logger.info('Returning cached stream sniff result with subtitles for:', normalizedUrl);
      return Promise.resolve(cached.result);
    }
  }

  // 2. Select specialized sniffer strategy based on URL
  const strategy = getSnifferStrategy(normalizedUrl);
  logger.info(`[Router] Selected sniffer strategy "${strategy.name}" for target: ${normalizedUrl}`);

  // 3. Delegate to core runner with selected strategy
  return runSnifferSession(normalizedUrl, timeoutMs, strategy);
}

module.exports = {
  extractStreamFromWebpage,
  registerStreamHostHeaders,
  getRequiredHeadersForUrl,
  isStrmCdnUrl,
  getSnifferStrategy,
  SNIFFER_STRATEGIES
};
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/updater.js
// GitHub Releases Auto-Updater Engine for VLCopyCat

const https = require('https');
const crypto = require('crypto');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const os = require('os');
const { URL } = require('url');
const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const { shell, app } = require('electron');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');

const logger = createLogger('Updater');
const GITHUB_REPO = 'georgschneider96-collab/CopyCat-media-player';

/**
 * Compare semantic versions (vX.Y.Z) with proper pre-release tag handling
 * @param {string} currentVer
 * @param {string} latestTag
 * @returns {boolean} True if latestTag is newer than currentVer
 */
function isNewerVersion(currentVer, latestTag) {
  if (!latestTag || !currentVer) return false;
  const cleanCurr = (currentVer || '').replace(/^v/i, '').trim();
  const cleanLate = (latestTag || '').replace(/^v/i, '').trim();

  const [coreCurr, preCurr] = cleanCurr.split('-', 2);
  const [coreLate, preLate] = cleanLate.split('-', 2);

  const pCurr = (coreCurr || '').split('.').map((n) => parseInt(n, 10) || 0);
  const pLate = (coreLate || '').split('.').map((n) => parseInt(n, 10) || 0);

  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(pCurr.length, pLate.length); i++) {
    const c = pCurr[i] || 0;
    const l = pLate[i] || 0;
    if (l > c) return true;
    if (l < c) return false;
  }

  // Core versions are identical (e.g. 1.3.0 vs 1.3.0-beta.1):
  // A release without pre-release suffix is newer than one with pre-release.
  if (!preCurr && preLate) return false; // 1.3.0 stable is newer than 1.3.0-beta.1
  if (preCurr && !preLate) return true;  // 1.3.0-beta.1 -> 1.3.0 stable is an upgrade
  return false;
}

/**
 * Extract trusted SHA-256 checksum for release asset from release body or companion metadata
 * @param {Object} release
 * @param {Object} asset
 * @returns {string|null}
 */
function extractExpectedSha256(release, asset) {
  if (!release || !asset) return null;
  const body = release.body || '';
  const assetName = asset.name || '';

  // 1. Filename-bound hash: `<hash> *CopyCat-Setup.exe` or `CopyCat-Setup.exe: <hash>`
  if (assetName) {
    const escapedName = assetName.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
    const nameBoundPattern = new RegExp(`([a-fA-F0-9]{64})\\s+[*]?${escapedName}|${escapedName}[:\\s]+([a-fA-F0-9]{64})`, 'i');
    const match = body.match(nameBoundPattern);
    if (match) return (match[1] || match[2]).toLowerCase();
  }

  // 2. Explicit SHA-256 tag in release notes: `SHA-256: <hash>` or `Checksum: <hash>`
  const explicitMatch = body.match(/(?:sha[-_]?256|checksum)[\s:=]+([a-fA-F0-9]{64})/i);
  if (explicitMatch) return explicitMatch[1].toLowerCase();

  return null;
}

/**
 * Fetch remote companion .sha256 checksum file
 * @param {string} url
 * @param {number} maxRedirects
 * @returns {Promise<string|null>}
 */
function fetchCompanionSha256(url, maxRedirects = 4) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (!url || maxRedirects <= 0) return resolve(null);
    https.get(url, { headers: { 'User-Agent': 'CopyCat-Media-Player' }, timeout: 6000 }, (res) => {
      if (res.statusCode >= 300 && res.statusCode < 400 && res.headers.location) {
        return fetchCompanionSha256(res.headers.location, maxRedirects - 1).then(resolve);
      }
      if (res.statusCode !== 200) return resolve(null);
      let content = '';
      res.on('data', chunk => content += chunk);
      res.on('end', () => {
        const hashMatch = content.match(/([a-fA-F0-9]{64})/);
        resolve(hashMatch ? hashMatch[1].toLowerCase() : null);
      });
    }).on('error', () => resolve(null));
  });
}

/**
 * Query GitHub Releases API for latest release info
 * @returns {Promise<Object>}
 */
function checkForUpdates() {
  const packageJsonPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'package.json');
  let currentVersion = '1.0.0';
  try {
    const pkg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(packageJsonPath, 'utf8'));
    currentVersion = pkg.version || '1.0.0';
  } catch {}

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const reqOptions = {
      hostname: 'api.github.com',
      path: `/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest`,
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'CopyCat-Media-Player',
        'Accept': 'application/vnd.github.v3+json'
      }
    };

    const req = https.get(reqOptions, (res) => {
      let data = '';
      res.on('data', (chunk) => {
        data += chunk;
      });

      res.on('end', () => {
        try {
          if (res.statusCode !== 200) {
            logger.warn(`GitHub API returned status ${res.statusCode}`);
            return resolve({ ok: false, error: `GitHub API status ${res.statusCode}` });
          }

          const release = JSON.parse(data);
          const latestTag = release.tag_name || '';
          const isNewer = isNewerVersion(currentVersion, latestTag);

          let asset = null;
          let checksumAsset = null;
          if (Array.isArray(release.assets) && release.assets.length > 0) {
            asset = release.assets.find((a) => a.name.toLowerCase().includes('setup') && a.name.endsWith('.exe'))
              || release.assets.find((a) => a.name.endsWith('.exe'))
              || release.assets[0];

            checksumAsset = release.assets.find((a) =>
              a.name.endsWith('.sha256') ||
              a.name.toLowerCase().includes('checksums') ||
              a.name.toLowerCase().includes('sha256sums') ||
              a.name === 'latest.yml'
            );
          }

          const expectedSha256 = extractExpectedSha256(release, asset);

          logger.info(`Update check: current=${currentVersion}, latest=${latestTag}, available=${isNewer}`);
          resolve({
            ok: true,
            updateAvailable: isNewer,
            currentVersion: `v${currentVersion.replace(/^v/i, '')}`,
            latestVersion: latestTag,
            releaseName: release.name || latestTag,
            releaseNotes: release.body || '',
            releaseUrl: release.html_url || '',
            asset: asset ? {
              name: asset.name,
              size: asset.size,
              downloadUrl: asset.browser_download_url,
              sha256: expectedSha256,
              checksumUrl: checksumAsset ? checksumAsset.browser_download_url : null
            } : null
          });
        } catch (err) {
          logger.error('Failed to parse update release response:', err.message);
          resolve({ ok: false, error: err.message });
        }
      });
    });

    req.on('error', (err) => {
      logger.warn('Update request network error:', err.message);
      resolve({ ok: false, error: err.message });
    });

    req.setTimeout(8000, () => {
      req.destroy();
      logger.warn('Update check timed out after 8s');
      resolve({ ok: false, error: 'Request timed out' });
    });
  });
}

/**
 * Download update setup asset with strict HTTPS, atomic staging, SHA-256 verification, and PE binary checks
 * @param {string} downloadUrl
 * @param {string} assetName
 * @param {Function} onProgress
 * @param {string|null} expectedSha256 Optional SHA-256 checksum to verify
 * @param {number} maxRedirects
 * @returns {Promise<string>} Downloaded verified file path
 */
function downloadUpdateAsset(downloadUrl, assetName, onProgress, expectedSha256 = null, maxRedirects = 6) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const finalName = assetName || 'CopyCat-Update-Setup.exe';
    const destPath = path.join(os.tmpdir(), finalName);
    const tempPartPath = path.join(os.tmpdir(), `${finalName}.${Date.now()}.part`);

    function cleanup() {
      try { if (fs.existsSync(tempPartPath)) fs.unlinkSync(tempPartPath); } catch {}
    }

    function get(currentUrl, redirectsLeft) {
      if (redirectsLeft <= 0) {
        cleanup();
        return reject(new Error('Too many redirects while downloading update'));
      }

      const parsed = new URL(currentUrl);
      if (parsed.protocol !== 'https:') {
        cleanup();
        return reject(new Error(`Insecure download protocol rejected: ${parsed.protocol}. Only HTTPS allowed.`));
      }

      https.get({
        protocol: parsed.protocol,
        hostname: parsed.hostname,
        port: parsed.port || 443,
        path: parsed.pathname + parsed.search,
        headers: {
          'User-Agent': 'CopyCat-Media-Player',
          'Accept': 'application/octet-stream'
        }
      }, (res) => {
        if (res.statusCode >= 300 && res.statusCode < 400 && res.headers.location) {
          const nextUrl = new URL(res.headers.location, currentUrl).href;
          return get(nextUrl, redirectsLeft - 1);
        }

        if (res.statusCode !== 200) {
          cleanup();
          return reject(new Error(`Download failed with HTTP ${res.statusCode}`));
        }

        const total = parseInt(res.headers['content-length'] || '0', 10);
        let transferred = 0;
        let lastReport = 0;
        const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256');
        const fileStream = fs.createWriteStream(tempPartPath);

        res.on('data', (chunk) => {
          transferred += chunk.length;
          hash.update(chunk);
          const now = Date.now();
          if (now - lastReport > 80 || transferred === total) {
            lastReport = now;
            const percent = total > 0 ? Math.round((transferred / total) * 100) : 0;
            if (onProgress) onProgress({ percent, transferred, total });
          }
        });

        res.pipe(fileStream);

        fileStream.on('finish', () => {
          fileStream.close(() => {
            try {
              // 1. Content size validation
              if (total > 0 && transferred !== total) {
                cleanup();
                return reject(new Error(`Incomplete download: transferred ${transferred}/${total} bytes`));
              }

              // 2. Computed SHA-256 hash & verification
              const computedSha256 = hash.digest('hex');
              logger.info(`Downloaded asset SHA-256: ${computedSha256}`);

              if (expectedSha256) {
                if (computedSha256.toLowerCase() !== expectedSha256.toLowerCase()) {
                  cleanup();
                  logger.error(`Cryptographic verification failure! Expected SHA-256: ${expectedSha256}, Got: ${computedSha256}`);
                  return reject(new Error(`Checksum mismatch! Expected: ${expectedSha256}, Got: ${computedSha256}`));
                }
                logger.info(`Cryptographic SHA-256 verification successful: ${computedSha256}`);
              } else {
                logger.warn('No published expected SHA-256 checksum found for this release; proceeding with Windows PE binary header verification.');
              }

              // 3. Binary header validation (Windows PE MZ signature: 0x4D, 0x5A)
              const fd = fs.openSync(tempPartPath, 'r');
              const headerBuf = Buffer.alloc(2);
              fs.readSync(fd, headerBuf, 0, 2, 0);
              fs.closeSync(fd);

              if (headerBuf[0] !== 0x4D || headerBuf[1] !== 0x5A) {
                cleanup();
                return reject(new Error('Downloaded asset is not a valid Windows executable (missing MZ signature)'));
              }

              // 4. Atomic move to final destination
              if (fs.existsSync(destPath)) {
                try { fs.unlinkSync(destPath); } catch {}
              }
              fs.renameSync(tempPartPath, destPath);
              logger.info(`Asset verified and atomically placed: ${destPath}`);
              resolve(destPath);
            } catch (postErr) {
              cleanup();
              reject(postErr);
            }
          });
        });

        fileStream.on('error', (err) => {
          cleanup();
          reject(err);
        });
      }).on('error', (err) => {
        cleanup();
        reject(err);
      });
    }

    get(downloadUrl, maxRedirects);
  });
}

/**
 * Download update and launch the Windows installer
 * @param {Object} asset
 * @param {BrowserWindow} win
 * @returns {Promise<Object>}
 */
async function downloadAndInstallUpdate(asset, win) {
  if (!asset || !asset.downloadUrl) {
    if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
      win.webContents.send('updater:error', { error: 'No download asset available' });
    }
    return { ok: false, error: 'No download asset' };
  }

  try {
    logger.info(`Starting download of update asset: ${asset.name}`);
    let expectedSha256 = asset.sha256 || null;
    if (!expectedSha256 && asset.checksumUrl) {
      try {
        expectedSha256 = await fetchCompanionSha256(asset.checksumUrl);
        if (expectedSha256) {
          logger.info(`Retrieved companion SHA-256 checksum: ${expectedSha256}`);
        }
      } catch (chkErr) {
        logger.warn('Failed to fetch companion checksum:', chkErr.message);
      }
    }

    const downloadedPath = await downloadUpdateAsset(
      asset.downloadUrl,
      asset.name,
      (progress) => {
        if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
          win.webContents.send('updater:downloadProgress', progress);
        }
      },
      expectedSha256
    );

    logger.info(`Download completed successfully: ${downloadedPath}`);
    if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
      win.webContents.send('updater:downloadComplete', { path: downloadedPath });
    }

    // Launch downloaded installer and quit current app instance
    setTimeout(() => {
      try {
        const child = spawn(downloadedPath, [], {
          detached: true,
          stdio: 'ignore'
        });
        child.unref();
      } catch (launchErr) {
        logger.warn('Direct launch failed, attempting shell.openPath:', launchErr.message);
        shell.openPath(downloadedPath);
      }
      app.quit();
    }, 1000);

    return { ok: true, path: downloadedPath };
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error('Update download or installation error:', err.message);
    if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
      win.webContents.send('updater:error', { error: err.message });
    }
    return { ok: false, error: err.message };
  }
}

module.exports = {
  GITHUB_REPO,
  isNewerVersion,
  checkForUpdates,
  downloadUpdateAsset,
  downloadAndInstallUpdate,
  extractExpectedSha256
};
``````

``````css
  box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(var(--accent-rgb), 0.35);
}

.sub-result-row {

}
``````
